      rec.evidenceHashes.push(h);
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidenceHashes, 24);
      var compact = /\b(?:again|same|practiced|repeated|once more|another)\b/i.test(clean) || (rec.evidence && rec.evidence.length >= 8);
      if (compact) {
        rec.compactUseCount = (rec.compactUseCount || 0) + 1;
        rec.lastCompactEvidence = { turn: turn, phrase: clean, source: source || 'observed' };
        if (!rec.evidenceSummary) rec.evidenceSummary = [];
        rec.evidenceSummary.push({ turn: turn, source: source || 'observed', note: WL.U.title(rec.name || 'Ability') + ' repeated/practiced evidence', phraseHash: h });
        WL.U.capArray(rec.evidenceSummary, 8);
      } else {
        rec.evidence.push({ turn: turn, phrase: clean, source: source || 'observed' });
        WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 8);
      }
      return rec;
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'ability_evidence_candidate' || p.type === 'failed_attempt_candidate' || p.type === 'training_evidence_candidate') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.recordEvidence(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'category_transfer_request') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.transferCategory(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'keys_update_request') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.setPlayerKeys(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'cost_pressure_candidate') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.recordCostPressure(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'cost_observation_candidate') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.recordCostObservation(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'skill_gain_candidate') return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.recordSkillGain(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: validation.family, type: validation.type, reason: 'unsupported_ability_event' };
    },
    recordEvidence: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || proposal.targetName || 'Unknown Ability';
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, proposal.type, proposal.textPreview);
      if (proposal.type === 'failed_attempt_candidate') {
        rec.failureCount += 1; rec.status = 'failed_candidate'; root.abilities.failedById[rec.id] = rec; if (root.abilities.failedIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.abilities.failedIds.push(rec.id);
      } else if (proposal.type === 'training_evidence_candidate') {
        rec.trainingCount += 1; rec.compactUseCount = (rec.compactUseCount || 0) + 1; rec.status = 'candidate'; root.abilities.candidatesById[rec.id] = rec; if (root.abilities.candidateIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.abilities.candidateIds.push(rec.id);
      } else {
        rec.successCount += 1; rec.compactUseCount = (rec.compactUseCount || 0) + 1; rec.status = 'candidate'; root.abilities.candidatesById[rec.id] = rec; if (root.abilities.candidateIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.abilities.candidateIds.push(rec.id);
      }
      var costRec = WL.AbilityCostSystem.observeUse(root, rec.name, { kind: proposal.type, textPreview: proposal.textPreview, key: ledger.key });
      rec.costId = costRec.id;
      rec.costConfidence = costRec.confidence;
      rec.developmentStage = costRec.developmentStage;
      WL.AbilityDevelopmentMatrix.touchRecord(root, rec.name, costRec.developmentStage, costRec.id);
      root.abilities.recent.push({ action: 'evidence', abilityId: rec.id, type: proposal.type, stage: rec.developmentStage, costId: rec.costId, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.recent, 50);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'evidence', abilityId: rec.id, stage: rec.developmentStage, costId: rec.costId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordSkillGain: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Skill');
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, 'explicit_skill_gain', proposal.textPreview);
      rec.successCount += 1;
      rec.status = 'candidate';
      root.abilities.candidatesById[rec.id] = rec;
      if (root.abilities.candidateIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.abilities.candidateIds.push(rec.id);
      WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.installSkill(root, rec, { status: 'stable', reason: 'explicit_story_skill_gain' });
      var skillCost = WL.AbilityCostSystem.observeUse(root, name, { kind: 'skill_gain_candidate', textPreview: proposal.textPreview, key: ledger.key });
      WL.AbilityDevelopmentMatrix.touchRecord(root, name, skillCost.developmentStage, skillCost.id);
      root.abilities.recent.push({ action: 'skill_gain', abilityId: rec.id, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.recent, 50);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'skill_gain', abilityId: rec.id, skillId: 'skill_' + WL.U.slug(name), key: ledger.key };
    },
    transferCategory: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Ability');
      var category = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.category || 'ability');
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, 'category_transfer_request', proposal.textPreview);
      var verdict = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.validateTransfer(root, rec, category);
      var log = { turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, abilityId: rec.id, name: name, category: category, status: verdict.status, reason: verdict.reason, key: ledger.key };
      root.abilities.transferLog.push(log); WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.transferLog, 60);
      if (verdict.status === 'denied') { root.abilities.denied.push(log); WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.denied, 40); return { status: 'DENIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'category_transfer', abilityId: rec.id, category: category, reason: verdict.reason, key: ledger.key }; }
      if (category === 'skill') WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.installSkill(root, rec, verdict);
      else if (category === 'talent') WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.installTalent(root, rec, verdict);
      else if (category === 'passive') WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.installPassive(root, rec, verdict);
      else WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.installAbility(root, rec, verdict);
      var transferCost = WL.AbilityCostSystem.estimate(root, name, category, verdict.reason);
      WL.AbilityDevelopmentMatrix.touchRecord(root, name, transferCost.developmentStage, transferCost.id);
      return { status: verdict.status === 'unstable' ? 'APPLIED_UNSTABLE' : 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'category_transfer', abilityId: rec.id, category: category, costId: transferCost.id, stage: transferCost.developmentStage, key: ledger.key };
    },
    validateTransfer: function (root, rec, category) {
      var name = WL.U.lower(rec.name);
      if (category === 'passive') {
        if (/fireball|meteor|lightning bolt|explosion|beam|blast/.test(name)) return { status: 'denied', reason: 'active_attack_spell_is_not_a_normal_passive' };
        if (/regen|regeneration|healing factor/.test(name)) return { status: 'weak', reason: 'passive_allowed_as_weak_adaptation_until_evidence_grows' };
        if (/boxing|swordsmanship|martial|stance|discipline/.test(name)) return { status: 'stable', reason: 'discipline_style_passive_is_plausible' };
        return { status: 'weak', reason: 'passive_candidate_needs_exposure_or_conditioning' };
      }
      if (category === 'skill') {
        if (rec.successCount <= 0 && rec.trainingCount <= 0) return { status: 'unstable', reason: 'skill_created_unstable_until_success_or_training_evidence' };
        return { status: 'stable', reason: 'skill_supported_by_observed_use_or_training' };
      }
      if (category === 'talent') {
        if (/sword|box|martial|stance|style|form|duel/.test(name) || rec.trainingCount > 0) return { status: 'stable', reason: 'talent_supported_by_style_or_training' };
        return { status: 'unstable', reason: 'talent_needs_style_evidence' };
      }
      return { status: 'stable', reason: 'general_ability_recorded' };
    },
    installSkill: function (root, rec, verdict) {
      var id = 'skill_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name);
      var target = verdict.status === 'unstable' ? root.skills.unstableById : root.skills.skillsById;
      var ids = verdict.status === 'unstable' ? root.skills.unstableIds : root.skills.skillIds;
      target[id] = { id: id, name: rec.name, level: 1, xp: 0, status: verdict.status, originAbilityId: rec.id, activationName: rec.name, costId: 'cost_skill_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name), notes: [verdict.reason] };
      if (ids.indexOf(id) < 0) ids.push(id);
    },
    installTalent: function (root, rec, verdict) {
      var id = 'talent_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name);
      root.talents.talentsById[id] = { id: id, name: rec.name, level: 1, xp: 0, status: verdict.status, originAbilityId: rec.id, keys: [rec.name], notes: [verdict.reason] };
      if (root.talents.talentIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.talents.talentIds.push(id);
      root.talents.keysByTalentId[id] = [rec.name];
    },
    installPassive: function (root, rec, verdict) {
      var id = 'passive_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name);
      var obj = { id: id, name: rec.name, level: 1, xp: 0, status: verdict.status, originAbilityId: rec.id, activationCost: 'none', keys: [rec.name], notes: [verdict.reason] };
      if (verdict.status === 'weak') { root.passives.weakCandidateById[id] = obj; if (root.passives.weakCandidateIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.passives.weakCandidateIds.push(id); }
      else { root.passives.passivesById[id] = obj; if (root.passives.passiveIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.passives.passiveIds.push(id); }
      root.passives.keysByPassiveId[id] = [rec.name];
    },
    installAbility: function (root, rec, verdict) {
      root.abilities.abilitiesById[rec.id] = { id: rec.id, name: rec.name, level: 1, xp: 0, status: verdict.status, stable: verdict.status === 'stable', notes: [verdict.reason], keys: root.abilities.keysByAbilityId[rec.id] || [] };
      if (root.abilities.abilityIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.abilities.abilityIds.push(rec.id);
    },
    categoryForName: function (root, name) {
      WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.ensure(root);
      var slug = WL.U.slug(name || '');
      if (root.skills.skillsById['skill_' + slug] || root.skills.unstableById['skill_' + slug]) return 'skill';
      if (root.talents.talentsById['talent_' + slug]) return 'talent';
      if (root.passives.passivesById['passive_' + slug] || root.passives.weakCandidateById['passive_' + slug]) return 'passive';
      if (root.abilities.abilitiesById['ability_' + slug]) return 'ability';
      return 'ability';
    },
    cleanKeys: function (keys) {
      var raw = Array.isArray(keys) ? keys : String(keys || '').split(/[,;|]/);
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      for (var i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
        var k = WL.U.norm(raw[i]).replace(/^(?:keys?|triggers?)\s*[:=]\s*/i, '').replace(/^and\s+/i, '').trim();
        if (!k || k.length > 48) continue;
        if (/^(?:story card trigger|trigger|key)$/i.test(k)) continue;
        var id = WL.U.slug(k);
        if (seen[id]) continue;
        seen[id] = true;
        out.push(k);
      }
      return out.slice(0, 12);
    },
    setPlayerKeys: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Ability');
      var keys = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.cleanKeys(proposal.data.keys || []);
      if (!keys.length) return { status: 'NO_KEYS', applied: false, family: 'ability', type: 'keys_update', abilityId: '', key: ledger.key };
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, 'keys_update_request', proposal.textPreview);
      rec.keys = keys.slice();
      root.abilities.keysByAbilityId[rec.id] = keys.slice();
      if (root.abilities.abilitiesById[rec.id]) root.abilities.abilitiesById[rec.id].keys = keys.slice();
      var slug = WL.U.slug(name);
      var skillId = 'skill_' + slug;
      if (root.skills.skillsById[skillId]) { root.skills.skillsById[skillId].keys = keys.slice(); root.skills.keysBySkillId[skillId] = keys.slice(); }
      if (root.skills.unstableById[skillId]) { root.skills.unstableById[skillId].keys = keys.slice(); root.skills.keysBySkillId[skillId] = keys.slice(); }
      var talentId = 'talent_' + slug;
      if (root.talents.talentsById[talentId]) { root.talents.talentsById[talentId].keys = keys.slice(); root.talents.keysByTalentId[talentId] = keys.slice(); }
      var passiveId = 'passive_' + slug;
      if (root.passives.passivesById[passiveId]) { root.passives.passivesById[passiveId].keys = keys.slice(); root.passives.keysByPassiveId[passiveId] = keys.slice(); }
      if (root.passives.weakCandidateById[passiveId]) { root.passives.weakCandidateById[passiveId].keys = keys.slice(); root.passives.keysByPassiveId[passiveId] = keys.slice(); }
      root.abilities.recent.push({ action: 'keys_update', abilityId: rec.id, keys: keys.slice(), key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.recent, 50);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'keys_update', abilityId: rec.id, keys: keys.slice(), key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordCostPressure: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Ability');
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, 'cost_pressure_candidate', proposal.textPreview);
      rec.evidence.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, phrase: proposal.textPreview, source: 'cost_pressure' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 12);
      var cost = WL.AbilityCostSystem.recordPressure(root, name, { words: proposal.data.riskWords || proposal.textPreview, textPreview: proposal.textPreview, hpRisk: proposal.data.hpRisk || 0, mpRisk: proposal.data.mpRisk || 0, epRisk: proposal.data.epRisk || 0, key: ledger.key });
      rec.costId = cost.id; rec.costConfidence = cost.confidence; rec.developmentStage = cost.developmentStage;
      WL.AbilityDevelopmentMatrix.touchRecord(root, name, cost.developmentStage, cost.id);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'cost_pressure', abilityId: rec.id, costId: cost.id, stage: cost.developmentStage, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordCostObservation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.abilityName || proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Ability');
      var rec = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.getOrCreateInbox(root, name, 'cost_observation_candidate', proposal.textPreview);
      var cost = WL.AbilityCostSystem.recordExplicitObservation(root, name, proposal.data.observedCost || proposal.data.cost || {}, { textPreview: proposal.textPreview, key: ledger.key });
      rec.costId = cost.id;
      rec.costConfidence = cost.confidence;
      rec.developmentStage = cost.developmentStage;
      root.abilities.recent.push({ action: 'cost_observation', abilityId: rec.id, costId: cost.id, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.abilities.recent, 50);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'ability', type: 'cost_observation', abilityId: rec.id, costId: cost.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.ensure(root);
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Inbox: ' + (root.abilities.inboxIds.map(function (id) { return root.abilities.inboxById[id].name + ' [' + root.abilities.inboxById[id].status + ']'; }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Skills: ' + (root.skills.skillIds.map(function (id) { return root.skills.skillsById[id].name; }).join(', ') || '(none)') + ' | Unstable: ' + (root.skills.unstableIds.map(function (id) { return root.skills.unstableById[id].name; }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Talents: ' + (root.talents.talentIds.map(function (id) { return root.talents.talentsById[id].name; }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Passives: ' + (root.passives.passiveIds.map(function (id) { return root.passives.passivesById[id].name; }).join(', ') || '(none)') + ' | Weak: ' + (root.passives.weakCandidateIds.map(function (id) { return root.passives.weakCandidateById[id].name; }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Denied transfers: ' + root.abilities.denied.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 07. EventScanner / ProposalBuilder / CanonValidator / ReducerRegistry
  // ============================================================
  WL.EventScanner = {
    makeEvent: function (family, type, clause, source, data) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      return {
        family: family,
        type: type,
        sourceHook: source.hook,
        sourceKind: source.kind,
        sourceHash: WL.U.hash(source.hook + '|' + c),
        localClauseHash: WL.U.hash(c),
        textPreview: c.slice(0, 220),
        data: data || {},
        confidence: data && data.confidence || 'candidate'
      };
    },
    scanInput: function (text, root, source) {
      var out = [];
      var clauses = WL.U.splitClauses(text);
      for (var i = 0; i < clauses.length; i++) {
        out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('input', 'intent_observed', clauses[i], source, { confidence: 'intent_only' }));
        var abilityEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectAbilityProgressionCandidates(clauses[i], 'input');
        for (var a = 0; a < abilityEvents.length; a++) out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent(abilityEvents[a].family, abilityEvents[a].type, clauses[i], source, abilityEvents[a]));
      }
      WL.EventScanner.record(root, out);
      return out;
    },
    scanOutput: function (text, root, source) {
      var out = [];
      var windowPayments = WL.EventScanner.detectCurrencyWindowPayments(text);
      for (var wp = 0; wp < windowPayments.length; wp++) {
        out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('inventory', 'payment_candidate', text, source, windowPayments[wp]));
        if (windowPayments[wp].direction !== 'spend' && windowPayments[wp].direction !== 'loss') out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('quest', 'reward_payment_candidate', text, source, windowPayments[wp]));
      }
      var clauses = WL.U.splitClauses(text);
      if (!clauses.length && WL.U.norm(text)) clauses = [WL.U.norm(text)];
      for (var i = 0; i < clauses.length; i++) {
        var clause = clauses[i];
        out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('system', 'output_observed', clause, source, { confidence: 'confirmed_output' }));

        var purchase = WL.EventScanner.detectPurchaseCandidate(clause);
        if (purchase) out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('inventory', 'purchase_candidate', clause, source, purchase));

        var payment = WL.EventScanner.detectCurrencyPayment(clause);
        if (payment) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('inventory', 'payment_candidate', clause, source, payment));
          if (payment.direction !== 'spend' && payment.direction !== 'loss') out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('quest', 'reward_payment_candidate', clause, source, payment));
        }

        var quest = WL.EventScanner.detectQuestCandidate(clause);
        if (quest) out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('quest', quest.type, clause, source, quest));

        var inventoryHits = WL.EventScanner.detectInventoryCandidates(clause);
        for (var h = 0; h < inventoryHits.length; h++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('inventory', inventoryHits[h].type, clause, source, inventoryHits[h]));
        }

        var formHits = WL.EventScanner.detectFormBodyCandidates(clause);
        for (var f = 0; f < formHits.length; f++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('form', formHits[f].type, clause, source, formHits[f]));
        }

        var abilityEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectAbilityProgressionCandidates(clause, 'output');
        for (var a = 0; a < abilityEvents.length; a++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent(abilityEvents[a].family, abilityEvents[a].type, clause, source, abilityEvents[a]));
        }

        var timeEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectTimeTravelCandidates(clause);
        for (var t = 0; t < timeEvents.length; t++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('time', timeEvents[t].type, clause, source, timeEvents[t]));
        }

        var placeEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectWorldPlaceCandidates(clause);
        for (var p = 0; p < placeEvents.length; p++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('place', placeEvents[p].type, clause, source, placeEvents[p]));
        }

        var actorEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectActorSocialCandidates(clause);
        for (var ac = 0; ac < actorEvents.length; ac++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('actor', actorEvents[ac].type, clause, source, actorEvents[ac]));
        }

        var socialEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectSocialConsequenceCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var sc = 0; sc < socialEvents.length; sc++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', socialEvents[sc].type, clause, source, socialEvents[sc]));
        }

        var witnessEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectWitnessCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var we = 0; we < witnessEvents.length; we++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', witnessEvents[we].type, clause, source, witnessEvents[we]));
        }

        var reputationEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectReputationCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var re = 0; re < reputationEvents.length; re++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', reputationEvents[re].type, clause, source, reputationEvents[re]));
        }

        var rumorEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectRumorCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var ru = 0; ru < rumorEvents.length; ru++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', rumorEvents[ru].type, clause, source, rumorEvents[ru]));
        }

        var crimeEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectCrimeEvidenceCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var cr = 0; cr < crimeEvents.length; cr++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', crimeEvents[cr].type, clause, source, crimeEvents[cr]));
        }

        var lawEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectFactionLawCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var lr = 0; lr < lawEvents.length; lr++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('social', lawEvents[lr].type, clause, source, lawEvents[lr]));
        }

        var contractLaborEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectContractLaborCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var cl = 0; cl < contractLaborEvents.length; cl++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent(contractLaborEvents[cl].family || 'contract', contractLaborEvents[cl].type, clause, source, contractLaborEvents[cl]));
        }

        var economyEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectEconomyTransactionCandidates(clause, root);
        for (var ec = 0; ec < economyEvents.length; ec++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('economy', economyEvents[ec].type, clause, source, economyEvents[ec]));
          if (economyEvents[ec].type === 'economy_wage_payment_candidate') {
            out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('inventory', 'payment_candidate', clause, source, { targetId: 'payment_' + WL.U.slug((economyEvents[ec].actorName || 'worker') + '_' + economyEvents[ec].amount + '_' + economyEvents[ec].currency + '_wage'), amount: economyEvents[ec].amount, currency: economyEvents[ec].currency, payer: 'player', payee: economyEvents[ec].actorName || 'worker', direction: 'spend', confidence: 'confirmed_wage_payment_spend' }));
          }
        }

        var combatEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectCombatThreatCandidates(clause);
        for (var ce = 0; ce < combatEvents.length; ce++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('combat', combatEvents[ce].type, clause, source, combatEvents[ce]));
        }

        var advancedEvents = WL.EventScanner.detectAdvancedBoundaryCandidates(clause);
        for (var be = 0; be < advancedEvents.length; be++) {
          out.push(WL.EventScanner.makeEvent('advanced', advancedEvents[be].type, clause, source, advancedEvents[be]));
        }
      }
      WL.EventScanner.record(root, out);
      return out;
    },
    hasNegativeAcquisition: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      if (WL.EventScanner.isInstructionalOrQuotedAcquisition(lower)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:asks?|asked|offers?|offered|hires?|hired|tasks?|charged|contracts?|contracted)\s+you\s+to\s+(?:recover|retrieve|find|fetch|bring|deliver|escort|repair|clear|investigate|protect)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:quest|task|mission|contract|objective)\b[\s\S]{0,90}\b(?:recover|retrieve|find|fetch|bring|deliver|escort|repair|clear|investigate|protect)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:rumor|rumour|dream|vision|joke|instruction|example sentence|grammar lesson|lesson|not an actual|nothing changes hands|hands are empty|empty-handed|only read|only written)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|take(?:s|n)?|grab(?:s|bed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?)\b/.test(lower)) return true;
      return /\b(?:do not|don't|does not|doesn't|did not|didn't|never|not)\s+(?:pick(?:s)? up|take(?:s)?|grab(?:s)?|snag(?:s)?|yoink(?:s)?|nab(?:s)?|receive(?:s)?|pocket(?:s)?|secure(?:s)?|retrieve(?:s)?|recover(?:s)?|loot(?:s)?|keep(?:s)?|claim(?:s)?|carry|carries)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(almost|try(?:ing)? to|tries to|attempts? to|fails? to|failed to|refuses? to|declines? to|cannot|can't|couldn't|doesn't|didn't|never)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(reaches? for|reached for|looks? at|sees?|saw|notices?|noticed|wants?|needs?|searches? for|promises?|objective|quest|task|mission|reward)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\btake(?:s)?\s+(?:a\s+|the\s+)?(?:long\s+)?look\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(?:pick(?:s)? up|grab(?:s)?|snag(?:s)?|yoink(?:s)?|nab(?:s)?|take(?:s)?|lift(?:s)?)\b[\s\S]{0,90}\band\s+(?:throw|throws|toss|tosses|hurl|hurls)\b/.test(lower) ||
        /\b(but|however)\b[\s\S]*\b(snatches?|takes?|pulls?|knocks?|steals?|catches?|away|out of reach|runs away with it|slips away|dissolves|illusion|nothing)\b/.test(lower);
    },
    isInstructionalOrQuotedAcquisition: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      var hasAcq = /\b(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|take(?:s|n)?|grab(?:s|bed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?)\b/.test(lower);
      if (!hasAcq) return false;
      if (/\b(?:sign|notice|poster|book|manual|scroll|title|example|lesson|grammar|says?|said|reads?|read|shouts?|claims?|joke|rumor|rumour)\b/.test(lower) && /"[^\"]*\b(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|take(?:s|n)?|grab(?:s|bed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?)\b[^\"]*"/.test(lower)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:sign|notice|poster)\s+reads?\s*:/.test(lower)) return true;
      return false;
    },
    positiveAcquisitionSegments: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var pieces = c.split(/\b(?:but|however)\b/i);
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 1; i < pieces.length; i++) {
        var p = WL.U.norm(pieces[i]);
        if (/\b(?:hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?|places?|sets?|slips?|pick(?:s)? up|take(?:s)?|grab(?:s)?|snag(?:s)?|yoink(?:s)?|nab(?:s)?|receive(?:s)?|pocket(?:s)?|retrieve(?:s)?|recover(?:s)?|secure(?:s)?|snatch(?:es)? up|scoop(?:s)? up|catch(?:es)?)\b/.test(WL.U.lower(p))) out.push(p);
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectCurrencyWindowPayments: function (text) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(text);
      var out = [];
      var num = '(\\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)';
      var re = new RegExp("\\b(?:[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}|he|she|they|the [a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\\s+counts?\\s+out\\s+" + num + "\\s+(?:dull\\s+|bright\\s+|old\\s+|small\\s+|worn\\s+){0,4}(copper|silver|gold)(?:\\s+coins?)?\\b[\\s\\S]{0,180}\\b(?:small\\s+|worn\\s+|leather\\s+)?pouch\\b[\\s\\S]{0,120}\\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\\s*(?:to\\s+you|you)\\b", 'i');
      var m = c.match(re);
      if (m) {
        var amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        if (amount && currency) out.push({ payer: 'unknown payer', amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: 'gain', container: 'pouch', confidence: 'confirmed_multiclause_currency_handoff' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectPurchaseCandidate: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:buy|bought|purchase|purchased)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)\s+for\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (!m) return null;
      var itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
      var amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(m[3] || 'gold');
      if (!itemName || !amount) return null;
      return { itemName: itemName, itemId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(itemName), amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: 'spend', confidence: 'confirmed_purchase' };
    },
    detectCurrencyPayment: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var m, payer = '', amount = 0, currency = '', direction = 'gain';
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50})\s+(?:pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?)\s+you\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
      if (m) { payer = WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:are\s+)?(?:paid|given|handed)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b(?:\s+by\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}))?/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = WL.U.title(m[3] || 'unknown payer'); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:take|pocket|receive|secure)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = 'self/scene'; }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}?)\b[\s\S]{0,90}\bcounts?\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(?:dull\s+|bright\s+|old\s+)?(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\s*(?:to\s+you|you)\b/i);
        if (m) { payer = WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:he|she|they|the\s+[a-z][a-z -]{1,30})\s+counts?\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(?:dull\s+|bright\s+|old\s+)?(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:hands?|gives?|passes?|offers?)\s+(?:it|them|the pouch|the coins)?\s*(?:to\s+you|you)\b/i);
        if (m) { payer = 'unknown payer'; amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); }
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:count|counts|counted)\s+out\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety|a|an)\s+(copper|silver|gold)(?:\s+coins?)?\b/i);
        if (m) { amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); payer = 'player'; direction = 'spend'; }
      }
      if (!amount || amount < 1 || !currency) return null;
      return { payer: payer, amount: amount, currency: currency, direction: direction, confidence: direction === 'spend' ? 'confirmed_currency_spend' : 'confirmed_payment_phrase' };
    },
    detectInventoryCandidate: function (clause) {
      var list = WL.EventScanner.detectInventoryCandidates(clause);
      return list.length ? list[0] : null;
    },
    detectInventoryCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      if (/\b(?:your\s+)?pockets?\s+(?:are|is|were|was|feel|feels|seem|seems|remain|remains)\s+empty\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (/\bno\s+(?:armor|weapon|coin|coins|items?|equipment)\b/i.test(c) && !/\b(?:pick|take|grab|receive|given|hands? you)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      var m;
      // Item loss / custody loss.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:drop|dropped|discard|discarded|throw|throws|threw|toss|tossed|hurl|hurled)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)(?:\s+(?:into|in|onto|on|at|toward|down|away|from)\b|[.!?;,]|$)/i);
      if (m) {
        var lost = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
        if (lost) out.push({ type: 'loss_candidate', targetName: lost, targetId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(lost), reason: 'dropped_or_thrown', confidence: 'confirmed_item_loss' });
        return out;
      }
      // Item damage / condition.
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?\b(?:sword|dagger|blade|key|shield|armor|lantern|ring|staff|bow|axe|spear))\s+(?:snaps?|breaks?|shatters?|cracks?|bends?|rusts?|burns?)\b(?:\s+(in half|apart|badly|completely))?/i);
      if (m) {
        var damaged = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(m[1]);
        if (damaged) out.push({ type: 'condition_candidate', targetName: damaged, targetId: 'item_' + WL.U.slug(damaged), condition: /snap|break|shatter/i.test(c) ? 'broken' : 'damaged', confidence: 'confirmed_item_condition' });
        return out;
      }
      if (WL.EventScanner.detectCurrencyPayment(c) || WL.EventScanner.detectPurchaseCandidate(c)) return out;
      var segments = [c];
      if (WL.EventScanner.hasNegativeAcquisition(c)) {
        segments = WL.EventScanner.positiveAcquisitionSegments(c);
        if (!segments.length) return out;
      }
      var seen = {};
      for (var s = 0; s < segments.length; s++) {
        var seg = WL.U.norm(segments[s]);
        if (!seg || WL.EventScanner.hasNegativeAcquisition(seg)) continue;
        var m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:pick(?:s)? up|grab(?:s)?|snag(?:s)?|yoink(?:s)?|nab(?:s)?|take(?:s)? hold of|take(?:s)?|loot(?:s)?|receive(?:s)?|pocket(?:s)?|snatch(?:es)? up|scoop(?:s)? up|secure(?:s)?|retrieve(?:s)?|recover(?:s)?|lift(?:s)?|catch(?:es)?)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+|your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:hands?|gives?|gave|tosses?)\s+you\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\b(?:places?|sets?|slips?)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100}?)\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b/i);
        if (!m2) m2 = seg.match(/\byou\s+(?:are\s+)?given\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
        if (!m2) continue;
        var parts = WL.EventScanner.splitCandidateTargets(m2[1]);
        for (var i = 0; i < parts.length; i++) {
          var target = WL.EventScanner.cleanCandidateTarget(parts[i]);
          if (!target) continue;
          var id = 'item_' + WL.U.slug(target);
          if (seen[id]) continue;
          seen[id] = true;
          out.push({ type: 'acquire_candidate', targetName: target, targetId: id, confidence: 'confirmed_acquisition_phrase' });
        }
      }
      return out;
    },
    splitCandidateTargets: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(target);
      if (!t) return [];
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:by|from)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}\s*$/i, '');
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:before|while|as|when|but|and then|from|off|out of|into|onto)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
      t = t.replace(/\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
      t = t.replace(/\s+and\s+(?:strap|straps|stow|stows|put|puts|place|places|slip|slips|tuck|tucks|hide|hides|wear|wears|equip|equips|sheath|sheathes|secure|secures)\s+(?:it|them|the\s+item|the\s+object|that|those)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
      var raw = t.split(/\s*,\s*|\s+and\s+(?=(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?[a-z][a-z' -]{1,60}$)/i);
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < raw.length; i++) {
        var p = WL.U.norm(raw[i]).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '');
        if (p) out.push(p);
      }
      return out.length ? out : [t];
    },
    isAbstractInventoryTarget: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.lower(target);
      if (!t) return true;
      return /^(?:are empty|empty|pockets? are empty|no armor|no weapon|no coin|no items?|idea|thought|concept|plan|rumor|rumour|promise|quest|task|mission|objective|reward|payment|thanks|gratitude|advice|information|info|news|look|glance|stare|view|breath|moment|time|chance|opportunity|effort|pain|fear|hope|memory|dream|vision|attention|hold|bearings|nap|sleep|rest|energy|stamina|breath|floor|ground|face|body|muscles?|lungs?|hands?|wrists?|fingers?)$/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:idea|thought|concept|rumor|rumour|dream|vision|memory|notion)\s+of\b/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:long\s+)?look\s+(?:at|over|around)\b/i.test(t) ||
        /^(?:your\s+)?(?:bearings|stamina|energy|body|muscles?|lungs?|hands?|wrists?|fingers?|quick\s+nap|nap|sleep|rest)\b/i.test(t) ||
        /\b(?:nap|sleep|rest)\s+(?:on|in|at)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:floor|ground|bed)\b/i.test(t);
    },
    cleanCandidateTarget: function (target) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(target)
        .replace(/\s+(?:by|from)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\b(before|while|as|when|but|and then|from|off|out of)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/^hold\s+of\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^up\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your|another|some|several)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (!t || t.length < 2) return '';
      if (/^(?:strap|straps|stow|stows|put|puts|place|places|slip|slips|tuck|tucks|hide|hides|wear|wears|equip|equips|sheath|sheathes|secure|secures)\s+(?:it|them|that|those|the\s+item|the\s+object)\b/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (/^(?:it|them|that|those)\b/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (WL.EventScanner.isAbstractInventoryTarget(t)) return '';
      if (/\b(quest|task|mission|objective|reward|promise)\b/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (/^(?:\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+(?:gold|silver|copper)(?:\s+coins?)?$/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (WL.ItemIdentitySystem && typeof WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName === 'function') t = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName(t);
      return t ? WL.U.title(t) : '';
    },
    numberWord: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value);
      var map = { a: 1, an: 1, one: 1, two: 2, three: 3, four: 4, five: 5, six: 6, seven: 7, eight: 8, nine: 9, ten: 10, eleven: 11, twelve: 12, thirteen: 13, fourteen: 14, fifteen: 15, sixteen: 16, seventeen: 17, eighteen: 18, nineteen: 19, twenty: 20, thirty: 30, forty: 40, fifty: 50, sixty: 60, seventy: 70, eighty: 80, ninety: 90 };
      if (/^\d+$/.test(v)) return Number(v);
      return map[v] || 0;
    },
    durationMinutes: function (amount, unit) {
      var n = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(amount);
      var u = WL.U.lower(unit);
      if (!n) return 0;
      if (/day/.test(u)) return n * 1440;
      if (/hour/.test(u)) return n * 60;
      return n;
    },
    cleanPlaceName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/["“”]/g, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/^(?:at|in|into|inside|to|the)\s+/i, function (m) { return /^the\s+/i.test(m) ? 'The ' : ''; });
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:near|beside|by|on|in|at|with|where)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').replace(/\s+and$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || raw.length < 3) return '';
      if (WL.EventScanner.isGenericPlaceName(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    isGenericPlaceName: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/, '');
      return /^(?:inn|tavern|street|road|door|counter|room|building|shop|market|square|town|city|village|guard|guards|prison|cell|forest|cave|house|home|wall|gate|alley|path|roadside)$/.test(v);
    },
    cleanActorName: function (value) {
      var original = WL.U.norm(value);
      var raw = original.replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:smiles?|frowns?|nods?|laughs?|glares?|swears?|promises?|thanks?|helps?|warns?|watches?|counts?|pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave)(?:\s+and)?$/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:smiles?|frowns?|nods?|laughs?|glares?|swears?|promises?|thanks?|helps?|warns?|watches?|counts?|pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:at|in|near|of|from)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:you|your|yours|he|she|they|it|this|that)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      if (/^(?:your\s+|my\s+|his\s+|her\s+|their\s+|our\s+)/i.test(original)) return '';
      if (/^(?:guard|guards|soldier|soldiers|villager|villagers|people|crowd|goblins?|wolves?|bandits?)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      if (/^(?:ring|keyring|keys?|stamina|energy|body|foot|feet|hands?|arms?|legs?|lungs?|muscles?|face|door|floor|ground|nap|sleep|rest|powers?|dark energy|manual labor|landing|idea|thought|voice|eyes)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      if (/\b(?:stamina|energy|body|foot|feet|hands?|arms?|legs?|lungs?|muscles?|face|nap|sleep|rest|landing|manual labor|dark energy)\b/i.test(raw)) return '';
      if (!/[A-Z]/.test(raw.charAt(0))) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    detectTimeTravelCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:road|route|path|trail|trip|journey)\s+from\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+to\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:takes?|lasts?|is)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var from = WL.U.title(m[1]);
        var to = WL.U.title(m[2]);
        var minutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[3], m[4]);
        if (from && to && minutes) out.push({ type: 'route_base_candidate', from: from, to: to, minutes: minutes, targetId: WL.EventScanner.routeId(from, to), confidence: 'confirmed_route_duration' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(rain|storm|mud|injury|detour|roadblock|fight|ambush|scouting|heavy burden|snow|fog|flood)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:trip|journey|route|travel|road)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:takes?|took|lasts?|lasted)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(rain|storm|mud|injury|detour|roadblock|fight|ambush|scouting|heavy burden|snow|fog|flood)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\bmakes?\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:trip|journey|route|travel)\s+take\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var delayMinutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[2], m[3]);
        if (delayMinutes) out.push({ type: 'route_delay_candidate', reason: WL.U.title(m[1]), actualMinutes: delayMinutes, confidence: 'confirmed_route_delay_reason' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\s+(?:pass|passes|passed|later)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:wait)\s+for\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty|a|an)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var elapsed = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[1], m[2]);
        if (elapsed) out.push({ type: 'elapsed_time_candidate', minutes: elapsed, confidence: 'confirmed_elapsed_time' });
      }
      // Work/labor shifts: time advances, and body/fatigue handling is produced by FormTransformationSystem scanner.
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|twenty)\s+(hours?)\s+pass\s+as\s+you\s+(?:work|labor|train|haul|march|dig|practice)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var workMinutes = WL.EventScanner.durationMinutes(m[1], m[2]);
        if (workMinutes && !out.some(function (e) { return e.type === 'elapsed_time_candidate' && e.minutes === workMinutes; })) out.push({ type: 'elapsed_time_candidate', minutes: workMinutes, confidence: 'confirmed_labor_elapsed_time' });
      }
      if (/\b(?:next day|the following day|tomorrow morning|next morning|the next morning)\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'next_day_candidate', hour: /dawn/i.test(c) ? 5 : 8, minute: 0, confidence: 'confirmed_next_day_language' });
      } else if (/\bbefore dawn\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'next_day_candidate', hour: 5, minute: 0, confidence: 'confirmed_before_dawn_language' });
      }
      if (WL.EventScanner.isRestRecoveryClause(c)) {
        var overnight = /\b(?:night|overnight|morning|dawn|next morning|before dawn)\b/i.test(c);
        var quality = /\b(?:safe|warm|comfortable|bed|inn|room|camp|shelter)\b/i.test(c) ? 'good' : 'ordinary';
        out.push({ type: 'rest_recovery_candidate', minutes: overnight ? 480 : 120, overnight: overnight, targetHour: /before dawn|dawn/i.test(c) ? 5 : 7, targetMinute: 0, restQuality: quality, hpRecover: quality === 'good' ? 25 : 18, mpRecover: quality === 'good' ? 20 : 12, epRecover: quality === 'good' ? 55 : 40, confidence: 'confirmed_rest_recovery' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:must|needs?|need|has to|have to|should)\b[\s\S]{0,100}\bby\s+(sunset|sunrise|dawn|dusk|midnight|noon|tomorrow|tonight|morning|evening|\d{1,2}(?::\d{2})?\s*(?:am|pm)?)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'deadline_candidate', label: WL.U.title(m[1]), task: WL.U.norm(c).slice(0, 120), confidence: 'confirmed_deadline_language' });
      return out;
    },
    isRestRecoveryClause: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      if (!/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:sleep|slept|rest|rested|recover|recovered)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (!/\b(?:night|overnight|morning|recover|recovering|recovered|resting|sleep|slept|safe|bed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:try|tries|tried|attempt|attempts|attempted|almost|cannot|can't|couldn't|fails?|failed)\b[\s\S]{0,50}\b(?:sleep|rest|recover)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:but|however)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:attack|attacks|ambush|interrupt|interrupted|wake|wakes|danger|enemy|monster|screams?|alarm|fire)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      // Place names like "Mourner's Rest" should not count without a sleep/rest verb.
      return true;
    },
    routeId: function (from, to) {
      var a = WL.U.title(from), b = WL.U.title(to);
      var pair = [a, b].sort();
      return 'route_' + WL.U.slug(pair[0] + '_to_' + pair[1]);
    },
    detectWorldPlaceCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/^\s*(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\s+is\s+(?:a|an|the)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,40}?)(?:\s+(near|in|on|at|beside|by)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,100}))?(?:[.!?])?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var placeName = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        var placeType = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        if (placeName && !WL.EventScanner.isGenericPlaceName(placeName) && !/^(?:left|right|north|south|east|west|front|rear|center|centre)$/.test(placeType) && !/^(?:counter|door|stairs|hearth|bar|gate|wall)$/.test(WL.U.lower(placeName))) {
          out.push({ type: 'place_definition_candidate', placeName: placeName, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(placeName), placeType: placeType, locationHint: WL.U.norm((m[3] || '') + ' ' + (m[4] || '')).trim(), confidence: 'confirmed_place_definition' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:enter|arrive at|reach|go to|return to|step into|walk into)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var current = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        if (current) out.push({ type: 'current_place_candidate', placeName: current, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(current), confidence: 'confirmed_current_place' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+owns\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var owner = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var ownedPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (owner && ownedPlace) out.push({ type: 'owner_relation_candidate', actorName: owner, placeName: ownedPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(ownedPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_place_owner' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:works at|works in|serves at|is employed at)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var worker = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var workPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (worker && workPlace) out.push({ type: 'worker_relation_candidate', actorName: worker, placeName: workPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(workPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_place_worker' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:known as|called|named)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b[\s\S]{0,100}\b(?:offers?|is|serves?|rents?|provides?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var infoPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        if (infoPlace) out.push({ type: 'place_definition_candidate', placeName: infoPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(infoPlace), placeType: /\b(?:flophouse|inn|tavern|lodge|hostel|shop|market|prison|guild)\b/i.test(c) ? WL.U.lower((c.match(/\b(flophouse|inn|tavern|lodge|hostel|shop|market|prison|guild)\b/i) || [,'place'])[1]) : 'place', locationHint: 'world-info reference', confidence: 'weak_world_info_place_candidate' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(The\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}|[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})'s\s+(common room|taproom|kitchen|cellar|upper room|barroom|hall|room)\b[\s\S]{0,120}/i);
      if (m) {
        var possessivePlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[1]);
        if (possessivePlace) {
          out.push({ type: 'place_definition_candidate', placeName: possessivePlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace), placeType: 'building', locationHint: WL.U.title(m[2]), confidence: 'possessive_room_place_reference' });
          out.push({ type: 'room_relation_candidate', placeName: possessivePlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace), roomName: WL.U.title(m[2]), roomId: 'room_' + WL.U.slug(possessivePlace + '_' + m[2]), confidence: 'confirmed_room_reference' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(counter|stairs|door|hearth|bar|fountain|notice board|gate|cell door|window)\s+(?:is|are|sits|stands|lies)\s+(?:on|at|to|in)\s+(?:the\s+)?(left|right|north|south|east|west|center|centre|front|rear|back)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'layout_fact_candidate', fixture: WL.U.title(m[1]), value: WL.U.lower(m[2]).replace('centre', 'center'), confidence: 'confirmed_layout_fact' });
      return out;
    },
    detectActorSocialCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/^\s*(?:\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)?\s*(guards?|soldiers?|villagers?|people|crowd|bandits?|goblins?|wolves?)\b[\s\S]{0,120}/i);
      if (m) {
        out.push({ type: 'scene_extra_candidate', label: WL.U.title(m[1]), note: c.slice(0, 160), confidence: 'scene_extra_only' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50})'s\s+(?:sharp\s+)?(?:eyes|voice|hand|hands|smile|face|expression|gaze|attention)\s+(?:snap|snaps|turn|turns|shift|shifts|fix|fixes|fall|falls|lock|locks)\b([\s\S]{0,120})/i);
      if (m) {
        var possActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (possActor) out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: possActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(possActor), socialKind: 'presence_evidence', verb: 'possessive_presence', note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_named_actor_possessive_presence' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(smiles?|frowns?|nods?|laughs?|glares?|swears?|promises?|thanks?|helps?|warns?)\b([\s\S]{0,140})/i);
      if (m) {
        var name = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var verb = WL.U.lower(m[2]);
        var rest = WL.U.lower(m[3] || '');
        if (name) {
          var kind = /swears?/.test(verb) && /revenge|vengeance|kill|pay/i.test(rest) ? 'grudge' : (/smiles?|thanks?|helps?/.test(verb) ? 'positive_attitude' : (/glares?|frowns?|warns?/.test(verb) ? 'negative_attitude' : 'social_evidence'));
          out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: name, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(name), socialKind: kind, verb: verb, note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_named_actor_social' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+owns\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var owner = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var place = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (owner && place) out.push({ type: 'actor_place_relation_candidate', actorName: owner, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(owner), relation: 'owner', placeName: place, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(place), confidence: 'confirmed_actor_place_relation' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:works at|works in|serves at|is employed at)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var worker = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        var workPlace = WL.EventScanner.cleanPlaceName(m[2]);
        if (worker && workPlace) out.push({ type: 'actor_place_relation_candidate', actorName: worker, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(worker), relation: 'worker', placeName: workPlace, placeId: 'place_' + WL.U.slug(workPlace), confidence: 'confirmed_actor_place_relation' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60}?)\s+(?:watches?|counts?|pays?|paid|hands?|gives?|gave)\b([\s\S]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) {
        var evidenceActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (evidenceActor) out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: evidenceActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(evidenceActor), socialKind: /pay|paid|count|hand|give|gave/i.test(m[0]) ? 'transaction_evidence' : 'presence_evidence', verb: 'evidence', note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_named_actor_evidence' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+(?:asks?|asked|hires?|hired|offers?|offered|tasks?|contracts?)\s+you\s+(?:to|with|for)\s+([^.!?;]{3,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        var questGiverActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver(m[1]);
        if (questGiverActor && !/^(?:unknown)$/i.test(questGiverActor)) out.push({ type: 'actor_social_candidate', actorName: questGiverActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(questGiverActor), socialKind: /guild|company|order|circle|clan|synod|dominion|consortium|covenant/i.test(questGiverActor) ? 'faction_or_institution_quest_evidence' : 'quest_giver_evidence', verb: 'quest_offer', note: c.slice(0, 180), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_giver_evidence' });
      }
      return out;
    },

    cleanKnowledgeFact: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^that\s+/i, '').replace(/["“”]/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:,?\s*(?:apparently|they say|so they say))$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || raw.length < 6) return '';
      if (/^(?:nothing|none|unknown|no one knows)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return raw.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + raw.slice(1).replace(/[.!?]+$/g, '');
    },
    socialHolderFromActor: function (name) {
      var clean = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(name || '');
      if (!clean) return null;
      return { holderType: 'actor', holderId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(clean), holderName: clean };
    },
    detectSocialConsequenceCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m, actor, fact, holder;
      if (!c || /\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(c)) return out;

      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:enters?|arrives?|approaches?|joins?|waits?|stands?|sits?|kneels?|leans?|remains?|is here|is nearby|stands nearby|waits nearby)\b([\s\S]{0,100})/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+meet|you\s+find|you\s+notice)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\b([\s\S]{0,100})/i);
      if (m) {
        actor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (actor) out.push({ type: 'presence_actor_candidate', actorName: actor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor), mode: /meet|find|notice/.test(lower) ? 'observed' : 'present', placeId: '', visibility: 'scene', note: c.slice(0, 180), targetId: 'presence_actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor), confidence: 'confirmed_scene_presence' });
      }

      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:leaves?|departs?|walks away|steps out|exits?|vanishes?|disappears?)\b([\s\S]{0,100})/i);
      if (m) {
        actor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (actor) out.push({ type: 'presence_departure_candidate', actorName: actor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor), mode: 'departed', note: c.slice(0, 180), targetId: 'presence_departure_' + WL.U.slug(actor), confidence: 'confirmed_scene_departure' });
      }

      m = c.match(/\b(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+|several\s+|two\s+|three\s+|four\s+|five\s+|\d+\s+)?(guards?|soldiers?|patrons?|villagers?|townsfolk|people|crowd|merchants?|children|workers?|servants?|guild clerks?|watchmen|bystanders?)\s+(?:gather|gathers|stand|stands|wait|waits|watch|watches|stare|stares|murmur|murmurs|pass|passes|flow|flows|linger|lingers|are nearby|are present)\b([\s\S]{0,120})/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(crowd|people|patrons|guards|bystanders)\s+(?:around\s+you\s+)?(?:watch|watches|stare|stares|murmur|murmurs|move|moves|flow|flows)\b([\s\S]{0,120})/i);
      if (m) {
        var group = WL.U.title(m[1]);
        var count = 0;
        var cm = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five)\s+"?"?\s*(?:guards?|soldiers?|patrons?|villagers?|merchants?|workers?|children|bystanders?)\b/i);
        if (cm) count = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(cm[1]);
        out.push({ type: 'presence_generic_candidate', groupLabel: group, groupId: 'presence_group_' + WL.U.slug(group), count: count || 0, mode: /watch|stare|murmur/.test(lower) ? 'observing' : 'present', note: c.slice(0, 180), targetId: 'presence_group_' + WL.U.slug(group), confidence: 'confirmed_generic_scene_presence' });
      }

      if (/\b(?:feel|sense)\s+(?:watched|observed)\b|\bsomeone\s+(?:may be|might be|is)\s+watching\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'presence_hidden_observer_candidate', groupLabel: 'Possible Hidden Observer', groupId: 'presence_hidden_observer', mode: 'possible', note: c.slice(0, 180), targetId: 'presence_hidden_observer', confidence: 'hidden_observer_possible_only' });
      }

      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:tells?|informs?|warns?|reveals?)\s+you\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        actor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        fact = WL.EventScanner.cleanKnowledgeFact(m[2]);
        if (actor && fact) out.push({ type: 'knowledge_fact_candidate', holderType: 'player', holderId: 'player', holderName: root && root.player && root.player.name || 'Player', fact: fact, sourceActorName: actor, sourceActorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor), sourceMode: /warn/.test(WL.U.lower(m[0])) ? 'warned_by_actor' : 'told_by_actor', truthStatus: 'alleged', visibilityScope: 'private_player', confidence: 'actor_told_player_knowledge', targetId: 'knowledge_player_' + WL.U.slug(fact) });
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:learn|learned|discover|discovered|realize|realized|notice|noticed|overhear|overheard)\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        fact = WL.EventScanner.cleanKnowledgeFact(m[1]);
        if (fact) out.push({ type: 'knowledge_fact_candidate', holderType: 'player', holderId: 'player', holderName: root && root.player && root.player.name || 'Player', fact: fact, sourceActorName: '', sourceActorId: '', sourceMode: /overhear/i.test(m[0]) ? 'overheard' : 'discovered', truthStatus: /overhear/i.test(m[0]) ? 'alleged' : 'observed', visibilityScope: 'private_player', confidence: 'player_learned_knowledge', targetId: 'knowledge_player_' + WL.U.slug(fact) });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:learns?|discovers?|realizes?|knows?|suspects?)\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        holder = WL.EventScanner.socialHolderFromActor(m[1]);
        fact = WL.EventScanner.cleanKnowledgeFact(m[2]);
        if (holder && fact) out.push({ type: 'knowledge_fact_candidate', holderType: holder.holderType, holderId: holder.holderId, holderName: holder.holderName, fact: fact, sourceActorName: '', sourceActorId: '', sourceMode: /suspects?/i.test(m[0]) ? 'inferred_suspicion' : 'learned', truthStatus: /suspects?/i.test(m[0]) ? 'uncertain' : 'alleged', visibilityScope: 'private_actor', confidence: 'actor_learned_knowledge', targetId: 'knowledge_' + holder.holderId + '_' + WL.U.slug(fact) });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:everyone|the\s+town|the\s+village|the\s+city|locals|people)\s+(?:knows?|learns?|hears?)\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        fact = WL.EventScanner.cleanKnowledgeFact(m[1]);
        if (fact) out.push({ type: 'knowledge_fact_candidate', holderType: 'public', holderId: 'public_local', holderName: 'Local Public', fact: fact, sourceActorName: '', sourceActorId: '', sourceMode: 'public_knowledge_statement', truthStatus: 'alleged', visibilityScope: 'local_public', confidence: 'explicit_local_public_knowledge', targetId: 'knowledge_public_local_' + WL.U.slug(fact) });
      }
      return out;
    },
    witnessEventKind: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      if (/\b(?:steal|stole|snatch|pocket|pickpocket|rob|loot)\b/.test(lower)) return 'property_or_theft_visible_event';
      if (/\b(?:attack|stab|slash|strike|punch|kill|murder|cut|shoot|burn)\b/.test(lower)) return 'violence_visible_event';
      if (/\b(?:cast|spell|fireball|summon|transform|shapeshift|evolve)\b/.test(lower)) return 'supernatural_visible_event';
      if (/\b(?:pay|paid|hand|hands|give|gives|receive|receives|buy|sell|trade)\b/.test(lower)) return 'transaction_visible_event';
      if (/\b(?:shout|argue|threaten|insult|confess|admit)\b/.test(lower)) return 'social_visible_event';
      return 'visible_event';
    },
    witnessActionEligible: function (clause) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(clause);
      if (/\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(clause || '')) return false;
      if (/\b(?:dream|vision|rumor|rumour|joke|example sentence|instruction)\b/i.test(lower)) return false;
      return /\b(?:steal|stole|snatch|pocket|pickpocket|rob|loot|attack|stab|slash|strike|punch|kill|murder|cut|shoot|burn|cast|spell|fireball|summon|transform|shapeshift|evolve|pay|paid|hand|hands|give|gives|receive|receives|buy|sell|trade|shout|argue|threaten|insult|confess|admit|draws?|brandish(?:es)?|break|breaks)\b/i.test(clause || '');
    },
    explicitWitnessesFromClause: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      function add(kind, id, name, mode, confidence) {
        if (!id || seen[id]) return;
        seen[id] = true;
        out.push({ kind: kind, id: id, name: name, mode: mode || 'witness', confidence: confidence || 'explicit_witness' });
      }
      var groupRe = /\b(?:the\s+)?(guards?|watchmen|crowd|bystanders?|patrons?|people|villagers?|townsfolk|merchants?|workers?|servants?)\s+(?:see|sees|saw|watch|watches|watched|notice|notices|noticed|spot|spots|spotted|witness|witnesses|witnessed|overhear|overhears|overheard|stare|stares)\b/i;
      var gm = c.match(groupRe);
      if (gm) add('group', 'presence_group_' + WL.U.slug(gm[1]), WL.U.title(gm[1]), 'explicit_group_witness', 'explicit_group_observer');
      var asRe = /\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:see|sees|saw|watch|watches|watched|notice|notices|noticed|spot|spots|spotted|witness|witnesses|witnessed|overhear|overhears|overheard)\s+(?:you|your|as\s+you)\b/i;
      var m = c.match(asRe);
      if (m) {
        var actor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]);
        if (actor) add('actor', 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor), actor, 'explicit_named_witness', 'explicit_named_observer');
      }
      var beforeRe = /\b(?:in\s+front\s+of|before|under\s+the\s+eyes\s+of)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2}|guards?|watchmen|crowd|bystanders?|patrons?|people)\b/i;
      m = c.match(beforeRe);
      if (m) {
        var raw = m[1];
        if (/^(?:guards?|watchmen|crowd|bystanders?|patrons?|people)$/i.test(raw)) add('group', 'presence_group_' + WL.U.slug(raw), WL.U.title(raw), 'explicit_group_witness', 'explicit_group_observer');
        else { var a = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(raw); if (a) add('actor', 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(a), a, 'explicit_named_witness', 'explicit_named_observer'); }
      }
      return out;
    },
    ambientWitnessesFromPresence: function (root, clause) {
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      function add(kind, id, name, mode, confidence) {
        if (!id || seen[id]) return;
        seen[id] = true;
        out.push({ kind: kind, id: id, name: name, mode: mode || 'ambient_presence', confidence: confidence || 'ambient_scene_presence' });
      }
      if (!root || !root.social || !root.social.presence) return out;
      var actorIds = root.social.presence.actorIds || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < actorIds.length && out.length < 8; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.presence.actorsById[actorIds[i]];
        if (!rec || rec.mode === 'departed') continue;
        add('actor', rec.actorId, rec.actorName, rec.mode || 'present', rec.visibility === 'hidden' ? 'low_hidden_possible' : 'ambient_named_present');
      }
      var groupIds = root.social.presence.genericGroupIds || [];
      for (var g = 0; g < groupIds.length && out.length < 12; g++) {
        var gr = root.social.presence.genericGroupsById[groupIds[g]];
        if (!gr) continue;
        add('group', gr.groupId, gr.label + (gr.count ? ' x' + gr.count : ''), gr.mode || 'present', /observ/.test(gr.mode || '') ? 'ambient_group_observing' : 'ambient_group_present');
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectWitnessCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var lowQuick = WL.U.lower(c);
      var witnessMarkers = ['steal','stole','snatch','pocket','rob','loot','attack','stab','slash','strike','punch','kill','murder','cut','shoot','burn','cast','spell','fireball','summon','transform','shapeshift','evolve','pay','paid','hand','give','receive','buy','sell','trade','shout','argue','threaten','insult','confess','admit','draw','brandish','break'];
      var maybeWitness = false;
      for (var wi = 0; wi < witnessMarkers.length; wi++) { if (lowQuick.indexOf(witnessMarkers[wi]) >= 0) { maybeWitness = true; break; } }
      if (!maybeWitness) return out;
      if (!WL.EventScanner.witnessActionEligible(c)) return out;
      var explicitNone = /\b(?:no\s+one|nobody)\s+(?:sees|saw|watches|watched|notices|noticed|hears|heard)|\b(?:unseen|unnoticed|out\s+of\s+sight|hidden\s+from\s+view|alone\s+in\s+the\s+(?:room|alley|street|cell))\b/i.test(c);
      var witnesses = explicitNone ? [] : WL.EventScanner.explicitWitnessesFromClause(c);
      var confidence = witnesses.length ? 'explicit_witnessed_event' : 'unwitnessed_or_private_event';
      var visibility = witnesses.length ? 'public_or_observed' : 'private_or_unseen';
      if (!witnesses.length && !explicitNone) {
        witnesses = WL.EventScanner.ambientWitnessesFromPresence(root, c);
        if (witnesses.length) { confidence = 'ambient_presence_witness_possible'; visibility = 'scene_possible'; }
      }
      if (!witnesses.length && !explicitNone) return out;
      out.push({
        type: 'witness_record_candidate',
        targetId: 'witness_' + WL.U.hash(c).slice(0, 16),
        eventKind: WL.EventScanner.witnessEventKind(c),
        eventSummary: c.slice(0, 180),
        witnesses: witnesses,
        witnessed: witnesses.length > 0,
        visibility: visibility,
        privacy: witnesses.length ? 'observed_by_witnesses' : 'not_observed_by_known_witnesses',
        confidence: confidence,
        note: c.slice(0, 180)
      });
      return out;
    },
    reputationAxisFromText: function (text) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      if (/\btrust|trusted|trusts|reliable|honest|confidence\b/.test(lower)) return 'trust';
      if (/\brespect|respected|honor|honour|admire|admires|impressed|praise|praises|praised\b/.test(lower)) return 'respect';
      if (/\bgrateful|gratitude|thanks|thankful|owes you|owes him|owes her\b/.test(lower)) return 'gratitude';
      if (/\bfear|fears|feared|afraid|intimidated|dangerous|terrified\b/.test(lower)) return 'fear';
      if (/\bsuspect|suspects|suspicious|distrust|distrusts|untrustworthy|watchlist|watched by\b/.test(lower)) return 'suspicion';
      if (/\bhostile|hostility|anger|angry|hates|hate|hated|enemy|condemn|condemns|condemned|dislike|dislikes\b/.test(lower)) return 'hostility';
      if (/\bnotorious|notoriety|infamous|wanted|criminal|dangerous reputation|bad reputation\b/.test(lower)) return 'notoriety';
      return 'standing';
    },
    reputationDeltaFromText: function (text) {
      var lower = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      var axis = WL.EventScanner.reputationAxisFromText(lower);
      var base = /\b(strongly|deeply|openly|clearly|majorly|greatly|far more|much more)\b/.test(lower) ? 2 : 1;
      if (/\b(no longer|less|loses|lose|lost|worsens|worsen|worsened|falls|drops|declines|distrust|distrusts|hates|hate|hostile|condemn|condemns|criminal|wanted|infamous|notorious|suspicious|suspects|fear|fears|afraid|dangerous)\b/.test(lower)) {
        if (axis === 'respect' || axis === 'trust' || axis === 'gratitude' || axis === 'standing') return -base;
        return base;
      }
      if (/\b(improves|improve|improved|rises|rise|grows|trusts|respects|praises|thanks|grateful|admires|honors|honours|is impressed|becomes better|warms to you)\b/.test(lower)) return base;
      return base;
    },
    cleanReputationScopeName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your)\b\s*/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:now|already|clearly|openly|quietly)$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:you|your|it|they|he|she|someone|no one|nobody)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    reputationScopeRecord: function (root, type, name, mode) {
      name = WL.EventScanner.cleanReputationScopeName(name || '');
      if (!name) return null;
      var scopeType = type || 'local';
      var scopeName = name;
      var scopeId;
      if (scopeType === 'actor') {
        var actor = WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(name);
        if (!actor) return null;
        scopeName = actor;
        scopeId = 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor);
      } else if (scopeType === 'faction') {
        scopeId = 'faction_' + WL.U.slug(scopeName);
      } else if (scopeType === 'group') {
        scopeId = 'group_' + WL.U.slug(scopeName);
      } else {
        var placeName = scopeName;
        if (!placeName || /^local public$/i.test(placeName)) placeName = WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : 'Local Public';
        if (!placeName || placeName === '(unknown)') placeName = 'Local Public';
        scopeName = placeName;
        scopeId = 'local_' + WL.U.slug(placeName);
        scopeType = 'local';
      }
      return { scopeType: scopeType, scopeId: scopeId, scopeName: scopeName, visibilityScope: mode || (scopeType === 'actor' ? 'private_actor' : (scopeType === 'faction' ? 'faction_scoped' : 'local_public')) };
    },
    detectReputationCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      if (!c || /\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (!/(trust|respect|fear|suspect|distrust|praise|condemn|reputation|standing|opinion|view|regard|dangerous|worsens?|improves?|hates?|likes?|dislikes?|grateful|admire|thanks?)/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (/\b(?:dream|vision|rumor|rumour|joke|example sentence|instruction|pretend)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      function push(scope, matchedText, note) {
        if (!scope) return;
        var axis = WL.EventScanner.reputationAxisFromText(matchedText || c);
        var delta = WL.EventScanner.reputationDeltaFromText(matchedText || c);
        out.push({
          type: 'reputation_evidence_candidate',
          targetId: 'reputation_' + scope.scopeId + '_' + axis,
          scopeType: scope.scopeType,
          scopeId: scope.scopeId,
          scopeName: scope.scopeName,
          axis: axis,
          delta: WL.U.clamp(delta, -3, 3),
          visibilityScope: scope.visibilityScope,
          sourceMode: 'explicit_reputation_statement',
          confidence: 'explicit_reputation_evidence',
          note: (note || c).slice(0, 180)
        });
      }
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:now\s+)?(?:trusts?|respects?|fears?|hates?|distrusts?|suspects?|likes?|dislikes?|thanks?|praises?|condemns?|admires?)\s+you\b([^.!?]{0,120})/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, 'actor', m[1], 'private_actor'), m[0], c);

      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})['’]s\s+(?:opinion|view|trust|respect|attitude)\s+(?:of|toward|towards)\s+you\s+(?:improves?|worsens?|rises?|falls?|softens?|hardens?)\b/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, 'actor', m[1], 'private_actor'), m[0], c);

      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(guards?|watchmen|crowd|bystanders?|patrons?|locals|townsfolk|villagers?|merchants?|workers?)\s+(?:now\s+)?(?:trust|trusts|respect|respects|fear|fears|suspect|suspects|distrust|distrusts|praise|praises|condemn|condemns|regard|regards|see|sees)\s+you\s*(?:as\s+([^.!?]{2,80}))?/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, /locals|townsfolk|villagers|crowd|bystanders/i.test(m[1]) ? 'local' : 'group', m[1], /locals|townsfolk|villagers|crowd|bystanders/i.test(m[1]) ? 'local_public' : 'group_public'), m[0] + ' ' + (m[2] || ''), c);

      m = c.match(/\b(?:your\s+)?(?:reputation|standing)\s+(?:in|around|within)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\s+(?:improves?|worsens?|rises?|falls?|grows|drops|declines|spreads|is damaged|is strengthened)\b/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, 'local', m[1], 'local_public'), m[0], c);

      m = c.match(/\b(?:your\s+)?(?:standing|reputation)\s+with\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90})\s+(?:improves?|worsens?|rises?|falls?|grows|drops|declines|is damaged|is strengthened)\b/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, 'faction', m[1], 'faction_scoped'), m[0], c);

      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90})\s+(?:guild|company|clan|circle|dominion|consortium|synod|order|faction)\s+(?:now\s+)?(?:trusts?|respects?|fears?|suspects?|distrusts?|praises?|condemns?)\s+you\b/i);
      if (m) push(WL.EventScanner.reputationScopeRecord(root, 'faction', m[1], 'faction_scoped'), m[0], c);
      return out;
    },

    rumorCleanSummary: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^that\s+/i, '').replace(/["“”]/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:,?\s*(?:so\s+they\s+say|apparently|or\s+so\s+the\s+rumor\s+goes))$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || raw.length < 6) return '';
      if (/^(?:nothing|none|unknown|no\s+one\s+knows)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return raw.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + raw.slice(1).replace(/[.!?]+$/g, '');
    },
    rumorScopeFromText: function (raw, root) {
      var name = WL.U.norm(raw || '').replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      if (!name || /^(?:here|nearby|locally|locals|people|town|city)$/i.test(name)) name = WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : 'Local Public';
      if (!name || name === '(unknown)') name = 'Local Public';
      var lower = WL.U.lower(name);
      var type = /tavern|inn|market|marketplace|square|street|guild|barracks|dock|port|temple|court|camp|caravan/.test(lower) ? 'local_channel' : (/guild|company|clan|circle|dominion|consortium|synod|order|faction/.test(lower) ? 'faction_channel' : 'local');
      return { scopeType: type, scopeId: 'rumor_scope_' + WL.U.slug(name), scopeName: WL.U.title(name), visibilityScope: type === 'faction_channel' ? 'faction_network' : 'local_rumor_network' };
    },
    rumorChannelFromScope: function (name, text) {
      var low = WL.U.lower((name || '') + ' ' + (text || ''));
      if (/tavern|inn|patron|ale|common room/.test(low)) return 'tavern_gossip';
      if (/market|square|merchant|shop|trade/.test(low)) return 'market_gossip';
      if (/guild|contract|adventurer/.test(low)) return 'guild_network';
      if (/road|traveler|traveller|caravan|messenger/.test(low)) return 'traveler_route';
      if (/guard|watch|barracks/.test(low)) return 'watch_network';
      if (/court|noble|palace/.test(low)) return 'court_gossip';
      return 'local_word_of_mouth';
    },
    rumorDelayTurns: function (text) {
      var low = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      var n = 0;
      var m = low.match(/\b(?:in|after|within)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven)\s+(minutes?|hours?|days?|weeks?)\b/);
      if (m) {
        n = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        if (/minute/.test(m[2])) return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(n / 30));
        if (/hour/.test(m[2])) return Math.max(1, Math.ceil(n / 6));
        if (/day/.test(m[2])) return Math.max(2, n * 4);
        if (/week/.test(m[2])) return Math.max(8, n * 28);
      }
      if (/by\s+(?:morning|evening|night|dawn|dusk)/.test(low)) return 2;
      if (/traveler|traveller|caravan|messenger|road|distant|far\s+village|remote/.test(low)) return 6;
      return 1;
    },
    detectRumorCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      if (!c || /\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (/\b(?:dream|vision|joke|example sentence|instruction|pretend)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
