      if (/\b(?:not\s+a\s+rumou?r|only\s+a\s+rumou?r|just\s+a\s+rumou?r|mere\s+rumou?r)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (!/\b(?:rumou?rs?|gossip|whispers?|word|news|talk|hearsay)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      function push(scopeRaw, summaryRaw, sourceRaw, modeText, explicitTargetRaw) {
        var summary = WL.EventScanner.rumorCleanSummary(summaryRaw);
        if (!summary) return;
        var scope = WL.EventScanner.rumorScopeFromText(scopeRaw || '', root);
        var target = explicitTargetRaw ? WL.EventScanner.rumorScopeFromText(explicitTargetRaw, root) : null;
        var channel = WL.EventScanner.rumorChannelFromScope(scope.scopeName, c);
        var delay = WL.EventScanner.rumorDelayTurns(c);
        var sourceActor = sourceRaw ? WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(sourceRaw) : '';
        var distortion = /\b(?:exaggerated|twisted|distorted|garbled|wild|ugly|false|half-true|half true)\b/i.test(c) ? 2 : (/\b(?:whisper|gossip|hearsay|claim|claims)\b/i.test(c) ? 1 : 0);
        var confidence = /\b(?:confirmed|reliable|clear|everyone saw|witnessed)\b/i.test(c) ? 0.75 : (/\b(?:claim|claims|hearsay|whisper|gossip|rumou?r)\b/i.test(c) ? 0.45 : 0.6);
        out.push({
          type: 'rumor_event_candidate',
          targetId: 'rumor_' + WL.U.hash(summary + '|' + scope.scopeId + '|' + channel).slice(0, 16),
          summary: summary,
          sourceActorName: sourceActor,
          sourceActorId: sourceActor ? 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(sourceActor) : '',
          sourceMode: modeText || 'rumor_spread_statement',
          originScopeType: scope.scopeType,
          originScopeId: scope.scopeId,
          originScopeName: scope.scopeName,
          visibilityScope: scope.visibilityScope,
          targetScopeId: target ? target.scopeId : '',
          targetScopeName: target ? target.scopeName : '',
          channel: channel,
          delayTurns: delay,
          confidenceScore: confidence,
          distortion: distortion,
          routeHint: WL.U.norm((explicitTargetRaw ? (scope.scopeName + ' -> ' + target.scopeName) : scope.scopeName) + ' via ' + channel),
          note: c.slice(0, 180)
        });
      }
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:rumou?rs?|gossip|whispers?)\s+(?:spread|spreads|circulate|circulates|travel|travels|move|moves)\s+(?:through|around|across|within|in|among)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?]{2,80}?)\s+(?:that|claim(?:s)?|say(?:s)?)\s+([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) push(m[1], m[2], '', 'rumor_spread_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:word|news|talk)\s+(?:spread|spreads|travels|travel|circulates|circulate|moves|move)\s+(?:through|around|across|within|in|among)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?]{2,80}?)\s+that\s+([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) push(m[1], m[2], '', 'word_spread_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:spreads?|passes?|starts?|whispers?)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:rumou?r|gossip|word)\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) push('Local Public', m[2], m[1], 'actor_spread_rumor');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([^.!?]{2,80}?)\s+(?:hears?|learns?|picks\s+up)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:rumou?r|gossip|word)\s+(?:that\s+)?([^.!?]{6,180})/i);
      if (m) push(m[1], m[2], '', 'scope_heard_rumor');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:rumou?r|word|news|gossip)\s+(?:reaches|arrives\s+in|travels\s+to)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})(?:\s+(?:that|claiming|saying)\s+([^.!?]{6,180}))?/i);
      if (m) push('Traveler Route', m[2] || 'A prior rumor reaches ' + WL.U.title(m[1]), '', 'rumor_route_reaches_target', m[1]);
      m = c.match(/\b(?:travelers?|travellers?|messengers?|caravaners?)\s+(?:say|claim|report)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:rumou?r|word|news|gossip)\s+(?:may\s+|will\s+|could\s+)?(?:reach|arrive\s+in|travel\s+to)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})(?:\s+(?:that|claiming|saying)\s+([^.!?]{6,180}))?/i);
      if (m) push('Traveler Route', m[2] || 'A prior rumor may reach ' + WL.U.title(m[1]), '', 'rumor_route_reaches_target', m[1]);
      return out;
    },
    cleanCrimeItemName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^that\s+/i, '').replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\b(?:while|when|before|after|because|from|in|inside|with|without|as)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\b(?:unseen|quietly|secretly|publicly|openly|again)$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:it|something|anything|nothing|none|unknown)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    cleanCrimeVictimName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\b(?:with|using|near|inside|outside|before|after|while)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:you|yourself|someone|anyone|nobody|no one)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    crimeKindFromText: function (text) {
      var low = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      if (/\b(?:steal|stole|stolen|theft|pickpocket|shoplift|rob|robs|robbed|loot|loots|looted)\b/.test(low)) return 'theft';
      if (/\b(?:murder|murders|murdered|kill|kills|killed|slay|slays|slain)\b/.test(low)) return 'killing';
      if (/\b(?:assault|assaults|assaulted|attack|attacks|attacked|strike|strikes|struck|stab|stabs|stabbed)\b/.test(low)) return 'assault';
      if (/\b(?:break\s+into|broke\s+into|trespass|trespasses|trespassed|sneak\s+into|sneaks\s+into)\b/.test(low)) return 'trespass';
      if (/\b(?:burn|burns|burned|set\s+fire|arson|vandalize|vandalizes|vandalized|smash|smashes|smashed)\b/.test(low)) return 'property_damage';
      if (/\b(?:bribe|bribes|bribed|blackmail|blackmails|blackmailed|forge|forges|forged)\b/.test(low)) return 'corruption_or_fraud';
      return 'unlawful_act';
    },
    detectCrimeEvidenceCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      if (!c || /\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (/\b(?:dream|vision|joke|example sentence|instruction|pretend|would steal|could steal|might steal|plan to steal|want to steal)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      var low = WL.U.lower(c);
      var hidden = /\b(?:no one sees|nobody sees|unseen|without witnesses|without anyone seeing|in secret|secretly|quietly|hidden from view)\b/i.test(c);
      var explicitWitnessed = /\b(?:watches?|sees?|saw|witnesses?|caught|spots?|notices?|guards? see|crowd sees|in public|publicly|openly)\b/i.test(c) && !hidden;
      function pushCrime(kind, itemName, victimName, mode) {
        var suspectName = root && root.player && root.player.name && root.player.name !== 'Unknown' ? root.player.name : 'Player';
        var item = WL.EventScanner.cleanCrimeItemName(itemName || '');
        var victim = WL.EventScanner.cleanCrimeVictimName(victimName || '');
        var basis = [kind, suspectName, item || victim || 'unknown', WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : 'unknown'].join('|');
        out.push({
          type: 'crime_case_candidate',
          targetId: 'crime_' + WL.U.hash(basis).slice(0, 16),
          crimeKind: kind,
          suspectId: 'player',
          suspectName: suspectName,
          victimId: victim ? 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(victim) : '',
          victimName: victim,
          itemId: item ? 'item_' + WL.U.slug(item) : '',
          itemName: item,
          placeId: root && root.places ? (root.places.currentId || '') : '',
          placeName: WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : '',
          visibility: hidden ? 'hidden_or_unseen' : (explicitWitnessed ? 'observed_or_public' : 'unknown_visibility'),
          witnessedHint: explicitWitnessed,
          hiddenHint: hidden,
          status: hidden ? 'hidden_unreported' : (explicitWitnessed ? 'witnessed_unadjudicated' : 'suspected_or_unconfirmed'),
          proofLevel: hidden ? 0 : (explicitWitnessed ? 1 : 0),
          sourceMode: mode || 'crime_event_statement',
          note: c.slice(0, 220),
          confidence: hidden ? 'confirmed_hidden_crime_event' : (explicitWitnessed ? 'confirmed_witnessed_crime_event' : 'crime_event_candidate')
        });
      }
      function pushClue(kind, summaryRaw, itemRaw, foundByRaw, mode) {
        var item = WL.EventScanner.cleanCrimeItemName(itemRaw || '');
        var foundBy = foundByRaw ? WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(foundByRaw) : '';
        var summary = WL.U.norm(summaryRaw || c).replace(/[.!?]+$/g, '').slice(0, 200);
        if (!summary || summary.length < 5) return;
        out.push({
          type: 'clue_evidence_candidate',
          targetId: 'clue_' + WL.U.hash(kind + '|' + summary + '|' + item).slice(0, 16),
          clueKind: kind || 'evidence',
          summary: summary.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + summary.slice(1),
          itemId: item ? 'item_' + WL.U.slug(item) : '',
          itemName: item,
          foundById: foundBy ? 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(foundBy) : '',
          foundByName: foundBy,
          placeId: root && root.places ? (root.places.currentId || '') : '',
          placeName: WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : '',
          proofLevel: /\b(?:bloody|fingerprint|marked|stolen|found in your pack|in your pocket|witness statement)\b/i.test(c) ? 2 : 1,
          visibilityScope: foundBy ? 'holder_known' : 'evidence_recorded',
          sourceMode: mode || 'evidence_statement',
          note: c.slice(0, 220),
          confidence: 'confirmed_evidence_or_clue'
        });
      }
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:secretly\s+|quietly\s+|openly\s+|publicly\s+)?(?:steal|stole|snatch|snatched|pocket|pocketed|rob|robbed|loot|looted|shoplift|shoplifted)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) pushCrime('theft', m[1], '', 'player_theft_event');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:Mira|[A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2}|the\s+guards?|the\s+watch|the\s+crowd)\s+(?:sees?|saw|watches?|witnesses?|catches?|spots?|notices?)\s+you\s+(?:steal|stole|snatch|snatched|pocket|pocketed|rob|robbed|loot|looted)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) pushCrime('theft', m[1], '', 'witnessed_player_theft_event');
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:kill|killed|murder|murdered|slay|slain|stab|stabbed|attack|attacked|assault|assaulted|strike|struck)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) pushCrime(WL.EventScanner.crimeKindFromText(c), '', m[1], 'player_violence_event');
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:break\s+into|broke\s+into|trespass\s+in|trespassed\s+in|sneak\s+into|sneaked\s+into|sneaks\s+into)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) pushCrime('trespass', '', m[1], 'trespass_event');
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:burn|burned|set\s+fire\s+to|vandalize|vandalized|smash|smashed)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) pushCrime('property_damage', m[1], '', 'property_damage_event');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:guards?|watchmen|the\s+watch|Mira|[A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:finds?|found|discovers?|discovered|notices?|noticed)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:stolen\s+)?([^.!?;]{2,90})\s+(?:in|inside|on|near)\s+(?:your|Zack's)\s+(?:pack|bag|pocket|hand|room|bedroll|wagon)/i);
      if (m) pushClue('found_possession_evidence', 'Found ' + WL.EventScanner.cleanCrimeItemName(m[1]) + ' in suspect possession', m[1], (m[0].match(/^(guards?|watchmen|the\s+watch|Mira|[A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})/i) || [''])[1] || '', 'evidence_found_on_suspect');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:guards?|watchmen|the\s+watch|investigators?|Mira|[A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:finds?|found|discovers?|discovered)\s+([^.!?;]{4,120}?(?:footprints?|blood|fingerprints?|broken\s+lock|scraps?|witness\s+statement|dropped\s+knife|torn\s+cloth))/i);
      if (m) pushClue('physical_clue', m[1], '', (m[0].match(/^(guards?|watchmen|the\s+watch|investigators?|Mira|[A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})/i) || [''])[1] || '', 'physical_evidence_found');
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z']+(?:\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z']+){0,2})\s+(?:accuses?|accused)\s+you\s+of\s+([^.!?;]{4,120})/i);
      if (m) pushClue('accusation_statement', m[1] + ' accuses you of ' + m[2], '', m[1], 'accusation_not_proof');
      return out;
    },


    cleanLawScopeName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\b(?:posts?|issues?|declares?|opens?|begins?|starts?|tries?|attempts?|orders?|drops?|closes?|dismisses?)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:you|your|it|they|someone|no one|nobody|rumor|gossip)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    lawAuthorityFromText: function (raw, fallback) {
      var name = WL.EventScanner.cleanLawScopeName(raw || '') || fallback || 'Local Authority';
      var low = WL.U.lower(name);
      var type = /watch|guard|watchmen|constable|sheriff|marshal/.test(low) ? 'watch' : (/court|magistrate|judge|tribunal/.test(low) ? 'court' : (/guild|company|clan|circle|dominion|consortium|synod|order|faction/.test(low) ? 'faction' : 'local_authority'));
      return { authorityType: type, authorityId: 'law_auth_' + WL.U.slug(name), authorityName: name };
    },
    lawScopeFromText: function (raw, root) {
      var name = WL.EventScanner.cleanLawScopeName(raw || '');
      if (!name || /^(?:watch|guards?|court|magistrate|local authority)$/i.test(name)) name = WL.ContextBudgeter && root ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : 'Local Law';
      if (!name || name === '(unknown)') name = 'Local Law';
      return { scopeType: /guild|company|clan|circle|dominion|consortium|synod|order|faction/i.test(name) ? 'faction_law' : 'local_law', scopeId: 'law_scope_' + WL.U.slug(name), scopeName: WL.U.title(name) };
    },
    lawStageFromText: function (text) {
      var low = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      if (/\bwarrants?\b|\bwanted\b/.test(low)) return 'warrant_posted';
      if (/\barrest|seize|detain|take you in|takes you in\b/.test(low)) return 'arrest_attempt';
      if (/\bfine|fines|penalty|penalizes\b/.test(low)) return 'fine_declared';
      if (/\bblacklist|ban|exile|sanction|expel\b/.test(low)) return 'sanction_declared';
      if (/\binvestigat|inquiry|case opened|opens? a case|begins? looking into\b/.test(low)) return 'investigation_opened';
      if (/\bdrop|dismiss|close|cleared|acquitted\b/.test(low)) return 'case_closed_or_dropped';
      return 'law_response_recorded';
    },
    detectFactionLawCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      if (!c || /\[WorldLedger\]|Command:\s*\/|WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META/i.test(c)) return out;
      if (/\b(?:dream|vision|joke|example sentence|instruction|pretend|rumou?r says|gossip claims|hearsay)\b/i.test(c)) return out;
      var m;
      function pushResponse(authorityRaw, actionText, scopeRaw, mode) {
        var authority = WL.EventScanner.lawAuthorityFromText(authorityRaw, 'Local Authority');
        var scope = WL.EventScanner.lawScopeFromText(scopeRaw || authority.authorityName, root);
        var stage = WL.EventScanner.lawStageFromText(actionText || c);
        var amount = 0, currency = '';
        var fm = c.match(/\b(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
        if (fm && /fine|penalty|penal/.test(WL.U.lower(c))) { amount = Number(fm[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(fm[2]); }
        var severity = /murder|kill|arson|warrant|arrest|wanted|blacklist|exile/i.test(c) ? 3 : (/theft|stolen|assault|fine|investigat|case/i.test(c) ? 2 : 1);
        out.push({
          type: 'law_response_candidate',
          targetId: 'law_response_' + WL.U.hash(authority.authorityId + '|' + stage + '|' + c).slice(0, 16),
          authorityType: authority.authorityType,
          authorityId: authority.authorityId,
          authorityName: authority.authorityName,
          scopeType: scope.scopeType,
          scopeId: scope.scopeId,
          scopeName: scope.scopeName,
          responseStage: stage,
          responseStatus: stage === 'case_closed_or_dropped' ? 'closed_or_reduced_by_story' : 'active_risk_record',
          amount: amount,
          currency: currency,
          severity: severity,
          suspectId: 'player',
          suspectName: root && root.player && root.player.name && root.player.name !== 'Unknown' ? root.player.name : 'Player',
          sourceMode: mode || 'explicit_law_response_statement',
          note: c.slice(0, 220),
          confidence: 'explicit_law_enforcement_response'
        });
      }
      function pushLaw(scopeRaw, lawKind, severity, mode) {
        var scope = WL.EventScanner.lawScopeFromText(scopeRaw, root);
        var kind = lawKind || WL.EventScanner.crimeKindFromText(c);
        out.push({
          type: 'law_record_candidate',
          targetId: 'law_record_' + scope.scopeId + '_' + WL.U.slug(kind),
          scopeType: scope.scopeType,
          scopeId: scope.scopeId,
          scopeName: scope.scopeName,
          lawKind: kind,
          severity: WL.U.clamp(severity || (/murder|arson|treason/i.test(c) ? 3 : 2), 1, 5),
          sourceMode: mode || 'explicit_law_rule_statement',
          note: c.slice(0, 220),
          confidence: 'explicit_law_scope_record'
        });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:in|within|around)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}),?\s+(?:theft|murder|assault|trespass|arson|bribery|blackmail|forgery)\s+(?:is|counts as|is treated as)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:crime|offense|offence|serious crime|capital crime)/i);
      if (m) pushLaw(m[1], WL.EventScanner.crimeKindFromText(c), /serious|capital|murder|arson/i.test(c) ? 3 : 2, 'local_law_rule_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|city watch|town watch|guards?|watchmen|court|magistrate|Adventurers Guild|Iron Dominion)\s+(?:opens?|begins?|starts?|launches?)\s+(?:an?\s+)?(?:investigation|inquiry|case)\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'investigation_opened_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|city watch|town watch|guards?|watchmen|court|magistrate|Adventurers Guild|Iron Dominion)\s+(?:posts?|issues?|declares?)\s+(?:a\s+)?(?:warrant|wanted notice)\s+(?:for|against)\s+(?:you|Zack|the player)\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'warrant_posted_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|city watch|town watch|guards?|watchmen|court|magistrate|Adventurers Guild|Iron Dominion)\s+(?:try|tries|attempts?|moves?)\s+to\s+(?:arrest|detain|seize)\s+(?:you|Zack|the player)\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'arrest_attempt_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|court|magistrate|guild|watch|guards?)\s+(?:fines?|penalizes?)\s+(?:you|Zack|the player)\s+(?:for\s+)?(\d{1,6}\s+(?:copper|silver|gold))?\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'fine_declared_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|guild|company|clan|circle|dominion|consortium|synod|order|faction)\s+(?:blacklists?|bans?|sanctions?|expels?)\s+(?:you|Zack|the player)\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'faction_sanction_statement');
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}|city watch|town watch|guards?|watchmen|court|magistrate|Adventurers Guild|Iron Dominion)\s+(?:drops?|dismisses?|closes?)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:case|charges?|investigation)\b([^.!?]{0,160})/i);
      if (m) pushResponse(m[1], m[0], '', 'case_closed_statement');
      return out;
    },
    cleanCreatureLabel: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|remaining)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:attack|attacks|charge|charges|march|marches|advance|advances|appear|appears|surround|surrounds|approach|approaches|die|dies|fall|falls|are defeated|is defeated|flee|fleeing|surrender|surrenders|are dead|are killed)[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    singularCreature: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an|remaining)\s+/, '').trim();
      if (/ies$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/ies$/, 'y');
      else if (/ves$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/ves$/, 'f');
      else if (/s$/.test(v) && !/ss$/.test(v)) v = v.replace(/s$/, '');
      return WL.U.title(v);
    },
    threatContextHints: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.lower(clause || '');
      var hints = { environment: [], territory: false, ambush: false, bossLike: false };
      if (/forest|woods|marsh|swamp|desert|mountain|cavern|cave|dungeon|ruin|barrow|village|city|road|bridge/.test(c)) hints.environment.push((c.match(/forest|woods|marsh|swamp|desert|mountain|cavern|cave|dungeon|ruin|barrow|village|city|road|bridge/) || [''])[0]);
      if (/lair|den|nest|territory|hunting ground|home ground/.test(c)) hints.territory = true;
      if (/ambush|ambushes|surprise|from the shadows|from the trees|surrounds|encircle/.test(c)) hints.ambush = true;
      if (/boss|alpha|lord|chieftain|captain|warlord|ancient|legendary|named/.test(c)) hints.bossLike = true;
      return hints;
    },
    detectCombatThreatCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:join|joins|arrive|arrives|reinforce|reinforces|enter|enters)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:fight|battle|combat|fray)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var addCount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var addLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (addCount > 0 && addLabel) out.push({ type: 'reinforcement_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(addLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(addLabel), count: addCount, confidence: 'confirmed_group_reinforcement' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:attack|attacks|charge|charges|march|marches|advance|advances|surround|surrounds|appear|appears|approach|approaches)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var count = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var label = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (count > 0 && label) out.push({ type: 'group_count_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(label), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(label), count: count, confidence: 'confirmed_group_count' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:a|an|the)\s+(horde|pack|swarm|mob|band|group|pair)\s+of\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,80}?)\s+(?:attack|attacks|surround|surrounds|appear|appears|emerge|emerges|approach|approaches|descend|descends|prowl|prowls|stalk|stalks)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var groupWord = WL.U.lower(m[1]);
        var packLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        var estimate = /horde|swarm/.test(groupWord) ? 20 : (/pack|band|group/.test(groupWord) ? 6 : (/pair/.test(groupWord) ? 2 : 10));
        if (packLabel) {
          var hints = WL.EventScanner.threatContextHints(c);
          out.push({ type: 'group_count_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(packLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(packLabel), count: estimate, estimated: true, groupWord: groupWord, pressure: hints, confidence: 'estimated_group_pressure' });
          out.push({ type: 'threat_profile_candidate', creatureName: WL.U.title(packLabel), threatId: 'threat_' + WL.U.slug(packLabel), groupWord: groupWord, pressure: hints, confidence: 'group_threat_presence' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:die|dies|are killed|are slain|fall|falls)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var casualties = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var deadLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2]);
        if (casualties > 0 && deadLabel) out.push({ type: 'casualty_candidate', groupName: WL.U.title(deadLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(deadLabel), casualties: casualties, confidence: 'confirmed_group_casualties' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:all|the remaining|the rest of the|the rest of)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:are|is)?\s*(defeated|dead|killed|slain|gone|fleeing|flee|flee\b|surrendering|surrender)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var allLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
        if (allLabel) out.push({ type: 'all_defeated_claim', groupName: WL.U.title(allLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(allLabel), resolution: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: /rest|remaining/.test(WL.U.lower(c)) ? 'confirmed_group_resolution' : 'claimed_group_resolution' });
      }
      if (!m) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?(?:remaining|rest of the|rest of)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(flee|fleeing|surrender|surrendering)\b/i);
        if (m) {
          var fleeingLabel = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
          if (fleeingLabel) out.push({ type: 'all_defeated_claim', groupName: WL.U.title(fleeingLabel), groupId: 'group_' + WL.U.slug(fleeingLabel), resolution: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: 'confirmed_group_resolution' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:a|an|the)\s+((?:(?:young|adult|ancient|elder|legendary|inferno|fire|flame|large|city|village|elite|veteran|captain|true|greater|lesser|normal|alpha|territorial|wounded)\s+)*(?:dragon|demon|wolf|wolves|rat|guard|soldier|goblin|orc|troll|beast|monster|wraith|spirit|giant|drake|wyvern|ogre|troll|slime|undead|construct))\s+(?:attacks|appears|emerges|lunges|blocks|approaches|waits|guards|meets|faces|surrounds|stalks|prowls|circles|flies)\b/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:a\s+level\s+\d+\s+player|level\s+\d+\s+player|you|the player)\s+(?:meets?|faces?|encounters?)\s+(?:an|a|the)?\s*([a-z][a-z -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var creature = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
        if (creature && !/^(?:idea|look|effort|quest|reward)$/i.test(creature)) out.push({ type: 'threat_profile_candidate', creatureName: WL.U.title(creature), threatId: 'threat_' + WL.U.slug(creature), pressure: WL.EventScanner.threatContextHints(c), confidence: 'confirmed_threat_presence' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:defeat|defeated|slay|slain|kill|killed|survive|survived|drive off|drove off)\s+(?:the\s+)?((?:(?:ancient|legendary|inferno|elite|veteran|captain|young|adult)\s+)*(?:dragon|demon|wolf|wolves|rat|guard|soldier|goblin|orc|troll|beast|monster|wraith|spirit|giant|drake|wyvern))\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var defeated = WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1]);
        if (defeated) out.push({ type: 'combat_reward_candidate', creatureName: WL.U.title(defeated), threatId: 'threat_' + WL.U.slug(defeated), outcome: 'defeat_or_survival', confidence: 'confirmed_combat_resolution_evidence' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    cleanAdvancedName: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, '').replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/^(?:merchant|shopkeeper|vendor|trader|seller|blacksmith|innkeeper)\s+(?:says?|said|claims?|tells? you)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i, '');
      raw = raw.replace(/^says?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i, '');
      raw = raw.replace(/\s+(?:from|with|using|by|near|at|in)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:it|this|that|something|anything)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    parseMaterialList: function (text) {
      var out = [];
      var t = WL.U.norm(text || '').replace(/\band\b/gi, ',');
      var re = /(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+([a-z][a-z' -]{2,40})/gi;
      var m;
      while ((m = re.exec(t))) {
        var qty = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]);
        var name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[2]);
        if (qty > 0 && name && !/gold|silver|copper|coin|coins|hours?|days?|weeks?|months?|years?/.test(WL.U.lower(name))) out.push({ name: name, quantity: qty });
      }
      return out;
    },

    cleanContractPartyName: function (value) {
      var n = WL.U.norm(value || '').replace(/^(?:the\s+)/i, '').replace(/\s+(?:agrees?|refuses?|demands?|breaches?|breached|breaks?|abandons?|leaves?|is|was|has|have|will)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      if (!n || /^(?:you|your|player|someone|workers?|crew|they|he|she)$/i.test(n)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(n);
    },
    cleanContractWork: function (value) {
      var n = WL.U.norm(value || '')
        .replace(/^(?:to\s+|on\s+|for\s+|as\s+)/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:for|at)\s+(?:\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(?:copper|silver|gold)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:per|a)\s+(?:day|week|month|shift|job|hour)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/^the\s+/i, '')
        .trim();
      return n || 'unspecified work';
    },
    contractStatusFromText: function (text) {
      var l = WL.U.lower(text || '');
      if (/\b(?:breach|breaches|breached|breaks?\s+the\s+(?:contract|agreement)|abandons?|walks?\s+off|leaves?\s+the\s+job|refuses?\s+to\s+continue)\b/.test(l)) return 'breached';
      if (/\b(?:complete|completed|fulfills?|fulfilled|finished|ends?\s+successfully)\b/.test(l)) return 'completed';
      if (/\b(?:fire|fired|dismiss|dismissed|terminate|terminated|cancel|cancelled)\b/.test(l)) return 'terminated';
      if (/\b(?:promise|promises|promised|offer|offers|offered)\b/.test(l)) return 'promised';
      return 'active';
    },
    contractTermRecord: function (party, work, amount, currency, interval, role, status, text, root) {
      party = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(party) || 'Unknown Worker';
      work = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractWork(work || role || 'unspecified work');
      role = WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(role || '').replace(/^as\s+/i, '')) || WL.EventScanner.inferLaborRole(work);
      var id = 'contract_' + WL.U.slug(party + '_' + work + '_' + (amount || 'terms'));
      return {
        family: 'contract',
        type: 'contract_lifecycle_candidate',
        targetId: id,
        contractId: id,
        partyA: root && root.player && root.player.name && root.player.name !== 'Unknown' ? root.player.name : 'Player',
        partyB: party,
        partyBId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(party),
        work: work,
        role: role,
        payment: amount ? { amount: Number(amount), currency: WL.U.lower(currency || 'silver'), interval: WL.U.lower(interval || 'job') } : null,
        status: status || WL.EventScanner.contractStatusFromText(text),
        deadline: WL.EventScanner.extractDeadline(text),
        phrase: WL.U.norm(text || ''),
        confidence: status === 'promised' ? 'contract_promise_evidence' : 'confirmed_contract_lifecycle'
      };
    },
    inferLaborRole: function (work) {
      var w = WL.U.lower(work || '');
      if (/guard|watch|patrol|protect/.test(w)) return 'Guard';
      if (/repair|build|wall|bridge|roof|road|construction/.test(w)) return 'Builder';
      if (/cook|kitchen|meal/.test(w)) return 'Cook';
      if (/smith|forge|shoe|blade|armor/.test(w)) return 'Smith';
      if (/carry|haul|porter|wagon/.test(w)) return 'Laborer';
      if (/heal|treat|medicine/.test(w)) return 'Healer';
      return 'Worker';
    },
    extractDeadline: function (text) {
      var m = WL.U.norm(text || '').match(/\b(?:by|before|until)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,60}?)(?:[.!?;,]|$)/i);
      return m ? WL.U.norm(m[1]) : '';
    },
    detectContractLaborCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var out = [];
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var m, rec, amount;
      if (!c) return out;
      if (/\b(?:rumor|dream|vision|joke|pretend|pretends|instruction|example|says\s+["'])\b/.test(lower) && !/\b(?:agrees?|signed|hired|employed|paid)\b/.test(lower)) return out;

      // Confirmed hiring/employment: creates a contract and a worker record, but no quest, domain progress, or item.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:hire|hired|employ|employed|contract|contracted)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(?:(?:as|to\s+be)\s+([^,.;]{2,60})\s+)?(?:for|at)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s*(?:\/|per\s+|a\s+)(day|week|month|shift|job|hour)(?:\s+(?:to|for|on)\s+([^.!?;]{3,160}))?/i);
      if (m) {
        amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[3]);
        rec = WL.EventScanner.contractTermRecord(m[1], m[6] || m[2] || 'work', amount, m[4], m[5], m[2] || '', 'active', c, root);
        out.push(rec);
        out.push({ family: 'labor', type: 'labor_employee_candidate', targetId: 'employee_' + WL.U.slug(rec.partyB), actorName: rec.partyB, actorId: rec.partyBId, role: rec.role, wage: rec.payment, contractId: rec.contractId, assignment: rec.work, status: 'employed', confidence: 'confirmed_labor_contract' });
      }

      // Worker agrees to work for wage.
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(?:agrees?|agreed|signs?|signed)\s+(?:to\s+)?(?:work|serve|guard|repair|build|help)(?:\s+(?:on|at|for|with)\s+([^.!?;,]{3,120}?))?\s+(?:for|at)\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s*(?:\/|per\s+|a\s+)(day|week|month|shift|job|hour)/i);
      if (m) {
        amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[3]);
        rec = WL.EventScanner.contractTermRecord(m[1], m[2] || 'work', amount, m[4], m[5], '', 'active', c, root);
        out.push(rec);
        out.push({ family: 'labor', type: 'labor_employee_candidate', targetId: 'employee_' + WL.U.slug(rec.partyB), actorName: rec.partyB, actorId: rec.partyBId, role: rec.role, wage: rec.payment, contractId: rec.contractId, assignment: rec.work, status: 'employed', confidence: 'confirmed_worker_agreement' });
      }

      // Promise/offer without completion: records terms but not payment or active employment unless accepted later.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:promise|promised|offer|offered)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s*(?:\/|per\s+|a\s+)(day|week|month|shift|job|hour)(?:\s+(?:to|for|on)\s+([^.!?;]{3,160}))?/i);
      if (m) {
        amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]);
        out.push(WL.EventScanner.contractTermRecord(m[1], m[5] || 'unspecified work', amount, m[3], m[4], '', 'promised', c, root));
      }

      // Payment evidence links to existing or likely contract; inventory system separately handles actual currency spend if funds exist.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:pay|paid)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+(?:for|as)\s+([^.!?;]{2,120})/i);
      if (m) {
        var payActor = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]);
        var payAmount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]);
        var payCurrency = WL.U.lower(m[3]);
        out.push({ family: 'contract', type: 'contract_payment_candidate', targetId: 'contract_payment_' + WL.U.slug(payActor + '_' + m[4]), actorName: payActor, actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(payActor), amount: payAmount, currency: payCurrency, reason: WL.EventScanner.cleanContractWork(m[4]), status: 'paid_evidence', confidence: 'confirmed_contract_payment_evidence' });
        out.push({ family: 'inventory', type: 'payment_candidate', targetId: 'payment_' + WL.U.slug(payActor + '_' + payAmount + '_' + payCurrency), amount: payAmount, currency: payCurrency, payer: 'player', payee: payActor, direction: 'spend', confidence: 'confirmed_contract_wage_payment_spend' });
      }

      // Breach/completion/termination/refusal.
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(?:breaches|breached|breaks?\s+the\s+(?:contract|agreement)|abandons?|leaves?\s+the\s+job|refuses?\s+to\s+continue)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ family: 'contract', type: 'contract_status_candidate', targetId: 'contract_status_' + WL.U.slug(m[1] + '_breached'), actorName: WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]), actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), status: 'breached', reason: 'breach_or_abandonment', confidence: 'confirmed_contract_breach' });
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+(?:completes|completed|fulfills|fulfilled|finishes|finished)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:contract|job|work)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ family: 'contract', type: 'contract_status_candidate', targetId: 'contract_status_' + WL.U.slug(m[1] + '_completed'), actorName: WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]), actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), status: 'completed', reason: 'completed_work', confidence: 'confirmed_contract_completion' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:fire|fired|dismiss|dismissed|terminate|terminated|cancel|cancelled)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ family: 'contract', type: 'contract_status_candidate', targetId: 'contract_status_' + WL.U.slug(m[1] + '_terminated'), actorName: WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]), actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), status: 'terminated', reason: 'dismissed_or_cancelled', confidence: 'confirmed_contract_termination' });
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70})\s+refuses?\s+(?:unpaid\s+)?(?:work|the\s+job|to\s+work)(?:\s+without\s+pay)?\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ family: 'labor', type: 'labor_refusal_candidate', targetId: 'labor_refusal_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), actorName: WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]), actorId: 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(m[1]), reason: /without\s+pay|unpaid/i.test(c) ? 'refuses_unpaid_work' : 'refuses_work', confidence: 'confirmed_labor_refusal' });

      return out;
    },
    cleanEconomySubject: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(value || '');
      raw = raw.replace(/\b(?:is|are|was|were|will be|costs?|priced|worth|available|for sale)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').trim();
      raw = raw.replace(/^(?:buy|bought|purchase|purchased|pay|paid|try to buy|tried to buy)\s+/i, '').trim();
      if (!raw || /^(?:it|this|that|something|anything|service|work|job|room|meal)$/i.test(raw)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    economyRecordId: function (kind, parts) {
      return 'econ_' + WL.U.slug(kind + '_' + WL.U.norm((parts || []).join('_'))).slice(0, 96);
    },
    detectEconomyTransactionCandidates: function (clause, root) {
      var out = [];
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause || '');
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var m, amount, currency, itemName, serviceName, actorName;
      if (!c) return out;
      if (/\b(?:rumor|dream|vision|joke|pretend|instruction|example|quoted|quote)\b/.test(lower) && !/\b(?:charges?|costs?|price|owe|debt|paid|pay|cannot afford|can't afford|refuses unpaid)\b/.test(lower)) return out;

      // Item price quote: record shop/economy evidence only; never grant the item.
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}?)\s+(?:costs?|is priced at|is worth|sells? for|is available for)\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanEconomySubject(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]);
        if (itemName && amount > 0) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_price_quote_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('quote', [itemName, amount, currency]), itemName: itemName, amount: amount, currency: currency, quoteKind: 'item', vendorName: '', confidence: 'confirmed_item_price_quote' });
      }

      // Vendor says/quotes a price.
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|merchant|shopkeeper|vendor|trader|seller|blacksmith|innkeeper|healer|ferryman)\s+(?:says?|quotes?|asks?|charges?)\s+(?:(?:you\s+)?(?:that\s+)?)?(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}?)\s+(?:costs?|is|for)?\s*(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        actorName = /^(?:merchant|shopkeeper|vendor|trader|seller|blacksmith|innkeeper|healer|ferryman)$/i.test(m[1]) ? WL.U.title(m[1]) : WL.U.title(m[1]);
        itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanEconomySubject(m[2]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[3]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[4]);
        if (itemName && amount > 0) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_price_quote_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('quote', [actorName, itemName, amount, currency]), itemName: itemName, amount: amount, currency: currency, quoteKind: 'item', vendorName: actorName, confidence: 'confirmed_vendor_price_quote' });
      }

      // Service quote: room, meal, healing, ferry, repair, guide, appraisal, messenger, stabling.
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|innkeeper|healer|ferryman|guide|smith|messenger|stablemaster)\s+(?:charges?|asks?|quotes?|demands?)\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+(?:for|to)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
      if (m) {
        actorName = WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); serviceName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractWork(m[4]);
        if (amount > 0 && serviceName) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_service_quote_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('service_quote', [actorName, serviceName, amount, currency]), serviceName: serviceName, actorName: actorName, amount: amount, currency: currency, status: 'quoted', confidence: 'confirmed_service_quote' });
      }

      // Failed purchase / affordability denial. This never creates the item and does not spend currency.
      m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:try|tried|attempt|attempted)\s+to\s+(?:buy|purchase)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)(?:\s+for\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold))?\s*(?:,\s*)?(?:but|and)\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:cannot|can't|could not|couldn't)\s+afford\b/i);
      if (m) {
        itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanEconomySubject(m[1]); amount = m[2] ? WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]) : 0; currency = WL.U.lower(m[3] || '');
        if (itemName) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_failed_purchase_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('failed_purchase', [itemName, amount, currency || 'unknown']), itemName: itemName, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: 'insufficient_funds_or_refused_purchase', confidence: 'confirmed_failed_purchase' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:try|tried|attempt|attempted)\s+to\s+(?:buy|purchase)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}?)(?:\s+for\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold))?\s*(?:,|but|and)?\s*(?:you\s+)?(?:cannot|can't|could not|couldn't|fail|failed)\s+(?:afford|buy|purchase|pay)/i);
      if (m) {
        itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanEconomySubject(m[1]); amount = m[2] ? WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]) : 0; currency = WL.U.lower(m[3] || '');
        if (itemName) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_failed_purchase_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('failed_purchase', [itemName, amount, currency || 'unknown']), itemName: itemName, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: 'insufficient_funds_or_refused_purchase', confidence: 'confirmed_failed_purchase' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:cannot|can't|could not|couldn't)\s+afford\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90})/i);
      if (m) {
        itemName = WL.EventScanner.cleanEconomySubject(m[1]);
        if (itemName) out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_failed_purchase_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('failed_purchase', [itemName, 'unpriced']), itemName: itemName, amount: 0, currency: '', reason: 'cannot_afford', confidence: 'confirmed_affordability_denial' });
      }

      // Debt and wage debt. Records obligation; does not spend/pay by itself.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+owe\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+to\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80}?)(?:\s+(?:for|in)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,100}))?(?:[.!?;,]|$)/i);
      if (m) {
        amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[2]); actorName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[3]) || WL.U.title(m[3]); serviceName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractWork(m[4] || 'debt');
        out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_debt_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('debt', [actorName, amount, currency, serviceName]), creditor: actorName, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: serviceName, debtKind: /wage|salary|pay/i.test(serviceName) ? 'wage' : 'general', status: 'owed', confidence: 'confirmed_debt' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80})\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:owed|due)\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+(?:in\s+)?(?:wages?|pay|salary)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        actorName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]) || WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]);
        out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_debt_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('wage_debt', [actorName, amount, currency]), creditor: actorName, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: 'wages', debtKind: 'wage', status: 'owed', confidence: 'confirmed_wage_debt' });
      }

      // Wage/service payment evidence. Inventory payment parser may separately spend currency if the story confirms payment and funds exist.
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:pay|paid)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80})\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+(?:in\s+)?(?:wages?|pay|salary)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        actorName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]) || WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]);
        out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_wage_payment_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('wage_payment', [actorName, amount, currency]), actorName: actorName, amount: amount, currency: currency, paymentKind: 'wage', confidence: 'confirmed_wage_payment' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:pay|paid)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,40})\s+(\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty)\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+(?:for|to)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,100})/i);
      if (m && !/wages?|salary/i.test(c)) {
        actorName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractPartyName(m[1]) || WL.U.title(m[1]); amount = WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[2]); currency = WL.U.lower(m[3]); serviceName = WL.EventScanner.cleanContractWork(m[4]);
        out.push({ family: 'economy', type: 'economy_service_payment_candidate', targetId: WL.EventScanner.economyRecordId('service_payment', [actorName, serviceName, amount, currency]), actorName: actorName, serviceName: serviceName, amount: amount, currency: currency, status: 'paid_evidence', confidence: 'confirmed_service_payment' });
      }
      return out;
    },
    detectAdvancedBoundaryCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)\s+costs?\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold)\b/i);
      if (m) { /* 9.6B2: economy price quotes are owned by EconomyTransactionSystem. */ }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+owe\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold)\s+to\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,80})\b/i);
      if (m) { /* 9.6B2: debt records are owned by EconomyTransactionSystem. */ }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:learn|learned|discover|discovered)\s+(?:the\s+)?recipe\s+for\s+([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'crafting_recipe_candidate', recipeName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), recipeId: 'recipe_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1])), confidence: 'confirmed_recipe_knowledge' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:craft|crafted|begin crafting|start crafting|work on|assemble|prepare)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:using|with|from)\s+([^.!?;]{3,160}))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m && !/complete|completed|finished|creates?|created/i.test(c)) {
        var craftName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (craftName) out.push({ type: 'crafting_material_candidate', projectName: craftName, projectId: 'craft_' + WL.U.slug(craftName), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[2] || ''), status: 'in_progress_evidence_only', confidence: 'crafting_material_accounting_boundary' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:after\s+)?(?:consuming|using)\s+([^.!?;]{3,160})\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:complete|finish|finished|complete crafting|finish crafting)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var completedName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[2]);
        if (completedName) out.push({ type: 'crafting_completion_candidate', projectName: completedName, projectId: 'craft_' + WL.U.slug(completedName), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[1] || ''), status: 'completion_evidence_no_item_grant', confidence: 'crafting_completion_boundary' });
      }
      function domainProjectId(name) { return 'project_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(name)); }
      function parseDomainTime(text) {
        var t = WL.U.norm(text || '');
        var tm = t.match(/(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve)\s+(?:more\s+)?(hours?|days?|weeks?)/i);
        if (!tm) return null;
        return { amount: WL.EventScanner.numberWord(tm[1]), unit: WL.U.lower(tm[2]).replace(/s$/, '') };
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:construction begins on|work begins on|project begins on|construction starts on|repairs begin on)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:using|with|from)\s+([^.!?;]{3,160}))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var dpn = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (dpn) out.push({ type: 'domain_project_candidate', projectName: dpn, projectId: domainProjectId(dpn), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[2] || ''), time: parseDomainTime(m[2] || ''), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_project_start' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)\s+(?:requires?|needs?|will require|will need)\s+([^.!?;]{3,180})/i);
      if (m && /stone|timber|wood|iron|days?|weeks?|workers?|crew|gold|silver|copper|materials?|supplies?/i.test(m[2])) {
        var reqName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (reqName && /wall|gate|bridge|road|tower|well|house|farm|mine|project|construction|repair|domain|settlement|hall|barracks|workshop/i.test(reqName + ' ' + m[2])) out.push({ type: 'domain_requirement_candidate', projectName: reqName, projectId: domainProjectId(reqName), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[2] || ''), time: parseDomainTime(m[2] || ''), rawRequirements: WL.U.norm(m[2] || ''), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_project_requirements' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:workers?|builders?|the crew|crew)\s+(?:make|makes|made|finish|finishes|finished|repair|repairs|repaired|advance|advances|advanced)\s+(\d{1,3})\s*%\s+(?:progress\s+)?(?:on|of|toward)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:after|by|while)\s+([^.!?;]{3,180}))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var progName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[2]);
        if (progName) out.push({ type: 'domain_progress_candidate', projectName: progName, projectId: domainProjectId(progName), progressPercent: WL.U.clamp(Number(m[1]), 0, 100), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[3] || ''), time: parseDomainTime(m[3] || ''), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_project_progress' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:workers?|builders?|the crew|crew)\s+(?:complete|completes|completed|finish|finishes|finished)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:after|by|using|with)\s+([^.!?;]{3,180}))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var doneName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (doneName) out.push({ type: 'domain_completion_candidate', projectName: doneName, projectId: domainProjectId(doneName), materials: WL.EventScanner.parseMaterialList(m[2] || ''), time: parseDomainTime(m[2] || ''), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_completion_evidence_pending_asset' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)\s+(?:stalls?|is stalled|is blocked|cannot continue|runs out of)\s*(?:because|due to|from|for)?\s*([^.!?;]{0,160})/i);
      if (m && /wall|gate|bridge|road|tower|well|house|farm|mine|project|construction|repair|domain|settlement|materials|workers|timber|stone/i.test(m[1] + ' ' + m[2])) {
        var blockName = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (blockName) out.push({ type: 'domain_blocked_candidate', projectName: blockName, projectId: domainProjectId(blockName), reason: WL.U.norm(m[2] || 'unspecified blockage'), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_project_blocked' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+(?:is assigned to|joins|leads|supervises|works on)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m && /wall|project|construction|repair|gate|bridge|mine|farm|domain|settlement|road|tower|well|hall|barracks|workshop/i.test(m[2])) out.push({ type: 'domain_assignment_candidate', actorName: WL.EventScanner.cleanActorName(m[1]), projectName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[2]), projectId: domainProjectId(m[2]), confidence: 'confirmed_domain_assignment' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:an\s+army\s+of\s+)?(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:march|marches|advance|advances|hold|holds|guard|guards)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'warfare_force_candidate', count: WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]), forceName: WL.U.title(WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2])), forceId: 'force_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2])), confidence: 'confirmed_warfare_force' });
      m = c.match(/\b(\d+|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+([a-z][a-z -]{2,50}?)\s+(?:die|are killed|fall|are lost)\s+(?:in|during|to|from)?\s*([^.!?;]{0,80})/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'warfare_casualty_candidate', casualties: WL.EventScanner.numberWord(m[1]), forceName: WL.U.title(WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2])), forceId: 'force_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[2])), reason: WL.U.norm(m[3] || ''), confidence: 'confirmed_warfare_casualties' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?)\s+(?:take|takes|capture|captures|seize|seizes|hold|holds)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m && /soldiers|army|guard|guards|militia|forces|company|legion|raiders|bandits/i.test(m[1])) out.push({ type: 'warfare_control_candidate', forceName: WL.U.title(WL.EventScanner.cleanCreatureLabel(m[1])), placeName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[2]), confidence: 'warfare_control_claim_evidence_only' });
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+absorb\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'absorption_candidate', sourceName: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), candidateId: 'absorb_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1])), progress: /again|repeated|second|another|more/.test(WL.U.lower(c)) ? 2 : 1, confidence: 'bounded_absorption_evidence' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:after\s+)?(?:repeated|several|many)\s+([^.!?;]{2,80}?)\s+(?:exposures?|absorptions?|essence)\s+([^.!?;]{0,120})/i);
      if (m) {
        var src = WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]);
        if (src) out.push({ type: 'absorption_candidate', sourceName: src, candidateId: 'absorb_' + WL.U.slug(src), progress: 3, thresholdNote: WL.U.norm(m[2] || ''), confidence: 'repeated_absorption_threshold_evidence' });
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+evolve\s+into\s+(?:an?\s+|the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'evolution_candidate', targetForm: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), candidateId: 'evolve_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1])), progress: 1, confidence: 'bounded_evolution_evidence' });
      m = c.match(/\b(?:your\s+)?(?:evolution|metamorphosis|adaptation)\s+(?:toward|into)\s+(?:an?\s+|the\s+)?([A-Za-z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})\s+(?:progresses|deepens|advances|stirs)\b/i);
      if (m) out.push({ type: 'evolution_candidate', targetForm: WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1]), candidateId: 'evolve_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(m[1])), progress: 2, confidence: 'evolution_threshold_progress_evidence' });
      return out;
    },
    detectFormBodyCandidates: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m;
      function cleanForm(v) { return WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(v).replace(/^(?:a|an|the)\s+/i, '').replace(/\s+(?:form|body)$/i, '')); }
      function injurySeverity() {
        if (/\b(?:severe|terrible|deep|gaping|crippling|crushed|shattered|broken bone|cracked ribs?|fractured ribs?|broken ribs?|spurts?|gushes?|badly)\b/.test(lower)) return 'severe';
        if (/\b(?:blood|bleeding|burns?|poison|venom|limps?|sprain|deep)\b/.test(lower)) return 'moderate';
        return 'minor';
      }
      function damageFor(sev) { return sev === 'severe' ? 16 : (sev === 'moderate' ? 8 : 4); }
      function isMetaphoricalDamageText(text) { return /\b(?:cuts?|slashes?)\s+through\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:air|noise|street noise|silence|crowd|conversation|attention|sound|whistle|voices?)\b/i.test(text); }
      function isNonPhysicalLocation(loc) { return /^(?:through\s+)?(?:the\s+)?(?:air|noise|street noise|silence|crowd|conversation|attention|sound|whistle|voice|words?)\b/i.test(WL.U.lower(loc || '')); }
      function exertionConditionName() { if (/\b(?:exhausted|exhaustion|collapse from exhaustion)\b/.test(lower)) return 'exhausted'; if (/\b(?:winded|panting|ragged gasps?|breath(?:ing)?\s+(?:quickens|comes)|out of breath)\b/.test(lower)) return 'winded'; return 'fatigued'; }
      function exertionSeverity() { if (/\b(?:dangerously low|collapse|collapsed|exhausted|all day|seven|eight|nine|ten|twelve|hours?)\b/.test(lower)) return 'moderate'; return 'minor'; }
      function exertionEpDamage() { if (/\b(?:burned? through a good chunk|good chunk|energy is low|energy is flagging|exhausted|all day)\b/.test(lower)) return 18; if (/\b(?:winded|panting|ragged gasps?|tired|fatigued|sweating|muscles? burn|legs? ache|calves? ache|thighs? ache)\b/.test(lower)) return 10; return 6; }
      if (/\b(dream|vision|nightmare)\b/.test(lower)) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into|looks? like)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
        if (m) out.push({ type: /\bvision\b/.test(lower) ? 'vision_form_candidate' : 'dream_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'non_canon_dream_or_vision' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:disguise(?:s)? yourself as|disguised as|pretend(?:s)? to be)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'disguise_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_disguise_only' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:illusion|glamor|glamour)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:look(?:s)? like|appear(?:s)? as|seem(?:s)? to be)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'illusion_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_illusion_only' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:temporarily|for now|for a moment|briefly)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'temporary_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_temporary_form' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:permanently|forever|irreversibly)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:becomes?|turns? into|transforms? into|changes? into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'permanent_form_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_permanent_form' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:your\s+)?(?:race|species)\s+(?:changes?|shifts?)\s+(?:to|into)\s+(?:a\s+|an\s+|the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) { out.push({ type: 'race_shift_candidate', targetForm: cleanForm(m[1]), confidence: 'confirmed_race_shift' }); return out; }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:grow|sprout|tear|burst|emerge|form)\b|\b(?:grow|sprout|tear|burst|emerge|form)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        var traitMatch = c.match(/\b(wings?|horns?|tail|claws?|fangs?|gills?)\b/i);
        out.push({ type: 'body_trait_candidate', trait: traitMatch ? cleanForm(traitMatch[1]) : 'Body Mutation', confidence: 'confirmed_body_trait' });
      }
      if (/\b(?:you\s+are|you're|you\s+lie)\s+(?:dead|dying)\b|\byou\s+(?:die|died)\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: 'dead', severity: 'fatal', hpDamage: 999999, duration: 'permanent_until_revival_or_retcon', confidence: 'confirmed_death_boundary' });
        return out;
      }
      if (/\b(?:cracks?|fractures?|breaks?)\s+(?:your\s+)?ribs?\b|\byour\s+ribs?\s+(?:crack|cracks|fracture|fractures|break|breaks|are\s+(?:cracked|fractured|broken))\b/i.test(c)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_injury_candidate', injury: 'Cracked Ribs', location: 'Ribs', severity: 'severe', hpDamage: 12, confidence: 'confirmed_rib_injury' });
        return out;
      }
      var oldWound = c.match(/\b(?:old\s+)?(?:wound|injury)\s+in\s+your\s+(calves?|calf|leg|arm|shoulder|side|ribs?)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:throb|throbs|ache|aches|hurt|hurts|pain|aggravat|burns?)\b/i);
      if (oldWound) {
        var oldLoc = cleanForm(oldWound[1]);
        out.push({ type: 'body_injury_candidate', injury: 'Old ' + oldLoc + ' Wound', location: oldLoc, severity: 'minor', hpDamage: 0, confidence: 'existing_injury_aggravation_no_new_hp_damage' });
      }
      if (!isMetaphoricalDamageText(c)) {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:cuts?|slashes?|stabs?|bites?|burns?|poisons?|breaks?|crushes?|shatters?|cracks?|fractures?)\s+(?:your\s+)?([a-z][a-z -]{1,40})\b|\byour\s+([a-z][a-z -]{1,40})\s+(?:is|are)\s+(cut|slashed|stabbed|bitten|burned|poisoned|broken|crushed|shattered|cracked|fractured)\b/i);
      } else m = null;
      if (m) {
        var rawLoc = WL.U.norm(m[1] || m[2] || 'body').replace(/\s+and\b[\s\S]*$/i, '').replace(/\s+(?:leaves?|leaving|sending|causing|forcing)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
        var loc = cleanForm(rawLoc);
        var sev = injurySeverity();
        if (isNonPhysicalLocation(rawLoc)) {
          // Live 9.3C: metaphorical cut/slash targets are not body injuries.
        } else if (/poison|venom|spider/.test(lower)) out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: 'poisoned', severity: sev === 'minor' ? 'moderate' : sev, hpDamage: 4, duration: 'until_treated_or_recovered', confidence: 'confirmed_poison_condition' });
        else out.push({ type: 'body_injury_candidate', injury: loc + ' wound', location: loc, severity: sev, hpDamage: damageFor(sev), confidence: 'confirmed_body_injury' });
      } else if ((/\bpoison\b|\bvenom\b/.test(lower) && /\b(?:burns?|courses?|spreads?|in your veins|bites?)\b/.test(lower)) || /\bspider\s+bites?\s+you\b[\s\S]{0,80}\bpoison\b/i.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: 'poisoned', severity: 'moderate', hpDamage: 4, duration: 'until_treated_or_recovered', confidence: 'confirmed_poison_condition' });
      }
      if (/\b(?:jump|jumps|jumping|run|running|sprint|sprinting|climb|climbing|train|training|practice|march|marching|swim|swimming|lift|lifting|work|labor|haul|dig|carry|docks?)\b[\s\S]{0,140}\b(?:tired|winded|exhausted|fatigued|fatigue|sweat|sweating|pant|panting|breath|ragged gasps?|energy|flagging|burn(?:ed)? through|muscles? burn|legs? ache|calves? ache|thighs? ache)\b/i.test(lower) || /\b(?:energy\s+(?:is\s+)?(?:low|flagging)|burned? through a good chunk|winded|panting|ragged gasps?|out of breath)\b/i.test(lower) || /\b(?:hours?|day|shift)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:work|labor|haul|dig|march|train|practice|carry|lifting|docks?)\b|\b(?:work|labor|haul|dig|march|train|practice|carry|lifting)\b[\s\S]{0,80}\b(?:hours?|shift|until exhausted|tired|fatigue)\b/i.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ type: 'body_condition_candidate', condition: exertionConditionName(), severity: exertionSeverity(), epDamage: exertionEpDamage(), hpDamage: 0, duration: 'until_rest', confidence: 'confirmed_exertion_ep_fatigue_no_hp_damage' });
      }
      return out;
    },

    cleanAbilityName: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.norm(name)
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:same\s+practiced|practiced)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:as\s+you\s+level\s+up|through\s+training|from\s+practice)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:at|toward|against|into|onto|on)\s+(?:the\s+)?[a-z][\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+until\s+[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:again|carefully|successfully|hard|under pressure)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\b(?:spell|technique|skill|ability|passive|talent)$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (!n || n.length < 2) return '';
      if (/^(?:sword|dagger|coin|key|ring|lantern|map|potion|quest|task|mission)$/i.test(n)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(n);
    },
    detectAbilityProgressionCandidates: function (clause, hookKind) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var out = [];
      var m, name;
      if (/\b(?:only read the title|learn nothing|no usable technique remains|do not know that technique|don't know that technique|pretend to use|joking|as a joke|manual says|scroll title says)\b/i.test(lower)) return out;
      if (hookKind === 'output') {
        var explicitCost = WL.AbilityCostSystem.parseExplicitCostClause(c);
        if (explicitCost && explicitCost.abilityName) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'cost_observation_candidate', abilityName: explicitCost.abilityName, targetName: explicitCost.abilityName, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(explicitCost.abilityName), observedCost: explicitCost, confidence: 'explicit_story_cost_observation' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:gain|gained|learn|learned|unlock|unlocked|acquire|acquired)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:skill|technique|ability)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80})(?:\s+as\s+you\s+level\s+up|\s+through\s+training|\s+from\s+practice)?/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'skill_gain_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), category: 'skill', confidence: 'explicit_story_skill_gain' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'skill', amount: 5, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'explicit_skill_gain', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:set|add|update)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+keys?\s*[:=]\s*([^.!?]{1,180})/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\b([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+keys?\s+(?:are|as)\s+([^.!?]{1,180})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        var keys = WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.cleanKeys ? WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.cleanKeys(m[2]) : String(m[2] || '').split(/[,;|]/);
        if (name && keys.length) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'keys_update_request', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), keys: keys, confidence: hookKind === 'input' ? 'player_management_request' : 'story_keys_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      if (hookKind === 'output') {
        m = c.match(/\b(?:overcharge|overcharges)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:until|beyond|past|with|through|so)\b|[.!?;,]|$)/i);
        if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:push|pushes|force|forces)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:beyond|past|over|through)\s+(?:its\s+|your\s+)?(?:limit|limits|maximum|safe limit)/i);
        if (m) {
          name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
          if (name) {
            var hpRisk = /blood|life force|lifeforce|sacrifice|vein|heart|flesh|body burns|burns inside|burning inside/.test(lower) ? 4 : 0;
            var mpRisk = /mana|mind|focus|headache|arcane|spell|magic/.test(lower) ? 3 : 1;
            var epRisk = /strain|fatigue|muscle|breath|exhaust|body/.test(lower) ? 3 : 1;
            out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'cost_pressure_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), riskWords: c, hpRisk: hpRisk, mpRisk: mpRisk, epRisk: epRisk, confidence: 'observed_overcharge_cost_pressure' });
          }
        }
      }
      if (hookKind === 'output') {
        m = c.match(/\b([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?(?:Ward|Step|Stance|Guard|Form|Style|Palm|Fang|Slash|Veil|Shield|Technique|Art))\s+(?:forms?|flares?|activates?|settles?|wraps?|surges?|ignites?|holds?)\b[\s\S]{0,120}\b(?:again|same|practiced|practice|repeated|familiar)\b/i);
        if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:you\s+)?(?:settle|settles|drop|drops|shift|shifts)\s+into\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,80}?(?:Stance|Guard|Form|Style|Art))\b/i);
        if (!m) m = c.match(/\b(?:the\s+)?same\s+practiced\s+([A-Z]?[A-Za-z' -]{2,60}?\s*(?:ward|stance|guard|technique|form|style))\b/i);
        if (m) {
          name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
          if (name) {
            out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'ability_evidence_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), compact: true, confidence: 'practiced_repeated_ability_evidence' });
            out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 1, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'practiced_ability_use_compact', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
          }
          return out;
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:make|set|classify|treat)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)\s+(?:(?:as|into|to be)\s+)?(?:a\s+|an\s+)?(skill|passive|talent|ability)\b/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'category_transfer_request', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), category: WL.U.lower(m[2]), confidence: hookKind === 'input' ? 'player_management_request' : 'story_category_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:try|tries|attempt|attempts)\s+to\s+(?:cast|use|perform|activate|shape)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s*,?\s*(?:but|and)\s+|\s+but\b|\s+fails?\b|\s+fizzles?\b|\s+sputters?\b)/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'failed_attempt_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'failed_attempt_evidence' });
        return out;
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:successfully\s+)?(?:cast|casts|perform|performs|activate|activates)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'ability_evidence_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'successful_use_evidence' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 5, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'successful_ability_use', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:practice|practices|train|trains|trained)\s+(?:your\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) {
          out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'training_evidence_candidate', abilityName: name, targetName: name, targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId(name), confidence: 'training_evidence' });
          out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 3, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: WL.EventScanner.statXpForAbility(name), reason: 'training_or_practice', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
        }
      }
      m = c.match(/\b(?:study|studies|studied)\s+([^.!?;,]{2,80})/i);
      if (m) {
        name = WL.EventScanner.cleanAbilityName(m[1]);
        if (name) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 2, domains: WL.AbilityCostSystem.classifyDomain(name), stats: { int: 2 }, reason: 'study_or_theory', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      }
      if (/\binspect(?:s|ed)?\b/.test(lower)) {
        out.push({ family: 'ability', type: 'ability_evidence_candidate', abilityName: 'Inspection', targetName: 'Inspection', targetId: WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.abilityId('Inspection'), confidence: 'practical_ability_use' });
        out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'ability', amount: 2, domains: ['investigation'], stats: { int: 1, lck: 1 }, reason: 'inspection_use', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      }
      if (/\b(sprint|dash|dodge|evade|outrun)\b/.test(lower)) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'stat', amount: 2, domains: ['mobility'], stats: { spd: 3 }, reason: 'mobility_pressure', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      if (/\b(block|endure|withstand|brace)\b/.test(lower)) out.push({ family: 'progression', type: 'xp_candidate', lane: 'stat', amount: 2, domains: ['defense'], stats: { def: 3 }, reason: 'defensive_pressure', confidence: 'confirmed_output_growth' });
      return out;
    },
    statXpForAbility: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name);
      if (/fire|spell|magic|rune|inspect|study/.test(n)) return { int: 2 };
      if (/sword|slash|blade/.test(n)) return { atk: 2, spd: 1 };
      if (/box|punch|fist/.test(n)) return { atk: 1, def: 1 };
      if (/regen|heal/.test(n)) return { def: 1 };
      return {};
    },
    detectQuestCandidate: function (clause) {
      var c = WL.U.norm(clause);
      var lower = WL.U.lower(c);
      var m, giver, objective, reward;
      // Build 7.4: quest offers may come from named people, organizations, or generic institutions like the guild.
      m = c.match(/^((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+(?:asks?|asked|hires?|hired|tasks?|contracts?|contracted|charges?)\s+you\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220}?)(?:\s+and\s+promises?\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/^((?:The\s+)?[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})\s+offers?\s+(?:you\s+)?(?:a\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220}?)(?:\s+for\s+(\d{1,6})\s+(copper|silver|gold))?[.!?]?$/i);
      if (m) {
        giver = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver(m[1]);
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[2]);
        reward = m[3] ? { amount: Number(m[3]), currency: WL.U.lower(m[4] || 'gold'), payer: giver } : null;
        return { type: 'offer_candidate', giver: giver, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), reward: reward, links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, giver), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_offer' };
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|agree\s+to|agreed\s+to|take|took)\s+((?:[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,70}|the\s+[a-z][a-z' -]{2,50})'?s)?\s*(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})[.!?]?/i);
      if (!m) m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:accept|accepted|agree\s+to|agreed\s+to|take|took)\s+(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission|contract)\s+to\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})/i);
      if (m) {
        if (m.length >= 3) { giver = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestGiver((m[1] || '').replace(/'s$/i, '')); objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[2]); }
        else { giver = ''; objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[1]); }
        return { type: 'accept_candidate', giver: giver, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, giver), confidence: 'confirmed_quest_acceptance' };
      }
      m = c.match(/\byou\s+(?:clear|cleared|finish|finished|complete|completed|resolve|resolved|defeat|defeated|drive off|drove off|repair|repaired|escort|escorted|deliver|delivered|recover|recovered|retrieve|retrieved|bring|brought|find|found|investigate|investigated|protect|protected)\s+([^.!?;]{3,220})/i);
      if (m) {
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(m[1]);
        return { type: 'status_candidate', status: 'ready_to_turn_in', objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, ''), confidence: 'objective_progress_or_completion_language' };
      }
      if (/\b(quest|task|mission|contract)\b/.test(lower) && /\b(complete|completed|finished|failed|abandoned|ready|turned in|turn in)\b/.test(lower)) {
        var status = /fail|abandon/.test(lower) ? 'failed' : (/turn(ed)? in|complete|completed/.test(lower) ? 'completed' : 'ready_to_turn_in');
        objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(c);
        return { type: 'status_candidate', status: status, objective: objective, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), links: WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(objective, ''), confidence: 'quest_status_language' };
      }
      return null;
    },
    cleanQuestGiver: function (value) {
      var raw = WL.U.norm(value).replace(/^(?:the\s+)/i, '');
      if (!raw) return 'Unknown';
      if (/^guild$/i.test(raw)) return 'Guild';
      return WL.U.title(raw);
    },
    extractQuestObjectiveLinks: function (objective, giver) {
      var o = WL.U.norm(objective);
      var links = { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      function add(list, name, kind) {
        name = WL.U.title(WL.U.norm(name).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/i, ''));
        if (!name || name.length < 2) return;
        var id = (kind || 'ref') + '_' + WL.U.slug(name);
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].id === id) return;
        list.push({ id: id, name: name, kind: kind || 'ref' });
      }
      var m;
      m = o.match(/\bescort\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})\s+to\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90})\b/i);
      if (m) { add(links.actors, m[1], 'actor'); add(links.places, m[2], 'place'); }
      m = o.match(/\b(?:meet|find|protect|rescue)\s+([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,60})(?:\s+(?:at|in|near|from)\s+(?:the\s+)?([A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{2,90}))?/i);
      if (m) { add(links.actors, m[1], 'actor'); if (m[2]) add(links.places, m[2], 'place'); }
      m = o.match(/\b(?:repair|rebuild|reinforce|inspect|investigate|protect)\s+(?:the\s+)?([a-z][a-z' -]{2,80}?\b(?:wall|gate|bridge|road|tower|well|house|farm|barrow|tavern|inn|hall|market|prison))\b/i);
      if (m) add(links.places, m[1], 'place');
