      m = o.match(/\b(?:recover|retrieve|find|fetch|bring)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,80}?)(?:\s+(?:from|at|in|inside|near)\s+(?:the\s+)?([^.!?;,]{2,90}))?$/i);
      if (m) {
        var item = WL.U.norm(m[1]).replace(/\s+(?:from|at|in|inside|near)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '');
        if (item && !/^(?:your|his|her|their)\s/i.test(item)) add(links.items, item, 'objective_item');
        if (m[2]) add(links.places, m[2], 'place');
      }
      if (giver && /guild|company|circle|clan|order|synod|dominion|consortium|covenant|faction/i.test(giver)) add(links.factions, giver, 'faction');
      return links;
    },
    cleanQuestObjective: function (objective) {
      var o = WL.U.norm(objective)
        .replace(/\s+and\s+promises?\s+\d{1,6}\s+(?:copper|silver|gold)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+for\s+\d{1,6}\s+(?:copper|silver|gold)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:the\s+)?(?:quest|task|mission)\s+to\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^resolve\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      return o || 'unspecified objective';
    },
    makeQuestTitle: function (objective) {
      var o = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(objective);
      o = o.replace(/^(?:to\s+)?/i, '');
      return WL.U.title(o).replace(/^(?:Resolve\s+)+/i, '') || 'Untitled Quest';
    },
    record: function (root, events) {
      for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) root.diagnostics.parser.push(events[i]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.parser, WL.Constants.maxParserHits);
    }
  };

  WL.ProposalBuilder = {
    fromEvents: function (root, events, source) {
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      for (var i = 0; i < events.length; i++) {
        var ev = events[i];
        var target = ev.data && (ev.data.targetId || ev.data.targetName || ev.data.objective || ev.data.title || ev.data.currency || ev.type) || '';
        var targetSlug = ev.data && ev.data.targetId ? ev.data.targetId : WL.U.slug(target);
        var localKey = [ev.family, ev.type, ev.sourceHash, ev.localClauseHash, targetSlug].join('|');
        if (seen[localKey]) continue;
        seen[localKey] = true;
        var proposal = {
          id: 'proposal_' + WL.U.hash(localKey + '|' + i),
          family: ev.family,
          type: ev.type,
          sourceHook: ev.sourceHook,
          sourceKind: ev.sourceKind,
          sourceHash: ev.sourceHash,
          localClauseHash: ev.localClauseHash,
          targetId: targetSlug,
          targetName: ev.data && ev.data.targetName || ev.data && ev.data.title || '',
          textPreview: ev.textPreview,
          data: ev.data || {},
          status: 'proposed',
          opKey: WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([ev.sourceHook, ev.sourceHash, ev.localClauseHash, ev.family, ev.type, targetSlug || 'none'])
        };
        out.push(proposal);
        root.diagnostics.proposals.push(proposal);
      }
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.proposals, WL.Constants.maxProposals);
      return out;
    }
  };

  WL.CanonValidator = {
    validateAll: function (root, proposals, source) {
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < proposals.length; i++) out.push(WL.CanonValidator.validate(root, proposals[i], source));
      for (var j = 0; j < out.length; j++) root.diagnostics.validations.push(out[j]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.validations, WL.Constants.maxValidations);
      return out;
    },
    validate: function (root, proposal, source) {
      var result = {
        id: 'validation_' + WL.U.hash(proposal.id + '|' + proposal.opKey),
        proposalId: proposal.id,
        proposal: proposal,
        family: proposal.family,
        type: proposal.type,
        accepted: false,
        status: 'REJECTED',
        reason: '',
        reducer: ''
      };
      var isAbilityManagementInput = source && source.kind === 'input' && proposal.family === 'ability' && (proposal.type === 'category_transfer_request' || proposal.type === 'keys_update_request');
      if ((!source || !source.eligibleForCanon) && !isAbilityManagementInput) {
        result.status = source && source.kind === 'input' ? 'PENDING' : 'REJECTED';
        result.reason = source && source.reason || 'source_not_eligible';
        return result;
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'system' && proposal.type === 'output_observed') {
        result.accepted = true;
        result.status = 'ACCEPTED';
        result.reason = 'pipeline_observation_only';
        result.reducer = 'SystemObservationReducer';
        return result;
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'inventory') {
        if (proposal.type === 'acquire_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.targetName) {
          if (WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem && WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.isProtectedObjectiveItem && WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.isProtectedObjectiveItem(root, proposal.data.targetName, proposal.textPreview)) {
            result.status = 'REJECTED'; result.reason = 'quest_objective_item_not_confirmed_possession'; return result;
          }
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_output_acquisition'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
        if ((proposal.type === 'payment_candidate' || proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate') && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0 && proposal.data.currency) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate' ? 'confirmed_purchase' : 'confirmed_output_payment'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
        if ((proposal.type === 'loss_candidate' || proposal.type === 'condition_candidate') && proposal.data && proposal.data.targetName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_inventory_state_change'; result.reducer = 'InventoryEquipmentReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'quest') {
        if (proposal.type === 'offer_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.objective) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_offer'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'accept_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.objective) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_acceptance'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'reward_payment_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_reward_payment_record'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'status_candidate' && proposal.data && (proposal.data.objective || proposal.data.status)) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_quest_progress_or_status'; result.reducer = 'QuestObjectiveRewardReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'form') {
        if (proposal.data && (proposal.data.targetForm || proposal.data.trait || proposal.data.injury || proposal.data.condition)) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_form_or_body_evidence'; result.reducer = 'FormTransformationReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'ability') {
        if (proposal.type === 'category_transfer_request' && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = isAbilityManagementInput ? 'ACCEPTED_MANAGEMENT_INPUT' : 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'validated_ability_category_transfer'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'keys_update_request' && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName && proposal.data.keys && proposal.data.keys.length) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = isAbilityManagementInput ? 'ACCEPTED_MANAGEMENT_INPUT' : 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'validated_player_authored_keys_update'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'skill_gain_candidate' && source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'explicit_story_skill_gain'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'cost_pressure_candidate' && source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_overcharge_cost_pressure'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.type === 'cost_observation_candidate' && source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'explicit_story_cost_observation'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
        if (proposal.data && proposal.data.abilityName) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_ability_evidence'; result.reducer = 'AbilityLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'progression') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.type === 'xp_candidate' && proposal.data && proposal.data.amount > 0) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_output_progression'; result.reducer = 'ProgressionReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'time') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_time_route_deadline_evidence'; result.reducer = 'TimeTravelReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'place') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_world_place_evidence'; result.reducer = 'WorldStructureReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'actor') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_actor_social_evidence'; result.reducer = 'ActorSocialReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'social') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          if (/^presence_/.test(proposal.type) || proposal.type === 'knowledge_fact_candidate' || proposal.type === 'witness_record_candidate' || proposal.type === 'reputation_evidence_candidate' || proposal.type === 'rumor_event_candidate' || proposal.type === 'crime_case_candidate' || proposal.type === 'clue_evidence_candidate' || proposal.type === 'law_record_candidate' || proposal.type === 'law_response_candidate') {
            result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = proposal.type === 'witness_record_candidate' ? 'confirmed_witness_eligibility_evidence' : (proposal.type === 'reputation_evidence_candidate' ? 'confirmed_scoped_reputation_evidence' : (proposal.type === 'rumor_event_candidate' ? 'confirmed_rumor_propagation_evidence' : ((proposal.type === 'law_record_candidate' || proposal.type === 'law_response_candidate') ? 'confirmed_faction_law_response_evidence' : ((proposal.type === 'crime_case_candidate' || proposal.type === 'clue_evidence_candidate') ? 'confirmed_crime_or_clue_foundation_evidence' : 'confirmed_social_presence_or_knowledge_evidence')))); result.reducer = 'SocialConsequenceReducer'; return result;
          }
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'contract') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && (proposal.type === 'contract_lifecycle_candidate' || proposal.type === 'contract_status_candidate' || proposal.type === 'contract_payment_candidate')) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_contract_lifecycle_evidence'; result.reducer = 'ContractLifecycleReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'labor') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && (proposal.type === 'labor_employee_candidate' || proposal.type === 'labor_refusal_candidate' || proposal.type === 'labor_assignment_candidate')) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_labor_evidence'; result.reducer = 'LaborSystemReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'economy') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data && /^economy_/.test(proposal.type)) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_economy_transaction_evidence'; result.reducer = 'EconomyTransactionReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'combat') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_combat_threat_evidence'; result.reducer = 'CombatThreatReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      if (proposal.family === 'advanced') {
        if (source && source.eligibleForCanon && proposal.data) {
          if (/^domain_/.test(proposal.type)) {
            result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_domain_project_evidence'; result.reducer = 'DomainProjectReducer'; return result;
          }
          result.accepted = true; result.status = 'ACCEPTED'; result.reason = 'confirmed_advanced_boundary_evidence'; result.reducer = 'AdvancedBoundaryReducer'; return result;
        }
      }
      result.status = 'PENDING';
      result.reason = 'no_owner_for_family_in_current_build';
      return result;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry = {
    handlers: {},
    applyAcceptedBatch: function (root, validations, source) {
      var results = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < validations.length; i++) {
        var v = validations[i];
        if (!v.accepted) {
          results.push({ status: v.status, applied: false, reason: v.reason, family: v.family, type: v.type, targetId: v.proposal && v.proposal.targetId || '' });
          continue;
        }
        var key = v.proposal.opKey;
        var ledger = WL.OperationLedger.add(root, key, v.reducer || v.family);
        if (!ledger.applied) {
          results.push({ status: 'DUPLICATE_SKIPPED', applied: false, duplicate: true, key: ledger.key, family: v.family, type: v.type });
          continue;
        }
        var handler = WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers[v.reducer];
        if (typeof handler !== 'function') {
          results.push({ status: 'NO_HANDLER', applied: false, key: ledger.key, family: v.family, type: v.type, reason: 'handler_missing' });
          continue;
        }
        var h = handler(root, v, ledger);
        results.push(h);
      }
      for (var j = 0; j < results.length; j++) root.diagnostics.reducers.push(results[j]);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.reducers, WL.Constants.maxReducerResults);
      return results;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.SystemObservationReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return {
      status: 'APPLIED_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY',
      applied: true,
      family: validation.family,
      type: validation.type,
      key: ledger.key,
      note: 'Pipeline observation recorded through the current reducer authority only.'
    };
  };

  WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var proposal = validation.proposal;
      if (proposal.type === 'acquire_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.acquire(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'payment_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.pay(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'purchase_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.purchase(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'loss_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.lose(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'condition_candidate') return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.setCondition(root, proposal, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: validation.family, type: validation.type, reason: 'unsupported_inventory_event' };
    },
    isStackable: function (name, identity) {
      if (WL.StackPolicy && typeof WL.StackPolicy.isStackable === 'function') return WL.StackPolicy.isStackable(identity || { displayName: name, category: '' });
      return /\b(coin|coins|arrow|arrows|bolt|bolts|ration|rations|herb|herbs|potion|potions)\b/i.test(name || '');
    },
    identify: function (root, name, preferredId, proposal, ledger) {
      if (WL.ItemIdentitySystem && typeof WL.ItemIdentitySystem.identify === 'function') return WL.ItemIdentitySystem.identify(root, name, preferredId, proposal, ledger);
      var display = WL.U.title(name || 'Unknown Item');
      return { id: preferredId || ('item_' + WL.U.slug(display)), displayName: display, baseName: display, stackable: WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.isStackable(display), stackKey: WL.U.slug(display), category: 'misc', tags: [] };
    },
    findItemId: function (root, name) {
      var identity = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.identify(root, name, '', null, null);
      var id = identity.id || ('item_' + WL.U.slug(identity.displayName || name || ''));
      if (root.inventory.itemsById[id]) return id;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById || {});
      var slug = WL.U.slug(identity.displayName || name || '');
      var stackKey = identity.stackKey || '';
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.inventory.itemsById[keys[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        if (stackKey && rec.stackKey === stackKey) return keys[i];
        var rslug = WL.U.slug((rec && rec.displayName) || keys[i]);
        if (rslug === slug) return keys[i];
      }
      return id;
    },
    addItemRecord: function (root, id, name, ledger, phrase, sourceHash, proposal) {
      var identity = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.identify(root, name, id, proposal, ledger);
      if (!identity || !identity.displayName || identity.rejected) return null;
      id = identity.id;
      name = identity.displayName;
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) {
        item = {
          id: id,
          displayName: name,
          baseName: identity.baseName || name,
          quantity: 1,
          stackable: !!identity.stackable,
          stackKey: identity.stackKey || WL.U.slug(name),
          category: identity.category || 'misc',
          descriptors: identity.descriptors || [],
          material: identity.material || '',
          quality: identity.quality || '',
          tags: identity.tags || [],
          condition: identity.condition || 'intact',
          custody: 'owned_by_player',
          equippedSlot: '',
          cardEligible: identity.cardEligible !== false,
          sourceHistory: []
        };
        root.inventory.itemsById[id] = item;
        if (root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id);
      } else {
        item.custody = 'owned_by_player';
        item.displayName = item.displayName || name;
        item.baseName = item.baseName || identity.baseName || name;
        item.category = item.category || identity.category || 'misc';
        item.stackKey = item.stackKey || identity.stackKey || WL.U.slug(name);
        item.stackable = !!(item.stackable || identity.stackable);
        if (root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.push(id);
        if (item.stackable) item.quantity = WL.U.clamp(Number(item.quantity || 1) + 1, 1, 999999);
      }
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, sourceHash: sourceHash || '', phrase: phrase || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      return item;
    },
    acquire: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var rawName = proposal.data.targetName || proposal.targetName || 'Unknown Item';
      var identity = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.identify(root, rawName, proposal.data.targetId || '', proposal, ledger);
      if (!identity || identity.rejected || !identity.displayName || /\bquest\b/i.test(identity.displayName) || (WL.GeneratedCardPolicy && WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle && WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle(identity.displayName))) return { status: 'REJECTED_BAD_ITEM_NAME', applied: false, family: 'inventory', type: 'acquire_candidate', key: ledger.key, reason: identity && identity.reason || 'bad_item_identity' };
      var item = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.addItemRecord(root, identity.id, identity.displayName, ledger, proposal.textPreview, proposal.sourceHash, proposal);
      if (!item) return { status: 'REJECTED_BAD_ITEM_NAME', applied: false, family: 'inventory', type: 'acquire_candidate', key: ledger.key };
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'acquire', itemId: item.id, name: item.displayName, quantity: item.quantity || 1, stackable: !!item.stackable, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.touchItem === 'function') WL.WorldGraphIndex.touchItem(root, item, 'owned_by_player');
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'acquire', itemId: item.id, targetName: item.displayName, quantity: item.quantity || 1, key: ledger.key };
    },
    pay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.amount, 1, 999999);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.currency || 'gold');
      var direction = proposal.data.direction || 'gain';
      if (!root.inventory.currency[currency] && root.inventory.currency[currency] !== 0) root.inventory.currency[currency] = 0;
      if (direction === 'spend' || direction === 'loss') root.inventory.currency[currency] = Math.max(0, root.inventory.currency[currency] - amount);
      else root.inventory.currency[currency] += amount;
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: direction === 'spend' ? 'currency_spend' : 'payment', amount: amount, currency: currency, payer: proposal.data.payer || '', key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: direction === 'spend' ? 'currency_spend' : 'payment', amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key };
    },
    purchase: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.amount, 1, 999999);
      var currency = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.currency || 'gold');
      var identity = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.identify(root, proposal.data.itemName || 'Unknown Item', proposal.data.itemId || '', proposal, ledger);
      var name = identity.displayName || 'Unknown Item';
      var id = identity.id || ('item_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      if (!root.inventory.currency[currency] && root.inventory.currency[currency] !== 0) root.inventory.currency[currency] = 0;
      if (root.inventory.currency[currency] < amount) {
        root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases.push({ key: ledger.key, itemName: name, amount: amount, currency: currency, reason: 'insufficient_funds' });
        WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases, 40);
        return { status: 'BLOCKED_INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'purchase', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
      }
      root.inventory.currency[currency] -= amount;
      var item = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.addItemRecord(root, id, name, ledger, proposal.textPreview, proposal.sourceHash, proposal);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'purchase', itemId: item && item.id || id, name: item && item.displayName || name, amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'purchase', itemId: item && item.id || id, amount: amount, currency: currency, key: ledger.key };
    },
    lose: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = (WL.ItemIdentitySystem && WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName) ? WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item') : WL.U.title(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.findItemId(root, name);
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) return { status: 'NO_MATCHING_ITEM', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'loss', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
      if (item.stackable && Number(item.quantity || 1) > 1) item.quantity = Math.max(0, Number(item.quantity || 1) - 1);
      if (!item.stackable || Number(item.quantity || 0) <= 0) {
        var idx = root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf(id);
        if (idx >= 0) root.inventory.ownedIds.splice(idx, 1);
        item.custody = 'lost_or_discarded';
      }
      item.lastLossReason = proposal.data.reason || 'lost';
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, action: 'loss' });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'loss', itemId: id, name: item.displayName, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'loss', itemId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    setCondition: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = (WL.ItemIdentitySystem && WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName) ? WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item') : WL.U.title(proposal.data.targetName || 'Unknown Item');
      var id = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.findItemId(root, name);
      var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
      if (!item) {
        var identity = WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.identify(root, name, id, proposal, ledger);
        item = { id: identity.id || id, displayName: identity.displayName || name, baseName: identity.baseName || name, quantity: 1, stackable: false, stackKey: identity.stackKey || WL.U.slug(name), category: identity.category || 'misc', condition: 'unknown', custody: 'not_confirmed_owned', equippedSlot: '', sourceHistory: [] };
        root.inventory.itemsById[item.id] = item;
        id = item.id;
      }
      item.condition = proposal.data.condition || 'damaged';
      item.stackable = false;
      if (item.tags && item.tags.indexOf('conditioned') < 0) item.tags.push('conditioned');
      item.sourceHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, action: 'condition', condition: item.condition });
      WL.U.capArray(item.sourceHistory, 12);
      root.inventory.log.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, action: 'condition', itemId: id, condition: item.condition, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(root.inventory.log, 100);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'inventory', type: 'condition', itemId: id, condition: item.condition, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < root.inventory.ownedIds.length; i++) {
        var id = root.inventory.ownedIds[i];
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id];
        if (item) lines.push(item.displayName + (item.stackable || (item.quantity || 1) > 1 ? ' x' + (item.quantity || 1) : '') + ' [' + (item.condition || 'unknown') + ']');
      }
      if (!lines.length) lines.push('(none)');
      lines.push('Currency: ' + (root.inventory.currency.gold || 0) + ' gold, ' + (root.inventory.currency.silver || 0) + ' silver, ' + (root.inventory.currency.copper || 0) + ' copper');
      return lines;
    }
  };


  WL.ItemIdentitySystem = {
    abstractWords: /^(?:are empty|empty|pockets? are empty|no armor|no weapon|no coin|no items?|idea|thought|concept|plan|rumor|rumour|promise|quest|task|mission|objective|reward|payment|thanks|gratitude|advice|information|info|news|look|glance|stare|view|breath|moment|time|chance|opportunity|effort|pain|fear|hope|memory|dream|vision|attention|hold|bearings|nap|sleep|rest|energy|stamina|body|muscles?|lungs?|hands?|wrists?|fingers?)$/i,
    itemHeadNouns: /\b(?:sword|blade|dagger|knife|axe|mace|hammer|spear|bow|crossbow|staff|wand|shield|armor|armour|helmet|gauntlets?|boots?|cloak|robe|ring|amulet|key|map|scroll|book|lantern|torch|rope|pickaxe|tools?|bottle|vial|potion|ration|rations|arrow|arrows|bolt|bolts|herb|herbs|ore|ingot|wood|stone|cloth|leather|gem|crystal|coin|coins)\b/i,
    cleanItemName: function (value) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(value || '')
        .replace(/["“”]/g, '')
        .replace(/^\s*(?:you\s+)?(?:pick(?:ed|s)? up|grab(?:s|bed)?|snag(?:s|ged)?|yoink(?:s|ed)?|nab(?:s|bed)?|take(?:s|n)? hold of|take(?:s|n)?|loot(?:s|ed)?|receive(?:s|d)?|pocket(?:s|ed)?|snatch(?:es|ed)? up|scoop(?:s|ed)? up|secure(?:s|d)?|retrieve(?:s|d)?|recover(?:s|ed)?|lift(?:s|ed)?|catch(?:es|ed)?)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^hold\s+of\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^up\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:merchant|shopkeeper|vendor|trader|seller|blacksmith|innkeeper)\s+(?:says?|said|claims?|tells? you)\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i, '')
        .replace(/^says?\s+(?:the\s+|a\s+|an\s+)?/i, '')
        .replace(/^(?:the|a|an|your)\s+/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:by|from)\s+[A-Z][A-Za-z' -]{1,50}\s*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:before|while|as|when|but|however|and then|from|off|out of|into|onto|because|where)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/\s+(?:in|into|onto)\s+your\s+(?:hand|hands|palm|pack|bag|inventory|pocket|pouch)\b[\s\S]*$/i, '')
        .replace(/[.!?;,]+$/g, '')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
      if (!t || t.length < 2) return '';
      if (WL.ItemIdentitySystem.abstractWords.test(t)) return '';
      if (/\b(?:quest|task|mission|objective|reward|promise)\b/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (/^(?:it|them|that|those|something|anything|everything|nothing)$/i.test(t)) return '';
      if (/^(?:\d{1,6}|one|two|three|four|five|six|seven|eight|nine|ten|eleven|twelve|thirteen|fourteen|fifteen|sixteen|seventeen|eighteen|nineteen|twenty|thirty|forty|fifty|sixty|seventy|eighty|ninety)\s+(?:gold|silver|copper)(?:\s+coins?)?$/i.test(t)) return '';
      return WL.U.title(t);
    },
    categoryFor: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name || '');
      if (/\b(sword|blade|dagger|knife|axe|mace|hammer|spear|bow|crossbow|staff|wand)\b/.test(n)) return 'weapon';
      if (/\b(shield|armor|armour|helmet|gauntlets?|boots?|cloak|robe)\b/.test(n)) return 'armor';
      if (/\b(key|map|scroll|book|lantern|torch|rope|tools?|pickaxe)\b/.test(n)) return 'tool';
      if (/\b(potion|bottle|vial|ration|rations|food|bread|water|wine|ale)\b/.test(n)) return 'consumable';
      if (/\b(arrow|arrows|bolt|bolts|shot|ammo|ammunition)\b/.test(n)) return 'ammunition';
      if (/\b(ring|amulet|necklace|bracelet|charm|talisman)\b/.test(n)) return 'accessory';
      if (/\b(herb|herbs|ore|ingot|wood|stone|cloth|leather|hide|gem|crystal|iron|copper|bronze|silver|gold)\b/.test(n)) return 'material';
      return 'misc';
    },
    descriptorsFor: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name || '');
      var desc = [];
      var words = ['rusty','old','ancient','broken','cracked','chipped','polished','beat-up','battered','bronze','iron','steel','silver','gold','wooden','bone','leather','cloth','healing','mana','fire','ice','shadow','blessed','cursed','enchanted','quest','named','unique'];
      for (var i = 0; i < words.length; i++) { var pat = new RegExp('\\b' + words[i].replace('-', '[- ]') + '\\b', 'i'); if (pat.test(name || '')) desc.push(WL.U.title(words[i])); }
      return desc;
    },
    tagsFor: function (name, category) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name || '');
      var tags = [];
      if (/\b(old|ancient|named|unique|heirloom|personal|signature)\b/.test(n)) tags.push('unique_history');
      if (/\b(enchanted|magic|magical|blessed|cursed|divine|infernal|artifact|relic)\b/.test(n)) tags.push('magical');
      if (/\b(quest|contract|sealed|marked)\b/.test(n)) tags.push('quest_or_legal');
      if (/\b(broken|cracked|chipped|rusty|damaged|battered|beat-up)\b/.test(n)) tags.push('conditioned');
      if (category === 'weapon' || category === 'armor' || category === 'accessory') tags.push('equipment_like');
      return tags;
    },
    baseNameFor: function (name) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(name || '');
      var stripped = t.replace(/\b(?:rusty|old|ancient|broken|cracked|chipped|polished|beat-up|battered|bronze|iron|steel|silver|gold|wooden|bone|leather|cloth|healing|mana|fire|ice|shadow|blessed|cursed|enchanted)\b/ig, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
      return WL.U.title(stripped || t);
    },
    materialFor: function (name) {
      var m = WL.U.lower(name || '').match(/\b(bronze|iron|steel|silver|gold|wooden|wood|bone|leather|cloth|stone|copper)\b/);
      return m ? WL.U.title(m[1]) : '';
    },
    conditionFor: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.lower(name || '');
      if (/\b(broken|shattered|snapped)\b/.test(n)) return 'broken';
      if (/\b(cracked|chipped|rusty|damaged|battered|beat-up)\b/.test(n)) return 'worn';
      return 'intact';
    },
    cardEligibleFor: function (identity) {
      if (!identity || !identity.displayName) return false;
      if (identity.stackable && /^(?:material|ammunition|consumable)$/.test(identity.category || '')) return false;
      if (/^(?:misc|material)$/i.test(identity.category || '') && !(identity.tags || []).length) return false;
      return true;
    },
    preferredIdFor: function (root, identity, preferredId, proposal, ledger) {
      var clean = identity.displayName || '';
      var base = 'item_' + WL.U.slug(clean);
      if (preferredId && /^item_/.test(preferredId) && WL.U.slug(clean) === preferredId.replace(/^item_/, '')) base = preferredId;
      if (identity.stackable) return base;
      var existing = root.inventory && root.inventory.itemsById && root.inventory.itemsById[base];
      if (!existing) return base;
      if (existing.stackable) return base;
      var phraseHash = proposal && proposal.localClauseHash || proposal && proposal.sourceHash || ledger && WL.U.hash(ledger.key) || WL.U.hash(clean + '_' + (root.runtime && root.runtime.currentTurn || 0));
      var unique = base + '_' + phraseHash.slice(0, 6);
      var n = 2;
      while (root.inventory.itemsById[unique]) { unique = base + '_' + phraseHash.slice(0, 4) + '_' + (n++); }
      return unique;
    },
    identify: function (root, name, preferredId, proposal, ledger) {
      var display = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cleanItemName(name || '');
      if (!display) return { rejected: true, reason: 'bad_or_abstract_item_name' };
      var category = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.categoryFor(display);
      var descriptors = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.descriptorsFor(display);
      var tags = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.tagsFor(display, category);
      var identity = {
        displayName: display,
        baseName: WL.ItemIdentitySystem.baseNameFor(display),
        category: category,
        descriptors: descriptors,
        material: WL.ItemIdentitySystem.materialFor(display),
        quality: descriptors.length ? descriptors[0] : '',
        condition: WL.ItemIdentitySystem.conditionFor(display),
        tags: tags,
        stackable: false,
        stackKey: '',
        cardEligible: true
      };
      identity.stackable = WL.StackPolicy.isStackable(identity);
      identity.stackKey = WL.StackPolicy.stackKey(identity);
      identity.cardEligible = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.cardEligibleFor(identity);
      identity.id = WL.ItemIdentitySystem.preferredIdFor(root, identity, preferredId, proposal, ledger);
      return identity;
    }
  };

  WL.StackPolicy = {
    stackableCategories: { currency: 1, ammunition: 1, material: 1 },
    stackableNames: /\b(?:arrow|arrows|bolt|bolts|ration|rations|herb|herbs|ore|ingots?|wood|stone|cloth|leather|hide|thread|nails?|coins?|copper|silver|gold)\b/i,
    neverStackCategories: { weapon: 1, armor: 1, accessory: 1, tool: 1 },
    isStackable: function (identity) {
      identity = identity || {};
      var name = identity.displayName || '';
      var category = identity.category || WL.ItemIdentitySystem.categoryFor(name);
      var tags = identity.tags || [];
      if (tags.indexOf('unique_history') >= 0 || tags.indexOf('magical') >= 0 || tags.indexOf('quest_or_legal') >= 0 || tags.indexOf('conditioned') >= 0) return false;
      if (WL.StackPolicy.neverStackCategories[category]) return false;
      if (category === 'consumable') return /\b(?:ration|rations|herb|herbs|plain|basic|minor healing potion|healing potion)\b/i.test(name) && !/\b(?:unique|named|sealed|quest|cursed|blessed)\b/i.test(name);
      if (WL.StackPolicy.stackableCategories[category]) return true;
      return WL.StackPolicy.stackableNames.test(name);
    },
    stackKey: function (identity) {
      identity = identity || {};
      var name = identity.displayName || '';
      var category = identity.category || 'misc';
      if (!WL.StackPolicy.isStackable(identity)) return 'unique:' + WL.U.slug(name);
      return 'stack:' + category + ':' + WL.U.slug(name);
    }
  };


  WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var proposal = validation.proposal;
      if (proposal.type === 'offer_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.offer(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'accept_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.accept(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'reward_payment_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.recordPayment(root, proposal, ledger);
      if (proposal.type === 'status_candidate') return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.status(root, proposal, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: validation.family, type: validation.type, reason: 'unsupported_quest_event' };
    },
    questId: function (giver, objective) {
      return 'quest_' + WL.U.slug((giver || 'unknown') + '_' + (objective || 'objective'));
    },
    findByObjective: function (root, giver, objective) {
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.questId(giver || '', objective || '');
      if (root.quests.questsById[id]) return id;
      var targetSlug = WL.U.slug(objective || '');
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        if (WL.U.slug(q.objective || '') === targetSlug && (!giver || !q.giver || WL.U.slug(q.giver) === WL.U.slug(giver))) return keys[i];
      }
      return '';
    },
    ensureQuest: function (root, data) {
      var giver = WL.U.title(data.giver || 'Unknown');
      var objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(data.objective || data.title || 'unspecified objective');
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.findByObjective(root, giver, objective) || WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.questId(giver, objective);
      var q = root.quests.questsById[id];
      if (!q) {
        q = { id: id, title: WL.EventScanner.makeQuestTitle(objective), giver: giver, objective: objective, status: 'offered', rewardsPromised: [], rewardsPaid: [], history: [], progress: [], objectiveLinks: { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] }, actorRefs: [], placeRefs: [], itemRefs: [], factionRefs: [], relationships: [] };
        root.quests.questsById[id] = q;
      }
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      return q;
    },
    offer: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var q = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuest(root, proposal.data);
      q.status = q.status || 'offered';
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      if (root.quests.offeredIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0 && root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0) root.quests.offeredIds.push(q.id);
      if (proposal.data.reward && proposal.data.reward.amount > 0) {
        var rid = 'reward_' + WL.U.hash(q.id + '|' + proposal.data.reward.amount + '|' + proposal.data.reward.currency);
        var exists = q.rewardsPromised.some(function (r) { return r.id === rid; });
        if (!exists) {
          var rec = { id: rid, questId: q.id, amount: proposal.data.reward.amount, currency: proposal.data.reward.currency, payer: proposal.data.reward.payer || q.giver, paid: false };
          q.rewardsPromised.push(rec);
          root.quests.rewardPromises.push(rec);
        }
      }
      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'offer', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'offer', questId: q.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    accept: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var q = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuest(root, proposal.data);
      q.status = 'active';
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      var oi = root.quests.offeredIds.indexOf(q.id);
      if (oi >= 0) root.quests.offeredIds.splice(oi, 1);
      if (root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(q.id) < 0) root.quests.activeIds.push(q.id);
      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'accept', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'accept', questId: q.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    objectiveScore: function (a, b) {
      var stop = { the:1, a:1, an:1, to:1, from:1, his:1, her:1, their:1, old:1, bren:1, s:1, of:1, in:1, on:1, at:1, and:1, you:1 };
      function toks(v) { return WL.U.lower(v).replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ').split(/\s+/).filter(function (x) { return x && !stop[x]; }); }
      var ta = toks(a), tb = toks(b), score = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < ta.length; i++) if (tb.indexOf(ta[i]) >= 0) score++;
      return score;
    },
    findBestByText: function (root, objective) {
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      var best = '', bestScore = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        var s = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.objectiveScore(q.objective || '', objective || '');
        if (s > bestScore) { best = keys[i]; bestScore = s; }
      }
      return bestScore >= 2 ? best : '';
    },
    status: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var wanted = proposal.data.status || 'ready_to_turn_in';
      var objective = WL.EventScanner.cleanQuestObjective(proposal.data.objective || proposal.textPreview || '');
      var id = WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.findBestByText(root, objective);
      if (!id) return { status: 'NO_MATCHING_QUEST', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'status', key: ledger.key };
      var q = root.quests.questsById[id];
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.linkQuestRelationships(root, q, proposal, ledger);
      q.status = wanted;
      q.progress = q.progress || [];
      q.progress.push({ key: ledger.key, status: wanted, objectiveEvidence: objective, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      q.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'status', status: wanted, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(q.progress, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.history, 30);
      if ((wanted === 'completed' || wanted === 'ready_to_turn_in') && root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(id) >= 0 && wanted === 'completed') root.quests.activeIds.splice(root.quests.activeIds.indexOf(id), 1);
      if (wanted === 'completed' && root.quests.completedIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.quests.completedIds.push(id);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'status', questId: id, questStatus: wanted, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordPayment: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var amount = proposal.data.amount;
      var currency = proposal.data.currency;
      var payerSlug = WL.U.slug(proposal.data.payer || '');
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      var matched = '';
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        for (var j = 0; j < q.rewardsPromised.length; j++) {
          var r = q.rewardsPromised[j];
          if (!r.paid && r.amount === amount && r.currency === currency && (!payerSlug || WL.U.slug(r.payer || q.giver) === payerSlug)) {
            r.paid = true;
            r.paidKey = ledger.key;
            q.rewardsPaid.push(r.id);
            matched = q.id;
            root.quests.rewardPayments.push({ questId: q.id, rewardId: r.id, amount: amount, currency: currency, payer: proposal.data.payer || '', key: ledger.key });
            break;
          }
        }
        if (matched) break;
      }
      return { status: matched ? 'APPLIED' : 'NO_MATCHING_PROMISE_RECORDED', applied: true, family: 'quest', type: 'reward_payment_record', questId: matched, key: ledger.key };
    },
    isProtectedObjectiveItem: function (root, targetName, textPreview) {
      var targetId = 'objective_item_' + WL.U.slug(targetName || '');
      if (!targetName || !targetId) return false;
      var lower = WL.U.lower(textPreview || '');
      // If the prose only says the objective was recovered/retrieved/found/completed, treat it as quest progress.
      // Confirmed possession can still be captured by stronger inventory language such as pocket/secure/take/hold.
      if (!/\b(?:recover|recovered|retrieve|retrieved|find|found|bring|brought|complete|completed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:pocket|pocketed|secure|secured|take|takes|took|pick(?:s|ed)? up|hold|holds|held|place(?:s|d)? in your hand|put(?:s)? into your pack|stow|stowed)\b/.test(lower)) return false;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests && root.quests.questsById || {});
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
        if (q.itemRefs && q.itemRefs.indexOf(targetId) >= 0 && /^(?:offered|active|ready_to_turn_in)$/i.test(q.status || '')) return true;
      }
      return false;
    },
    ensureQuestArrays: function (q) {
      if (!q.objectiveLinks) q.objectiveLinks = { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(q.actorRefs)) q.actorRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.placeRefs)) q.placeRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.itemRefs)) q.itemRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.factionRefs)) q.factionRefs = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.relationships)) q.relationships = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(q.progress)) q.progress = [];
      return q;
    },
    mergeObjectiveLinks: function (q, links) {
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      links = links || { actors: [], places: [], items: [], factions: [] };
      function mergeList(src, dest, objectList) {
        for (var i = 0; i < (src || []).length; i++) {
          var rec = src[i];
          if (!rec || !rec.id) continue;
          if (dest.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) dest.push(rec.id);
          var exists = objectList.some(function (x) { return x.id === rec.id; });
          if (!exists) objectList.push(rec);
        }
      }
      mergeList(links.actors, q.actorRefs, q.objectiveLinks.actors);
      mergeList(links.places, q.placeRefs, q.objectiveLinks.places);
      mergeList(links.items, q.itemRefs, q.objectiveLinks.items);
      mergeList(links.factions, q.factionRefs, q.objectiveLinks.factions);
    },
    linkQuestRelationships: function (root, q, proposal, ledger) {
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.ensureQuestArrays(q);
      WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.mergeObjectiveLinks(q, proposal.data.links || WL.EventScanner.extractQuestObjectiveLinks(q.objective, q.giver));
      function pushRel(list, rec) {
        var key = WL.U.slug([rec.kind, rec.questId, rec.actorId || rec.placeId || rec.itemId || rec.factionId].join('|'));
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) if (list[i].relKey === key) return;
        rec.relKey = key;
        list.push(rec);
      }
      if (q.giver && q.giver !== 'Unknown') {
        var giver = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, q.giver);
        pushRel(giver.relationships, { kind: 'quest_giver', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, actorId: giver.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'giver', actorId: giver.id, actorName: giver.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      for (var a = 0; a < q.objectiveLinks.actors.length; a++) {
        var ar = q.objectiveLinks.actors[a];
        var actor = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, ar.name);
        pushRel(actor.relationships, { kind: 'quest_target_actor', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, actorId: actor.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'objective_actor', actorId: actor.id, actorName: actor.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      for (var p = 0; p < q.objectiveLinks.places.length; p++) {
        var pr = q.objectiveLinks.places[p];
        var place = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, pr.name, /wall|gate|bridge|road|barrow/i.test(pr.name) ? 'place' : 'place');
        pushRel(place.relations, { kind: 'quest_objective', questId: q.id, questTitle: q.title, placeId: place.id, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(root.places.relations, { kind: 'quest_objective', questId: q.id, placeId: place.id, placeName: place.name, key: ledger.key });
        pushRel(q.relationships, { kind: 'objective_place', placeId: place.id, placeName: place.name, questId: q.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      WL.U.capArray(q.actorRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.placeRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.itemRefs, 20); WL.U.capArray(q.relationships, 40);
      return q;
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      var keys = Object.keys(root.quests.questsById);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var q = root.quests.questsById[keys[i]];
        lines.push(q.title + ' [' + q.status + '] — ' + q.objective);
      }
      return lines.length ? lines : ['(none)'];
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.InventoryEquipmentReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.QuestObjectiveRewardReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.FormTransformationReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.FormTransformationSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.AbilityLifecycleReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.AbilityLifecycleSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.ProgressionReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.ProgressionSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };


  WL.TimeTravelSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'elapsed_time_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.advance(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'route_base_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordRouteBase(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'route_delay_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordRouteDelay(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'deadline_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.recordDeadline(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'rest_recovery_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.restRecover(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'next_day_candidate') return WL.TimeTravelSystem.nextDay(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'time', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_time_event' };
    },
    advanceClock: function (root, minutes) {
      minutes = WL.U.clamp(minutes, 0, 5256000);
      root.time.elapsedMinutes += minutes;
      var total = ((root.time.day - 1) * 1440) + (root.time.hour * 60) + root.time.minute + minutes;
      root.time.day = Math.floor(total / 1440) + 1;
      var rem = total % 1440;
      root.time.hour = Math.floor(rem / 60);
      root.time.minute = rem % 60;
    },
    advance: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var minutes = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.minutes, 1, 5256000);
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, minutes);
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'elapsed', minutes: minutes, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'elapsed', minutes: minutes, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRouteBase: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var data = proposal.data;
      var id = data.targetId || data.routeId || WL.EventScanner.routeId(data.from, data.to);
      var route = root.time.routesById[id];
      if (!route) {
        route = { id: id, from: data.from, to: data.to, baseMinutes: data.minutes, delays: [], history: [], aliases: [data.from + ' -> ' + data.to, data.to + ' -> ' + data.from] };
        root.time.routesById[id] = route;
        root.time.routeIds.push(id);
      } else if (!route.baseMinutes) {
        route.baseMinutes = data.minutes;
      }
      root.time.currentRouteId = id;
      route.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'base', minutes: data.minutes, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(route.history, 20);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'route_base', routeId: id, baseMinutes: route.baseMinutes, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRouteDelay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = root.time.currentRouteId || 'route_unknown_current_trip';
      var route = root.time.routesById[id];
      if (!route) {
        route = { id: id, from: '', to: '', baseMinutes: 0, delays: [], history: [], aliases: [] };
        root.time.routesById[id] = route;
        root.time.routeIds.push(id);
      }
      route.delays.push({ key: ledger.key, reason: proposal.data.reason || 'Unknown', actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      route.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'delay', reason: proposal.data.reason || 'Unknown', actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0 });
      WL.U.capArray(route.delays, 20);
      WL.U.capArray(route.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'route_delay', routeId: id, actualMinutes: proposal.data.actualMinutes || 0, key: ledger.key };
    },
    restRecover: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var data = proposal.data || {};
      var minutes = WL.U.clamp(data.minutes || 480, 1, 1440);
      if (data.overnight) {
        WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceToNextDayTime(root, data.targetHour == null ? 7 : data.targetHour, data.targetMinute || 0);
      } else {
        WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, minutes);
      }
      root.body.hp = Math.min(root.body.maxHp, root.body.hp + WL.U.clamp(data.hpRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      root.body.mp = Math.min(root.body.maxMp, root.body.mp + WL.U.clamp(data.mpRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      root.body.ep = Math.min(root.body.maxEp, root.body.ep + WL.U.clamp(data.epRecover || 0, 0, 999));
      var removed = [];
      if (Array.isArray(root.body.conditions)) {
        var kept = [];
        for (var i = 0; i < root.body.conditions.length; i++) {
          var cond = root.body.conditions[i];
          var cname = WL.U.lower(cond && cond.name || cond && cond.id || '');
          if (/fatigue|fatigued|exhausted|strain/.test(cname) && data.restQuality !== 'poor') removed.push(cond.name || cond.id);
          else kept.push(cond);
        }
        root.body.conditions = kept;
      }
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'rest_recovery', minutes: minutes, overnight: !!data.overnight, quality: data.restQuality || 'ordinary', removedConditions: removed, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'rest_recovery', minutes: minutes, overnight: !!data.overnight, hp: root.body.hp, mp: root.body.mp, ep: root.body.ep, key: ledger.key };
    },
    advanceToNextDayTime: function (root, hour, minute) {
      hour = WL.U.clamp(hour, 0, 23);
      minute = WL.U.clamp(minute || 0, 0, 59);
      var currentAbs = ((root.time.day - 1) * 1440) + (root.time.hour * 60) + root.time.minute;
      var targetAbs = (root.time.day * 1440) + (hour * 60) + minute;
      if (targetAbs <= currentAbs) targetAbs += 1440;
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceClock(root, targetAbs - currentAbs);
    },
    nextDay: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var targetHour = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.hour || 8, 0, 23);
      WL.TimeTravelSystem.advanceToNextDayTime(root, targetHour, proposal.data.minute || 0);
      root.time.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'next_day', hour: root.time.hour, minute: root.time.minute, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'next_day', day: root.time.day, hour: root.time.hour, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordDeadline: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var label = proposal.data.label || 'Unknown Deadline';
      var id = 'deadline_' + WL.U.hash(label + '|' + proposal.data.task);
      if (!root.time.deadlines.some(function (d) { return d.id === id; })) {
        root.time.deadlines.push({ id: id, label: label, task: proposal.data.task || '', status: 'active', key: ledger.key });
      }
      WL.U.capArray(root.time.deadlines, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'time', type: 'deadline', deadlineId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Day ' + root.time.day + ', ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0') + ' / elapsed ' + root.time.elapsedMinutes + ' min');
      lines.push('Routes: ' + (root.time.routeIds.length ? root.time.routeIds.map(function (id) { var r = root.time.routesById[id]; return (r.from || '?') + ' <-> ' + (r.to || '?') + ' base ' + (r.baseMinutes || 0) + 'm delays ' + (r.delays || []).length; }).join('; ') : '(none)'));
      lines.push('Deadlines: ' + (root.time.deadlines.length ? root.time.deadlines.map(function (d) { return d.label + ' — ' + d.task; }).join('; ') : '(none)'));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.WorldStructureSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'place_definition_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.definePlace(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'current_place_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.setCurrent(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'layout_fact_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordLayout(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'owner_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRelation(root, p, ledger, 'owner');
      if (p.type === 'worker_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRelation(root, p, ledger, 'worker');
      if (p.type === 'room_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordRoom(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'quest_place_relation_candidate') return WL.WorldStructureSystem.recordQuestRelation(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'place', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_place_event' };
    },
    ensurePlace: function (root, name, type) {
      name = WL.U.title(name || 'Unknown Place');
      var id = 'place_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      var p = root.places.placesById[id];
      if (!p) {
        p = { id: id, name: name, type: type || 'place', locationHint: '', owners: [], workers: [], layout: {}, layoutHistory: [], contradictions: [], relations: [], history: [] };
        root.places.placesById[id] = p;
        root.places.recent.push(id);
      }
      if (type && (!p.type || p.type === 'place')) p.type = type;
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.recent, 40);
      return p;
    },
    definePlace: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      if (proposal.data.locationHint && !p.locationHint) p.locationHint = proposal.data.locationHint;
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'define', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      if (!root.places.currentId && proposal.data.confidence !== 'weak_world_info_place_candidate') root.places.currentId = p.id;
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'define', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    setCurrent: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      root.places.currentId = p.id;
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'current', phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'current', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordLayout: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var placeId = root.places.currentId;
      if (!placeId) return { status: 'PENDING_NO_CURRENT_PLACE', applied: false, family: 'place', type: 'layout', key: ledger.key };
      var p = root.places.placesById[placeId];
      if (!p) return { status: 'PENDING_NO_CURRENT_PLACE_RECORD', applied: false, family: 'place', type: 'layout', key: ledger.key };
      var fixture = proposal.data.fixture;
      var value = proposal.data.value;
      var old = p.layout[fixture];
      if (old && old !== value) {
        var rec = { key: ledger.key, placeId: placeId, fixture: fixture, oldValue: old, newValue: value, phrase: proposal.textPreview };
        p.contradictions.push(rec);
        root.places.contradictions.push(rec);
        WL.U.capArray(p.contradictions, 20);
        WL.U.capArray(root.places.contradictions, 40);
        return { status: 'CONTRADICTION_FLAGGED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'layout_contradiction', placeId: placeId, key: ledger.key };
      }
      p.layout[fixture] = value;
      p.layoutHistory.push({ key: ledger.key, fixture: fixture, value: value, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.layoutHistory, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'layout', placeId: placeId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger, kind) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, 'place');
      var actorName = proposal.data.actorName || '';
      var list = kind === 'owner' ? p.owners : p.workers;
      if (actorName && list.indexOf(actorName) < 0) list.push(actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: kind, actorName: actorName, placeId: p.id, placeName: p.name };
      p.relations.push(rec);
      root.places.relations.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(p.relations, 30);
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.relations, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: kind + '_relation', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRoom: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, 'building');
      if (!p.rooms) p.rooms = [];
      if (!p.roomIds) p.roomIds = [];
      var roomName = WL.U.title(proposal.data.roomName || 'Room');
      var roomId = proposal.data.roomId || ('room_' + WL.U.slug(p.name + '_' + roomName));
      if (p.roomIds.indexOf(roomId) < 0) {
        p.roomIds.push(roomId);
        p.rooms.push({ id: roomId, name: roomName, parentPlaceId: p.id, key: ledger.key });
      }
      p.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'room_reference', roomName: roomName, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(p.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'room_relation', placeId: p.id, roomId: roomId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordQuestRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var p = WL.WorldStructureSystem.ensurePlace(root, proposal.data.placeName, proposal.data.placeType || 'place');
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: 'quest_objective', questId: proposal.data.questId || '', questTitle: proposal.data.questTitle || '', placeId: p.id, placeName: p.name };
      p.relations.push(rec);
      root.places.relations.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(p.relations, 40);
      WL.U.capArray(root.places.relations, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'place', type: 'quest_place_relation', placeId: p.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var ids = Object.keys(root.places.placesById);
      var lines = [];
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var p = root.places.placesById[ids[i]];
        lines.push(p.name + ' [' + p.type + ']' + (root.places.currentId === p.id ? ' <current>' : '') + ' owners: ' + (p.owners.join(', ') || '-') + ' workers: ' + (p.workers.join(', ') || '-') + ' rooms:' + ((p.roomIds && p.roomIds.length) || 0) + ' rels:' + ((p.relations && p.relations.length) || 0));
      }
      if (root.places.contradictions.length) lines.push('Contradictions: ' + root.places.contradictions.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ActorSocialSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'scene_extra_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.sceneExtra(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'actor_social_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.social(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'actor_place_relation_candidate') return WL.ActorSocialSystem.placeRelation(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'actor', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_actor_event' };
    },
    ensureActor: function (root, name) {
      name = WL.U.title(name || 'Unknown Actor');
      var id = 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      var a = root.actors.actorsById[id];
      if (!a) {
        a = { id: id, name: name, role: '', places: [], relationships: [], opinions: [], goals: [], promises: [], debts: [], grudges: [], faction: '', currentPresence: '', lastSeen: '', notes: [], history: [] };
        root.actors.actorsById[id] = a;
        root.actors.referencedIds.push(id);
      }
      if (root.actors.presentIds.indexOf(id) < 0) root.actors.presentIds.push(id);
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.presentIds, 40);
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.referencedIds, 80);
      return a;
    },
    sceneExtra: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      root.actors.sceneExtras.push({ key: ledger.key, label: proposal.data.label || 'Extras', note: proposal.data.note || proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(root.actors.sceneExtras, 40);
      return { status: 'SCENE_EXTRA_ONLY', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'scene_extra', key: ledger.key };
    },
    social: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var a = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, proposal.data.actorName);
      var kind = proposal.data.socialKind || 'social_evidence';
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, kind: kind, note: proposal.data.note || proposal.textPreview };
      if (kind === 'grudge') a.grudges.push(rec);
      else if (kind === 'positive_attitude' || kind === 'negative_attitude') a.opinions.push(rec);
      else a.relationships.push(rec);
      a.history.push(rec);
      root.actors.socialLog.push({ actorId: a.id, kind: kind, key: ledger.key });
      WL.U.capArray(a.grudges, 20); WL.U.capArray(a.opinions, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.relationships, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.history, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.actors.socialLog, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'social', actorId: a.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    placeRelation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var a = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, proposal.data.actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, relation: proposal.data.relation || 'related', placeId: proposal.data.placeId || '', placeName: proposal.data.placeName || '' };
      if (rec.placeId && a.places.indexOf(rec.placeId) < 0) a.places.push(rec.placeId);
      a.relationships.push(rec);
      a.history.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(a.places, 20); WL.U.capArray(a.relationships, 30); WL.U.capArray(a.history, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'actor', type: 'place_relation', actorId: a.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var ids = Object.keys(root.actors.actorsById);
      var lines = [];
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var a = root.actors.actorsById[ids[i]];
        lines.push(a.name + ' opinions:' + a.opinions.length + ' grudges:' + a.grudges.length + ' places:' + a.places.length);
      }
      if (root.actors.sceneExtras.length) lines.push('Scene extras: ' + root.actors.sceneExtras.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };



  WL.InvestigationCaseSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.social || typeof root.social !== 'object') root.social = WL.Schema.root().social;
      if (!root.social.crimes || typeof root.social.crimes !== 'object') root.social.crimes = { casesById: {}, caseIds: [], recent: [] };
      if (!root.social.clues || typeof root.social.clues !== 'object') root.social.clues = { cluesById: {}, clueIds: [], recent: [] };
      if (!root.social.crimes.casesById || typeof root.social.crimes.casesById !== 'object') root.social.crimes.casesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.caseIds)) root.social.crimes.caseIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.recent)) root.social.crimes.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.clues.cluesById || typeof root.social.clues.cluesById !== 'object') root.social.clues.cluesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.clueIds)) root.social.clues.clueIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.recent)) root.social.clues.recent = [];
      return { crimes: root.social.crimes, clues: root.social.clues };
    },
    recentWitnessLinks: function (root, proposal) {
      var links = [];
      var ids = root.social && root.social.witnesses && root.social.witnesses.recordIds || [];
      var preview = WL.U.lower(proposal.textPreview || '');
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 8); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.witnesses.recordsById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var sameTurn = rec.turn === root.runtime.currentTurn;
        var summary = WL.U.lower(rec.eventSummary || '');
        var shared = summary && preview && (preview.indexOf(summary.slice(0, Math.min(30, summary.length))) >= 0 || summary.indexOf(preview.slice(0, Math.min(30, preview.length))) >= 0 || /steal|stole|theft|kill|attack|burn|break/.test(summary + ' ' + preview));
        if (sameTurn && (shared || rec.witnessed)) links.push({ witnessId: rec.id, witnessed: !!rec.witnessed, visibility: rec.visibility || '', witnessCount: (rec.witnesses || []).length });
      }
      return links.slice(0, 6);
    },
    recordCrime: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var buckets = WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = proposal.data || {};
      var id = d.targetId || ('crime_' + WL.U.hash((d.crimeKind || 'crime') + '|' + (d.suspectId || 'player') + '|' + (d.itemId || d.victimId || proposal.textPreview)).slice(0, 16));
      var rec = buckets.crimes.casesById[id] || { id: id, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, evidence: [], clueIds: [], witnessRecordIds: [], rumorIds: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.crimeKind = d.crimeKind || rec.crimeKind || 'unlawful_act';
      rec.suspectId = d.suspectId || rec.suspectId || 'player';
      rec.suspectName = d.suspectName || rec.suspectName || (root.player && root.player.name || 'Player');
      rec.victimId = d.victimId || rec.victimId || '';
      rec.victimName = d.victimName || rec.victimName || '';
      rec.itemId = d.itemId || rec.itemId || '';
      rec.itemName = d.itemName || rec.itemName || '';
      rec.placeId = d.placeId || rec.placeId || (root.places && root.places.currentId || '');
      rec.placeName = d.placeName || rec.placeName || (WL.ContextBudgeter ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : '');
      rec.visibility = d.visibility || rec.visibility || 'unknown_visibility';
      rec.status = d.status || rec.status || 'suspected_or_unconfirmed';
      rec.legalStatus = rec.legalStatus || 'none_not_enforced_yet';
      rec.enforcementStage = rec.enforcementStage || 'not_implemented_in_9_5f';
      rec.proofLevel = Math.max(rec.proofLevel || 0, Number(d.proofLevel || 0));
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.confidence = d.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'crime_case_candidate';
      var witnessLinks = WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.recentWitnessLinks(root, proposal);
      for (var i = 0; i < witnessLinks.length; i++) {
        if (rec.witnessRecordIds.indexOf(witnessLinks[i].witnessId) < 0) rec.witnessRecordIds.push(witnessLinks[i].witnessId);
        if (witnessLinks[i].witnessed) { rec.proofLevel = Math.max(rec.proofLevel, 1); if (rec.status === 'hidden_unreported') rec.status = 'witnessed_unadjudicated'; }
      }
      rec.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, kind: 'crime_event', proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, phrase: proposal.textPreview || d.note || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 16); WL.U.capArray(rec.witnessRecordIds, 12);
      buckets.crimes.casesById[id] = rec;
      if (buckets.crimes.caseIds.indexOf(id) < 0) buckets.crimes.caseIds.push(id);
      buckets.crimes.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, caseId: id, kind: rec.crimeKind, proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, status: rec.status });
      root.social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'crime_case', caseId: id, proofLevel: rec.proofLevel });
      WL.U.capArray(buckets.crimes.caseIds, 120); WL.U.capArray(buckets.crimes.recent, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.social.recent, 80);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var caseNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'crime_case', id, rec.crimeKind, { weight: 3, tags: ['crime', rec.status, rec.visibility], summary: (rec.itemName || rec.victimName || rec.placeName || rec.crimeKind).slice(0, 80) });
        var playerNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'player', 'player', rec.suspectName, { weight: 5, tags: ['player'] });
        if (caseNode && playerNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, playerNode.id, caseNode.id, 'suspected_in', Math.max(1, rec.proofLevel || 1));
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'crime_case', caseId: id, proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, key: ledger.key };
    },
    findRelatedCase: function (root, data) {
      var ids = root.social && root.social.crimes && root.social.crimes.caseIds || [];
      var itemSlug = WL.U.slug(data.itemName || data.itemId || '');
      for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0 && i >= ids.length - 20; i--) {
        var rec = root.social.crimes.casesById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        if (itemSlug && (WL.U.slug(rec.itemName || rec.itemId || '') === itemSlug)) return rec;
        if (data.placeId && rec.placeId === data.placeId) return rec;
      }
      return null;
    },
    recordClue: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var buckets = WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = proposal.data || {};
      var id = d.targetId || ('clue_' + WL.U.hash((d.clueKind || 'clue') + '|' + (d.summary || proposal.textPreview)).slice(0, 16));
      var rec = buckets.clues.cluesById[id] || { id: id, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, observations: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.clueKind = d.clueKind || rec.clueKind || 'evidence';
      rec.summary = WL.U.safeString(d.summary || proposal.textPreview || rec.summary || 'Evidence').slice(0, 220);
      rec.itemId = d.itemId || rec.itemId || '';
      rec.itemName = d.itemName || rec.itemName || '';
      rec.foundById = d.foundById || rec.foundById || '';
      rec.foundByName = d.foundByName || rec.foundByName || '';
      rec.placeId = d.placeId || rec.placeId || (root.places && root.places.currentId || '');
      rec.placeName = d.placeName || rec.placeName || (WL.ContextBudgeter ? WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) : '');
      rec.visibilityScope = d.visibilityScope || rec.visibilityScope || 'evidence_recorded';
      rec.proofLevel = Math.max(rec.proofLevel || 0, Number(d.proofLevel || 1));
      rec.confidence = d.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'confirmed_evidence_or_clue';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      var related = WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.findRelatedCase(root, d);
      if (related) {
        rec.caseId = related.id;
        if (related.clueIds.indexOf(id) < 0) related.clueIds.push(id);
        related.proofLevel = Math.max(related.proofLevel || 0, rec.proofLevel || 1);
        related.status = related.status === 'hidden_unreported' ? 'evidence_found_unadjudicated' : related.status;
        related.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, kind: 'clue_link', clueId: id, proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, phrase: rec.summary });
        WL.U.capArray(related.evidence, 16); WL.U.capArray(related.clueIds, 16);
      }
      rec.observations.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, phrase: proposal.textPreview || d.note || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.observations, 12);
      buckets.clues.cluesById[id] = rec;
      if (buckets.clues.clueIds.indexOf(id) < 0) buckets.clues.clueIds.push(id);
      buckets.clues.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, clueId: id, proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, caseId: rec.caseId || '' });
      root.social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'clue_evidence', clueId: id, caseId: rec.caseId || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(buckets.clues.clueIds, 120); WL.U.capArray(buckets.clues.recent, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.social.recent, 80);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var clueNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'clue', id, rec.clueKind, { weight: 2, tags: ['clue', rec.visibilityScope], summary: rec.summary.slice(0, 80) });
        if (rec.caseId) {
          var caseNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'crime_case', rec.caseId, 'Crime Case', { weight: 3, tags: ['crime'] });
          if (caseNode && clueNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, clueNode.id, caseNode.id, 'evidence_for', Math.max(1, rec.proofLevel || 1));
        }
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'clue_evidence', clueId: id, caseId: rec.caseId || '', proofLevel: rec.proofLevel, key: ledger.key };
    },
    crimeSummary: function (root) {
      WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.crimes.caseIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Crime Cases: (none)'];
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 5); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.crimes.casesById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var target = rec.itemName || rec.victimName || rec.placeName || 'unknown target';
