        lines.push((rec.crimeKind || 'crime') + ' [' + (rec.status || 'case') + ', proof ' + (rec.proofLevel || 0) + ']: ' + (rec.suspectName || 'Unknown') + ' / ' + target + (rec.witnessRecordIds && rec.witnessRecordIds.length ? ' / witnesses ' + rec.witnessRecordIds.length : '') + (rec.clueIds && rec.clueIds.length ? ' / clues ' + rec.clueIds.length : ''));
      }
      return lines;
    },
    clueSummary: function (root) {
      WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.clues.clueIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Clues: (none)'];
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 5); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.clues.cluesById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        lines.push((rec.clueKind || 'clue') + ' [proof ' + (rec.proofLevel || 1) + ']: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.summary).slice(0, 100) + (rec.caseId ? ' -> ' + rec.caseId : ''));
      }
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.RumorPropagationSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.social || typeof root.social !== 'object') root.social = WL.Schema.root().social;
      if (!root.social.rumors || typeof root.social.rumors !== 'object') root.social.rumors = { rumorsById: {}, rumorIds: [], queue: [], recent: [] };
      if (!root.social.rumors.rumorsById || typeof root.social.rumors.rumorsById !== 'object') root.social.rumors.rumorsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.rumorIds)) root.social.rumors.rumorIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.queue)) root.social.rumors.queue = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.recent)) root.social.rumors.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.crimes.casesById || typeof root.social.crimes.casesById !== 'object') root.social.crimes.casesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.caseIds)) root.social.crimes.caseIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.recent)) root.social.crimes.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.clues.cluesById || typeof root.social.clues.cluesById !== 'object') root.social.clues.cluesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.clueIds)) root.social.clues.clueIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.recent)) root.social.clues.recent = [];
      return root.social.rumors;
    },
    record: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.RumorPropagationSystem.ensure(root);
      var data = proposal.data || {};
      var id = data.targetId || ('rumor_' + WL.U.hash((data.summary || proposal.textPreview || '') + '|' + ledger.key).slice(0, 16));
      var rec = bucket.rumorsById[id] || { id: id, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, hops: [], evidence: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.summary = WL.U.safeString(data.summary || proposal.textPreview || '').slice(0, 220);
      rec.originScopeType = data.originScopeType || rec.originScopeType || 'local';
      rec.originScopeId = data.originScopeId || rec.originScopeId || 'rumor_scope_local_public';
      rec.originScopeName = data.originScopeName || rec.originScopeName || 'Local Public';
      rec.visibilityScope = data.visibilityScope || rec.visibilityScope || 'local_rumor_network';
      rec.channel = data.channel || rec.channel || 'local_word_of_mouth';
      rec.sourceActorId = data.sourceActorId || rec.sourceActorId || '';
      rec.sourceActorName = data.sourceActorName || rec.sourceActorName || '';
      rec.sourceMode = data.sourceMode || rec.sourceMode || 'rumor_spread_statement';
      rec.confidenceScore = WL.U.clamp(Number(data.confidenceScore == null ? rec.confidenceScore || 0.5 : data.confidenceScore), 0, 1);
      rec.distortion = WL.U.clamp(Number(data.distortion == null ? rec.distortion || 0 : data.distortion), 0, 5);
      rec.routeHint = data.routeHint || rec.routeHint || rec.originScopeName;
      rec.status = data.targetScopeId ? 'queued_or_reached_named_target_by_story_evidence' : 'local_circulating';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      var hop = {
        key: ledger.key,
        turn: root.runtime.currentTurn,
        fromScopeId: rec.originScopeId,
        fromScopeName: rec.originScopeName,
        toScopeId: data.targetScopeId || rec.originScopeId,
        toScopeName: data.targetScopeName || rec.originScopeName,
        channel: rec.channel,
        delayTurns: WL.U.clamp(Number(data.delayTurns || 1), 1, 999),
        confidenceScore: rec.confidenceScore,
        distortion: rec.distortion,
        routeHint: rec.routeHint
      };
      rec.hops.push(hop);
      WL.U.capArray(rec.hops, 12);
      rec.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview || data.note || '', confidenceScore: rec.confidenceScore, distortion: rec.distortion });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 10);
      bucket.rumorsById[id] = rec;
      if (bucket.rumorIds.indexOf(id) < 0) bucket.rumorIds.push(id);
      if (hop.delayTurns > 1 || data.targetScopeId) bucket.queue.push({ rumorId: id, targetScopeId: hop.toScopeId, targetScopeName: hop.toScopeName, channel: hop.channel, earliestTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn + hop.delayTurns, confidenceScore: rec.confidenceScore, distortion: rec.distortion });
      bucket.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, rumorId: id, scope: hop.toScopeName, channel: hop.channel, confidenceScore: rec.confidenceScore });
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.recent)) root.social.recent = [];
      root.social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'rumor_event', rumorId: id, scope: hop.toScopeName, channel: hop.channel });
      WL.U.capArray(bucket.rumorIds, 100); WL.U.capArray(bucket.queue, 40); WL.U.capArray(bucket.recent, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.social.recent, 80);
      if (rec.sourceActorName) WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordActorPresence(root, { data: { actorName: rec.sourceActorName, actorId: rec.sourceActorId, mode: 'rumor_source', visibility: 'scene', confidence: 'rumor_source_presence' }, textPreview: proposal.textPreview }, ledger);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var rumorNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'rumor', id, rec.summary.slice(0, 60), { weight: 2, tags: ['rumor', rec.channel, rec.visibilityScope], summary: 'confidence ' + rec.confidenceScore + ', distortion ' + rec.distortion });
        var scopeNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, rec.originScopeType || 'rumor_scope', rec.originScopeId, rec.originScopeName, { weight: 2, tags: ['rumor_scope', rec.channel] });
        if (rumorNode && scopeNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, scopeNode.id, rumorNode.id, 'carries_rumor', 2);
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'rumor_event', rumorId: id, channel: rec.channel, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      WL.RumorPropagationSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.rumors.rumorIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Rumors: (none)'];
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 6); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.rumors.rumorsById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var last = rec.hops && rec.hops.length ? rec.hops[rec.hops.length - 1] : null;
        lines.push((rec.originScopeName || 'Local') + ' via ' + (rec.channel || 'word_of_mouth') + ' [' + (rec.status || 'circulating') + ', conf ' + Math.round((rec.confidenceScore || 0) * 100) + '%, dist ' + (rec.distortion || 0) + ']: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.summary).slice(0, 90) + (last && last.delayTurns > 1 ? ' (delay ' + last.delayTurns + ')' : ''));
      }
      return lines;
    }
  };


  WL.FactionLawSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.social || typeof root.social !== 'object') root.social = WL.Schema.root().social;
      if (!root.social.law || typeof root.social.law !== 'object') root.social.law = { lawsByScopeId: {}, lawScopeIds: [], responsesById: {}, responseIds: [], enforcementQueue: [], recent: [] };
      if (!root.social.law.lawsByScopeId || typeof root.social.law.lawsByScopeId !== 'object') root.social.law.lawsByScopeId = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.lawScopeIds)) root.social.law.lawScopeIds = [];
      if (!root.social.law.responsesById || typeof root.social.law.responsesById !== 'object') root.social.law.responsesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.responseIds)) root.social.law.responseIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.enforcementQueue)) root.social.law.enforcementQueue = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.recent)) root.social.law.recent = [];
      return root.social.law;
    },
    findRelatedCase: function (root, data) {
      var ids = root.social && root.social.crimes && root.social.crimes.caseIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return null;
      var text = WL.U.lower((data && data.note || '') + ' ' + (data && data.responseStage || '') + ' ' + (data && data.scopeName || ''));
      for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0 && i >= ids.length - 20; i--) {
        var rec = root.social.crimes.casesById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var hay = WL.U.lower((rec.crimeKind || '') + ' ' + (rec.itemName || '') + ' ' + (rec.victimName || '') + ' ' + (rec.status || '') + ' ' + (rec.placeName || ''));
        if (!text || /case|crime|theft|stolen|warrant|arrest|investigat|fine|charge/.test(text)) return rec;
        if (hay && text.indexOf((rec.itemName || '').toLowerCase()) >= 0) return rec;
      }
      return null;
    },
    recordLaw: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var law = WL.FactionLawSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = proposal.data || {};
      var scopeId = d.scopeId || ('law_scope_' + WL.U.slug(d.scopeName || 'local law'));
      var rec = law.lawsByScopeId[scopeId] || { id: scopeId, scopeId: scopeId, scopeName: d.scopeName || 'Local Law', scopeType: d.scopeType || 'local_law', rulesByKind: {}, evidence: [] };
      var kind = d.lawKind || 'unlawful_act';
      rec.scopeName = d.scopeName || rec.scopeName;
      rec.scopeType = d.scopeType || rec.scopeType;
      rec.rulesByKind[kind] = { kind: kind, severity: WL.U.clamp(d.severity || 1, 1, 5), sourceMode: d.sourceMode || 'explicit_law_rule_statement', lastTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      rec.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, kind: kind, phrase: proposal.textPreview || d.note || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 12);
      law.lawsByScopeId[scopeId] = rec;
      if (law.lawScopeIds.indexOf(scopeId) < 0) law.lawScopeIds.push(scopeId);
      law.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'law_record', scopeId: scopeId, kind: kind, severity: d.severity || 1 });
      WL.U.capArray(law.lawScopeIds, 80); WL.U.capArray(law.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'law_record', scopeId: scopeId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordResponse: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var law = WL.FactionLawSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = proposal.data || {};
      var id = d.targetId || ('law_response_' + WL.U.hash((d.authorityId || '') + '|' + (d.responseStage || '') + '|' + proposal.textPreview).slice(0, 16));
      var rec = law.responsesById[id] || { id: id, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, evidence: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.authorityType = d.authorityType || rec.authorityType || 'local_authority';
      rec.authorityId = d.authorityId || rec.authorityId || 'law_auth_local_authority';
      rec.authorityName = d.authorityName || rec.authorityName || 'Local Authority';
      rec.scopeType = d.scopeType || rec.scopeType || 'local_law';
      rec.scopeId = d.scopeId || rec.scopeId || 'law_scope_local_law';
      rec.scopeName = d.scopeName || rec.scopeName || 'Local Law';
      rec.responseStage = d.responseStage || rec.responseStage || 'law_response_recorded';
      rec.responseStatus = d.responseStatus || rec.responseStatus || 'active_risk_record';
      rec.suspectId = d.suspectId || rec.suspectId || 'player';
      rec.suspectName = d.suspectName || rec.suspectName || (root.player && root.player.name || 'Player');
      rec.severity = WL.U.clamp(d.severity || rec.severity || 1, 1, 5);
      rec.amount = Number(d.amount || rec.amount || 0);
      rec.currency = d.currency || rec.currency || '';
      rec.noAutomaticPunishment = true;
      rec.sourceMode = d.sourceMode || rec.sourceMode || 'explicit_law_response_statement';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.confidence = d.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'explicit_law_enforcement_response';
      var related = WL.FactionLawSystem.findRelatedCase(root, d);
      if (related) {
        rec.caseId = related.id;
        related.legalStatus = rec.responseStage;
        related.enforcementStage = rec.responseStage;
        related.evidence = related.evidence || [];
        related.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, kind: 'law_response_link', responseId: id, stage: rec.responseStage, phrase: proposal.textPreview || d.note || '' });
        WL.U.capArray(related.evidence, 16);
      }
      rec.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, stage: rec.responseStage, phrase: proposal.textPreview || d.note || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 12);
      law.responsesById[id] = rec;
      if (law.responseIds.indexOf(id) < 0) law.responseIds.push(id);
      if (rec.responseStatus !== 'closed_or_reduced_by_story' && law.enforcementQueue.indexOf(id) < 0) law.enforcementQueue.push(id);
      law.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'law_response', responseId: id, stage: rec.responseStage, authority: rec.authorityName, caseId: rec.caseId || '' });
      root.social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'law_response', responseId: id, stage: rec.responseStage, authority: rec.authorityName });
      WL.U.capArray(law.responseIds, 120); WL.U.capArray(law.enforcementQueue, 40); WL.U.capArray(law.recent, 40); WL.U.capArray(root.social.recent, 80);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var lawNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'law_response', id, rec.responseStage, { weight: 3, tags: ['law', rec.responseStage, rec.authorityType], summary: rec.authorityName + ' ' + rec.responseStage });
        var pNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'player', 'player', root.player.name || 'Player', { weight: 5, tags: ['player'] });
        if (lawNode && pNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, pNode.id, lawNode.id, 'subject_to_law_response', Math.max(1, rec.severity || 1));
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'law_response', responseId: id, stage: rec.responseStage, caseId: rec.caseId || '', key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var law = WL.FactionLawSystem.ensure(root);
      var lines = [];
      var rids = law.responseIds || [];
      if (!rids.length) lines.push('Law/Enforcement: (none)');
      for (var i = Math.max(0, rids.length - 5); i < rids.length; i++) {
        var rec = law.responsesById[rids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        lines.push((rec.authorityName || 'Authority') + ': ' + (rec.responseStage || 'response') + (rec.caseId ? ' linked ' + rec.caseId : '') + (rec.amount ? ' fine ' + rec.amount + ' ' + rec.currency : '') + ' [no auto-punishment]');
      }
      var lids = law.lawScopeIds || [];
      if (lids.length && lines.length < 6) {
        var scope = law.lawsByScopeId[lids[lids.length - 1]];
        if (scope) lines.push('Law scope: ' + scope.scopeName + ' rules ' + Object.keys(scope.rulesByKind || {}).slice(0, 4).join(', '));
      }
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.SocialConsequenceSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.social || typeof root.social !== 'object') root.social = WL.Schema.root().social;
      if (!root.social.presence || typeof root.social.presence !== 'object') root.social.presence = WL.Schema.root().social.presence;
      if (!root.social.knowledge || typeof root.social.knowledge !== 'object') root.social.knowledge = WL.Schema.root().social.knowledge;
      if (!root.social.witnesses || typeof root.social.witnesses !== 'object') root.social.witnesses = WL.Schema.root().social.witnesses;
      if (!root.social.reputation || typeof root.social.reputation !== 'object') root.social.reputation = WL.Schema.root().social.reputation;
      if (!root.social.rumors || typeof root.social.rumors !== 'object') root.social.rumors = WL.Schema.root().social.rumors || { rumorsById: {}, rumorIds: [], queue: [], recent: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.presence.actorIds)) root.social.presence.actorIds = [];
      if (!root.social.presence.actorsById || typeof root.social.presence.actorsById !== 'object') root.social.presence.actorsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.presence.genericGroupIds)) root.social.presence.genericGroupIds = [];
      if (!root.social.presence.genericGroupsById || typeof root.social.presence.genericGroupsById !== 'object') root.social.presence.genericGroupsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.knowledge.factIds)) root.social.knowledge.factIds = [];
      if (!root.social.knowledge.factsById || typeof root.social.knowledge.factsById !== 'object') root.social.knowledge.factsById = {};
      if (!root.social.knowledge.byHolder || typeof root.social.knowledge.byHolder !== 'object') root.social.knowledge.byHolder = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.knowledge.recent)) root.social.knowledge.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.witnesses.recordsById || typeof root.social.witnesses.recordsById !== 'object') root.social.witnesses.recordsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.witnesses.recordIds)) root.social.witnesses.recordIds = [];
      if (!root.social.reputation.scopesById || typeof root.social.reputation.scopesById !== 'object') root.social.reputation.scopesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.reputation.scopeIds)) root.social.reputation.scopeIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.reputation.recent)) root.social.reputation.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.rumors.rumorsById || typeof root.social.rumors.rumorsById !== 'object') root.social.rumors.rumorsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.rumorIds)) root.social.rumors.rumorIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.queue)) root.social.rumors.queue = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.rumors.recent)) root.social.rumors.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.crimes.casesById || typeof root.social.crimes.casesById !== 'object') root.social.crimes.casesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.caseIds)) root.social.crimes.caseIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.crimes.recent)) root.social.crimes.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.clues.cluesById || typeof root.social.clues.cluesById !== 'object') root.social.clues.cluesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.clueIds)) root.social.clues.clueIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.clues.recent)) root.social.clues.recent = [];
      if (!root.social.law.lawsByScopeId || typeof root.social.law.lawsByScopeId !== 'object') root.social.law.lawsByScopeId = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.lawScopeIds)) root.social.law.lawScopeIds = [];
      if (!root.social.law.responsesById || typeof root.social.law.responsesById !== 'object') root.social.law.responsesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.responseIds)) root.social.law.responseIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.enforcementQueue)) root.social.law.enforcementQueue = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.law.recent)) root.social.law.recent = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.social.recent)) root.social.recent = [];
      return root.social;
    },
    sceneId: function (root) {
      var place = root.places && root.places.currentId || 'unknown_place';
      return 'scene_' + WL.U.slug(place + '_day_' + root.time.day + '_' + root.time.hour + '_' + root.time.minute);
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'presence_actor_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordActorPresence(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'presence_generic_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordGenericPresence(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'presence_hidden_observer_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordHiddenObserver(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'presence_departure_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordDeparture(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'knowledge_fact_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordKnowledge(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'witness_record_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordWitness(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'reputation_evidence_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordReputation(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'rumor_event_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordRumor(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'crime_case_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordCrime(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'clue_evidence_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordClue(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'law_record_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordLaw(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'law_response_candidate') return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordLawResponse(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'social', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_social_event' };
    },
    recordActorPresence: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.actorName || proposal.targetName || 'Unknown Actor');
      var actor = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, name);
      var rec = social.presence.actorsById[actor.id] || { actorId: actor.id, actorName: actor.name, firstSeenTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, modes: [], history: [] };
      rec.actorId = actor.id; rec.actorName = actor.name; rec.lastSeenTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn; rec.sceneId = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.sceneId(root); rec.placeId = root.places.currentId || ''; rec.visibility = proposal.data.visibility || 'scene'; rec.mode = proposal.data.mode || 'present'; rec.confidence = proposal.data.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'confirmed_scene_presence';
      if (rec.modes.indexOf(rec.mode) < 0) rec.modes.push(rec.mode);
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, mode: rec.mode, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 10);
      social.presence.actorsById[actor.id] = rec;
      if (social.presence.actorIds.indexOf(actor.id) < 0) social.presence.actorIds.push(actor.id);
      social.presence.updatedTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'presence_actor', actorId: actor.id, name: actor.name });
      WL.U.capArray(social.presence.actorIds, 40); WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var node = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'actor', actor.id, actor.name, { weight: 4, tags: ['actor','present'], summary: 'currently or recently present' });
        var player = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'player', 'player', root.player.name || 'Player', { weight: 5, tags: ['player'] });
        if (node && player) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, player.id, node.id, 'scene_presence', 2);
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'presence_actor', actorId: actor.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordDeparture: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var name = WL.U.title(proposal.data.actorName || proposal.targetName || 'Unknown Actor');
      var id = proposal.data.actorId || ('actor_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      if (social.presence.actorIds.indexOf(id) >= 0) social.presence.actorIds.splice(social.presence.actorIds.indexOf(id), 1);
      if (root.actors && root.actors.presentIds && root.actors.presentIds.indexOf(id) >= 0) root.actors.presentIds.splice(root.actors.presentIds.indexOf(id), 1);
      if (social.presence.departedActorIds.indexOf(id) < 0) social.presence.departedActorIds.push(id);
      if (social.presence.actorsById[id]) { social.presence.actorsById[id].departedTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn; social.presence.actorsById[id].mode = 'departed'; }
      social.presence.updatedTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'presence_departure', actorId: id, name: name });
      WL.U.capArray(social.presence.departedActorIds, 40); WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'presence_departure', actorId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordGenericPresence: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var label = WL.U.title(proposal.data.groupLabel || 'Bystanders');
      var id = proposal.data.groupId || ('presence_group_' + WL.U.slug(label));
      var rec = social.presence.genericGroupsById[id] || { groupId: id, label: label, firstSeenTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, count: 0, history: [] };
      rec.label = label; rec.mode = proposal.data.mode || 'present'; rec.count = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.count || rec.count || 0, 0, 9999); rec.lastSeenTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn; rec.sceneId = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.sceneId(root); rec.placeId = root.places.currentId || ''; rec.confidence = proposal.data.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'confirmed_generic_scene_presence';
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, mode: rec.mode, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 10);
      social.presence.genericGroupsById[id] = rec;
      if (social.presence.genericGroupIds.indexOf(id) < 0) social.presence.genericGroupIds.push(id);
      social.presence.updatedTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'presence_generic', groupId: id, label: label });
      WL.U.capArray(social.presence.genericGroupIds, 24); WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'presence_generic', groupId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordHiddenObserver: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      social.presence.hiddenObserverCandidates.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, note: proposal.data.note || proposal.textPreview, confidence: proposal.data.confidence || 'possible_only' });
      WL.U.capArray(social.presence.hiddenObserverCandidates, 20);
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'presence_hidden_observer_candidate' });
      WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'RECORDED_CANDIDATE', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'presence_hidden_observer', key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordKnowledge: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var fact = WL.EventScanner.cleanKnowledgeFact(proposal.data.fact || proposal.targetName || '');
      if (!fact) return { status: 'REJECTED_EMPTY_FACT', applied: false, family: 'social', type: 'knowledge_fact', key: ledger.key };
      var holderType = proposal.data.holderType || 'unknown';
      var holderId = proposal.data.holderId || (holderType + '_' + WL.U.slug(proposal.data.holderName || 'unknown'));
      var holderName = proposal.data.holderName || holderId;
      var factId = 'knowledge_' + WL.U.slug(holderId + '_' + fact).slice(0, 90);
      var rec = social.knowledge.factsById[factId] || { id: factId, holderType: holderType, holderId: holderId, holderName: holderName, fact: fact, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, observations: [] };
      rec.holderType = holderType; rec.holderId = holderId; rec.holderName = holderName; rec.fact = fact; rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn; rec.sourceMode = proposal.data.sourceMode || 'learned'; rec.truthStatus = proposal.data.truthStatus || 'alleged'; rec.visibilityScope = proposal.data.visibilityScope || 'private'; rec.confidence = proposal.data.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'knowledge_evidence'; rec.sourceActorId = proposal.data.sourceActorId || rec.sourceActorId || ''; rec.sourceActorName = proposal.data.sourceActorName || rec.sourceActorName || '';
      rec.observations.push({ key: ledger.key, sourceMode: rec.sourceMode, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.observations, 8);
      social.knowledge.factsById[factId] = rec;
      if (social.knowledge.factIds.indexOf(factId) < 0) social.knowledge.factIds.push(factId);
      if (!social.knowledge.byHolder[holderId]) social.knowledge.byHolder[holderId] = [];
      if (social.knowledge.byHolder[holderId].indexOf(factId) < 0) social.knowledge.byHolder[holderId].push(factId);
      social.knowledge.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, factId: factId, holderId: holderId, fact: fact.slice(0, 100) });
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'knowledge_fact', factId: factId, holderId: holderId });
      WL.U.capArray(social.knowledge.factIds, 120); WL.U.capArray(social.knowledge.byHolder[holderId], 40); WL.U.capArray(social.knowledge.recent, 40); WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      if (rec.sourceActorName) WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.recordActorPresence(root, { data: { actorName: rec.sourceActorName, actorId: rec.sourceActorId, mode: 'knowledge_source', visibility: 'scene', confidence: 'knowledge_source_presence' }, textPreview: proposal.textPreview }, ledger);
      if (holderType === 'actor' && holderName) WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, holderName);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var kNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'knowledge', factId, fact.slice(0, 60), { weight: 2, tags: ['knowledge', rec.visibilityScope], summary: rec.truthStatus });
        var hNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, holderType || 'holder', holderId, holderName, { weight: holderType === 'player' ? 5 : 3, tags: ['knowledge_holder', holderType] });
        if (kNode && hNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, hNode.id, kNode.id, 'knows', 2);
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'knowledge_fact', factId: factId, holderId: holderId, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordWitness: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var data = proposal.data || {};
      var witnesses = Array.isArray(data.witnesses) ? data.witnesses.slice(0, 12) : [];
      var id = data.targetId || ('witness_' + WL.U.hash((data.eventSummary || proposal.textPreview || '') + '|' + ledger.key).slice(0, 16));
      var rec = social.witnesses.recordsById[id] || { id: id, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, observations: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.turn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.sceneId = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.sceneId(root);
      rec.placeId = root.places.currentId || '';
      rec.eventKind = data.eventKind || 'visible_event';
      rec.eventSummary = WL.U.safeString(data.eventSummary || proposal.textPreview || '').slice(0, 220);
      rec.witnessed = !!data.witnessed && witnesses.length > 0;
      rec.witnesses = witnesses.map(function (w) { return { kind: w.kind || 'unknown', id: w.id || '', name: WL.U.safeString(w.name || w.id || 'Unknown'), mode: w.mode || 'witness', confidence: w.confidence || data.confidence || 'witness_candidate' }; });
      rec.visibility = data.visibility || (rec.witnessed ? 'observed' : 'private_or_unseen');
      rec.privacy = data.privacy || (rec.witnessed ? 'observed_by_witnesses' : 'not_observed_by_known_witnesses');
      rec.confidence = data.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'witness_candidate';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.observations.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, witnessCount: rec.witnesses.length });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.observations, 8);
      social.witnesses.recordsById[id] = rec;
      if (social.witnesses.recordIds.indexOf(id) < 0) social.witnesses.recordIds.push(id);
      WL.U.capArray(social.witnesses.recordIds, 120);
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'witness_record', witnessId: id, witnessed: rec.witnessed, count: rec.witnesses.length });
      WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 60);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var eNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'witness_event', id, rec.eventKind, { weight: rec.witnessed ? 3 : 1, tags: ['witness', rec.visibility], summary: rec.eventSummary.slice(0, 80) });
        for (var i = 0; i < rec.witnesses.length && i < 6; i++) {
          var w = rec.witnesses[i];
          var wNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, w.kind || 'witness', w.id || ('witness_' + i), w.name || 'Witness', { weight: 2, tags: ['witness_holder', w.kind || 'unknown'] });
          if (eNode && wNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, wNode.id, eNode.id, 'witnessed', 2);
        }
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'witness_record', witnessId: id, witnessed: rec.witnessed, count: rec.witnesses.length, key: ledger.key };
    },

    recordRumor: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      return WL.RumorPropagationSystem.record(root, proposal, ledger);
    },
    recordCrime: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      return WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.recordCrime(root, proposal, ledger);
    },
    recordClue: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      return WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.recordClue(root, proposal, ledger);
    },
    crimeSummary: function (root) { return WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.crimeSummary(root); },
    clueSummary: function (root) { return WL.InvestigationCaseSystem.clueSummary(root); },
    recordLaw: function (root, proposal, ledger) { return WL.FactionLawSystem.recordLaw(root, proposal, ledger); },
    recordLawResponse: function (root, proposal, ledger) { return WL.FactionLawSystem.recordResponse(root, proposal, ledger); },
    lawSummary: function (root) { return WL.FactionLawSystem.summary(root); },
    reputationAxisWeight: function (axis, value) {
      value = Number(value || 0);
      if (/^(?:trust|respect|gratitude|standing)$/.test(axis || '')) return value;
      if (/^(?:hostility|suspicion|notoriety)$/.test(axis || '')) return -Math.abs(value);
      if (/^fear$/.test(axis || '')) return 0;
      return value;
    },
    reputationScore: function (rec) {
      var axes = rec && rec.axes || {};
      var keys = Object.keys(axes);
      var total = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) total += WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.reputationAxisWeight(keys[i], axes[keys[i]]);
      return WL.U.clamp(total, -100, 100);
    },
    recordReputation: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var social = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var data = proposal.data || {};
      var scopeType = data.scopeType || 'local';
      var scopeName = WL.U.safeString(data.scopeName || data.scopeId || 'Local Public');
      var scopeId = data.scopeId || (scopeType + '_' + WL.U.slug(scopeName));
      var axis = WL.U.slug(data.axis || 'standing');
      var delta = WL.U.clamp(Number(data.delta || 0), -3, 3);
      if (!axis || !delta) return { status: 'REJECTED_EMPTY_REPUTATION_DELTA', applied: false, family: 'social', type: 'reputation_evidence', key: ledger.key };
      var rec = social.reputation.scopesById[scopeId] || { id: scopeId, scopeType: scopeType, scopeName: scopeName, firstTurn: root.runtime.currentTurn, axes: {}, evidence: [] };
      rec.id = scopeId;
      rec.scopeType = scopeType;
      rec.scopeName = scopeName;
      rec.placeId = data.placeId || rec.placeId || (root.places && root.places.currentId || '');
      rec.visibilityScope = data.visibilityScope || rec.visibilityScope || (scopeType === 'actor' ? 'private_actor' : (scopeType === 'faction' ? 'faction_scoped' : 'local_public'));
      rec.axes = rec.axes || {};
      rec.axes[axis] = WL.U.clamp((rec.axes[axis] || 0) + delta, -100, 100);
      rec.lastAxis = axis;
      rec.lastDelta = delta;
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.confidence = data.confidence || proposal.confidence || 'explicit_reputation_evidence';
      rec.sourceMode = data.sourceMode || 'explicit_reputation_statement';
      rec.score = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.reputationScore(rec);
      rec.evidence.push({ key: ledger.key, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, axis: axis, delta: delta, phrase: proposal.textPreview || data.note || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.evidence, 12);
      social.reputation.scopesById[scopeId] = rec;
      if (social.reputation.scopeIds.indexOf(scopeId) < 0) social.reputation.scopeIds.push(scopeId);
      social.reputation.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, scopeId: scopeId, scopeName: rec.scopeName, axis: axis, delta: delta });
      social.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'reputation_evidence', scopeId: scopeId, axis: axis, delta: delta });
      WL.U.capArray(social.reputation.scopeIds, 100); WL.U.capArray(social.reputation.recent, 50); WL.U.capArray(social.recent, 80);
      if (scopeType === 'actor') {
        var actor = WL.ActorSocialSystem.ensureActor(root, rec.scopeName);
        actor.opinions = actor.opinions || [];
        actor.opinions.push({ key: ledger.key, axis: axis, delta: delta, scope: rec.visibilityScope, evidence: proposal.textPreview || data.note || '' });
        WL.U.capArray(actor.opinions, 30);
      }
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.node === 'function') {
        var pNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'player', 'player', root.player.name || 'Player', { weight: 5, tags: ['player'] });
        var rNode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'reputation', scopeId, rec.scopeName, { weight: 2, tags: ['reputation', rec.scopeType, rec.visibilityScope], summary: axis + ' ' + (delta > 0 ? '+' : '') + delta });
        if (pNode && rNode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, pNode.id, rNode.id, 'standing_in', Math.max(1, Math.abs(delta)));
      }
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'social', type: 'reputation_evidence', scopeId: scopeId, axis: axis, delta: delta, key: ledger.key };
    },
    reputationSummary: function (root) {
      WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.reputation.scopeIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Reputation: (none)'];
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 6); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.reputation.scopesById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var axes = rec.axes || {};
        var keys = Object.keys(axes).sort();
        var bits = [];
        for (var k = 0; k < keys.length && k < 5; k++) bits.push(keys[k] + ' ' + (axes[keys[k]] > 0 ? '+' : '') + axes[keys[k]]);
        lines.push((rec.scopeName || rec.id) + ' [' + (rec.scopeType || 'scope') + '/' + (rec.visibilityScope || 'scoped') + ']: ' + (bits.join(', ') || 'standing 0'));
      }
      return lines;
    },
    rumorSummary: function (root) {
      return WL.RumorPropagationSystem.summary(root);
    },
    witnessSummary: function (root) {
      WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.witnesses.recordIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Witnesses: (none)'];
      var lines = [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 5); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.witnesses.recordsById[ids[i]];
        if (!rec) continue;
        var names = (rec.witnesses || []).map(function (w) { return w.name; }).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 4).join(', ');
        lines.push((rec.witnessed ? 'Witnessed' : 'Unwitnessed/unknown') + ' [' + (rec.visibility || 'unknown') + ']: ' + (rec.eventKind || 'event') + (names ? ' by ' + names : '') + ' — ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.eventSummary).slice(0, 90));
      }
      return lines;
    },
    presenceSummary: function (root) {
      WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var lines = [];
      var ids = root.social.presence.actorIds || [];
      var actors = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && actors.length < 8; i++) { var rec = root.social.presence.actorsById[ids[i]]; if (rec) actors.push(rec.actorName + ' [' + (rec.mode || 'present') + ']'); }
      var gids = root.social.presence.genericGroupIds || [];
      var groups = [];
      for (var g = 0; g < gids.length && groups.length < 6; g++) { var gr = root.social.presence.genericGroupsById[gids[g]]; if (gr) groups.push(gr.label + (gr.count ? ' x' + gr.count : '') + ' [' + (gr.mode || 'present') + ']'); }
      lines.push('Named present: ' + (actors.join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Generic groups: ' + (groups.join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Hidden observer candidates: ' + ((root.social.presence.hiddenObserverCandidates || []).length));
      return lines;
    },
    knowledgeSummary: function (root) {
      WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.ensure(root);
      var ids = root.social.knowledge.factIds || [];
      var lines = [];
      if (!ids.length) return ['Knowledge: (none)'];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, ids.length - 8); i < ids.length; i++) {
        var rec = root.social.knowledge.factsById[ids[i]];
        if (rec) lines.push((rec.holderName || rec.holderId) + ' knows [' + (rec.visibilityScope || 'private') + '/' + (rec.truthStatus || 'alleged') + ']: ' + rec.fact);
      }
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.TimeTravelReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.TimeTravelSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.WorldStructureReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.WorldStructureSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.ActorSocialReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.ActorSocialSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.SocialConsequenceReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };


  WL.CombatThreatSystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'threat_profile_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordThreat(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'group_count_candidate' || p.type === 'reinforcement_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordGroup(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'casualty_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordCasualty(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'all_defeated_claim') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.resolveGroupClaim(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'combat_reward_candidate') return WL.CombatThreatSystem.recordRewardEstimate(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'combat', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_combat_event' };
    },
    tierForLevel: function (level) {
      if (level <= 1) return 0;
      if (level <= 5) return 1;
      if (level <= 10) return 2;
      if (level <= 20) return 3;
      if (level <= 35) return 4;
      if (level <= 55) return 5;
      if (level <= 80) return 6;
      if (level <= 120) return 7;
      if (level <= 180) return 8;
      if (level <= 260) return 9;
      if (level <= 375) return 10;
      if (level <= 525) return 11;
      if (level <= 700) return 12;
      if (level <= 850) return 13;
      if (level <= 1000) return 14;
      return 15;
    },
    dangerLabel: function (level, playerLevel) {
      var gap = (level || 1) - (playerLevel || 1);
      if (gap >= 21) return 'impossible_by_direct_means_without_special_leverage';
      if (gap >= 11) return 'nearly_impossible_without_leverage';
      if (gap >= 6) return 'overwhelming';
      if (gap >= 3) return 'dangerous';
      if (gap >= 1) return 'challenging';
      if (gap <= -20) return 'trivial_for_xp';
      return 'even_or_manageable';
    },
    overlevelModifier: function (playerLevel, challengeLevel) {
      var gap = (playerLevel || 1) - (challengeLevel || 1);
      if (gap >= 20) return 0;
      if (gap >= 15) return 0.05;
      if (gap >= 10) return 0.10;
      if (gap >= 7) return 0.20;
      if (gap >= 5) return 0.35;
      if (gap >= 3) return 0.60;
      if (gap >= 2) return 0.75;
      if (gap >= 1) return 0.90;
      return 1;
    },
    challengeXpEstimate: function (level, playerLevel, contribution) {
      var base = 20 * Math.max(1, Number(level) || 1);
      var over = WL.CombatThreatSystem.overlevelModifier(playerLevel || 1, level || 1);
      var underGap = (level || 1) - (playerLevel || 1);
      var difficulty = underGap >= 20 ? 10 : underGap >= 15 ? 7 : underGap >= 10 ? 4.5 : underGap >= 7 ? 3 : underGap >= 5 ? 2.2 : underGap >= 3 ? 1.6 : underGap >= 2 ? 1.35 : underGap >= 1 ? 1.15 : 1;
      var contrib = contribution == null ? 1 : contribution;
      return Math.round(base * difficulty * over * contrib);
    },
    estimateThreat: function (label, context) {
      var l = WL.U.lower(label);
      var ctx = context || {};
      var level = 5;
      var stats = { atk: 5, def: 5, spd: 5, int: 3, lck: 3 };
      var tags = [];
      if (/rat/.test(l)) { level = 1; stats = { atk: 1, def: 1, spd: 4, int: 1, lck: 2 }; tags.push('small'); }
      if (/slime/.test(l)) { level = 2; stats = { atk: 2, def: 5, spd: 2, int: 1, lck: 3 }; tags.push('amorphous'); }
      if (/goblin/.test(l)) { level = 3; stats = { atk: 4, def: 3, spd: 5, int: 3, lck: 3 }; tags.push('small_group_threat'); }
      if (/wolf/.test(l)) { level = 5; stats = { atk: 8, def: 5, spd: 10, int: 2, lck: 4 }; tags.push('predator'); }
      if (/soldier/.test(l)) { level = 8; stats = { atk: 10, def: 9, spd: 7, int: 5, lck: 4 }; tags.push('armed_human'); }
      if (/village guard/.test(l) || (/guard/.test(l) && !/city|elite|captain|veteran/.test(l))) { level = 25; stats = { atk: 28, def: 30, spd: 18, int: 10, lck: 8 }; tags.push('guard_baseline'); }
      if (/city guard|large city guard/.test(l)) { level = 50; stats = { atk: 55, def: 60, spd: 35, int: 20, lck: 15 }; tags.push('large_city_guard'); }
      if (/(elite|veteran|captain).*guard|guard.*(elite|veteran|captain)/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, /captain/.test(l) ? 75 : /veteran/.test(l) ? 70 : 65); stats = { atk: 78, def: 80, spd: 50, int: 32, lck: 20 }; tags.push('elite_guard'); }
      if (/ogre|troll/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 28); stats = { atk: 42, def: 36, spd: 16, int: 6, lck: 6 }; tags.push('large_monster'); }
      if (/wyvern/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 65); stats = { atk: 78, def: 62, spd: 92, int: 18, lck: 22 }; tags.push('wyvern', 'aerial_predator'); }
      else if (/drake/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 85); stats = { atk: 105, def: 95, spd: 62, int: 35, lck: 25 }; tags.push('drake', 'lesser_dragonkin'); }
      else if (/young dragon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 90); stats = { atk: 120, def: 110, spd: 78, int: 65, lck: 30 }; tags.push('young_dragon'); }
      else if (/adult dragon|dragon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, /adult/.test(l) ? 180 : 120); stats = { atk: 180, def: 160, spd: 100, int: 110, lck: 45 }; tags.push('dragon'); }
      if (/ancient|elder|legendary/.test(l) && /dragon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, 600); stats.atk += 180; stats.def += 160; stats.spd += 80; stats.int += 140; stats.lck += 60; tags.push('ancient_dragon_tier'); }
      else if (/ancient|legendary/.test(l)) { level += 40; stats.atk += 45; stats.def += 45; stats.spd += 25; stats.int += 35; tags.push('legendary'); }
      if (/inferno|fire|flame/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level + 20, 25); stats.atk += 20; stats.def += 8; stats.spd += 5; stats.int += 4; tags.push('elemental_fire'); }
      if (/demon/.test(l)) { level = Math.max(level, /greater|lord|true/.test(l) ? 140 : /lesser/.test(l) ? 35 : 60); stats = { atk: Math.max(stats.atk, 80), def: Math.max(stats.def, 60), spd: Math.max(stats.spd, 55), int: Math.max(stats.int, 45), lck: Math.max(stats.lck, 20) }; tags.push('demonic'); }
      if (ctx.groupWord) { level += /horde|swarm/.test(ctx.groupWord) ? 10 : /pack|band|group/.test(ctx.groupWord) ? 5 : 0; stats.atk += 5; stats.spd += 3; tags.push('group_pressure_' + ctx.groupWord); }
      if (ctx.pressure && ctx.pressure.territory) { level += 8; stats.def += 6; stats.atk += 4; tags.push('territory_advantage'); }
      if (ctx.pressure && ctx.pressure.ambush) { level += 5; stats.spd += 5; stats.lck += 3; tags.push('ambush_pressure'); }
      var playerLevel = (ctx.playerLevel || 1);
      return { level: level, stats: stats, tags: tags, tier: WL.CombatThreatSystem.tierForLevel(level), danger: WL.CombatThreatSystem.dangerLabel(level, playerLevel), xpEstimateForPlayerLevel: WL.CombatThreatSystem.challengeXpEstimate(level, playerLevel, 1), confidence: 'inferred_from_description', noPlayerRubberBand: true };
    },
    recordThreat: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.creatureName || 'Unknown Threat';
      var id = proposal.data.threatId || ('threat_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      var ctx = { groupWord: proposal.data.groupWord || '', pressure: proposal.data.pressure || {}, playerLevel: root.progression && root.progression.level || 1 };
      var est = WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat(name, ctx);
      var rec = root.combat.threatProfilesById[id];
      if (!rec) {
        rec = { id: id, name: name, level: est.level, tier: est.tier, danger: est.danger, xpEstimateForPlayerLevel: est.xpEstimateForPlayerLevel, stats: est.stats, tags: est.tags, confidence: est.confidence, history: [], noPlayerRubberBand: true };
        root.combat.threatProfilesById[id] = rec;
        root.combat.threatProfileIds.push(id);
      } else {
        rec.level = Math.max(rec.level || 1, est.level || 1);
        rec.tier = Math.max(rec.tier || 0, est.tier || 0);
        rec.danger = est.danger;
        rec.xpEstimateForPlayerLevel = est.xpEstimateForPlayerLevel;
        rec.tags = WL.U.unique((rec.tags || []).concat(est.tags || []));
      }
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: proposal.textPreview, tags: est.tags, danger: est.danger });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 20);
      root.combat.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'threat_profile', id: id, name: name, level: rec.level, tier: rec.tier });
      WL.U.capArray(root.combat.recent, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'threat_profile', threatId: id, level: rec.level, tier: rec.tier, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordRewardEstimate: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.creatureName || 'Unknown Threat';
      var id = proposal.data.threatId || ('threat_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      var threat = root.combat.threatProfilesById[id];
      var est = threat || WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat(name, { playerLevel: root.progression && root.progression.level || 1 });
      var playerLevel = root.progression && root.progression.level || 1;
      var xp = WL.CombatThreatSystem.challengeXpEstimate(est.level || 1, playerLevel, 1);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, threatId: id, name: name, level: est.level || 1, playerLevel: playerLevel, xpEstimate: xp, danger: WL.CombatThreatSystem.dangerLabel(est.level || 1, playerLevel), noRubberBand: true, phrase: proposal.textPreview };
      root.combat.rewardEstimates.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.combat.rewardEstimates, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'combat_reward_estimate', threatId: id, xpEstimate: xp, key: ledger.key };
    },
    recordGroup: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var name = proposal.data.groupName || 'Unknown Group';
      var id = proposal.data.groupId || ('group_' + WL.U.slug(name));
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      if (!group) {
        group = { id: id, name: name, total: proposal.data.count || 0, remaining: proposal.data.count || 0, casualties: 0, groupWord: proposal.data.groupWord || '', pressure: proposal.data.pressure || {}, history: [] };
        root.combat.groupCountsById[id] = group;
        root.combat.groupIds.push(id);
      } else if (proposal.type === 'reinforcement_candidate') {
        group.total += proposal.data.count || 0;
        group.remaining += proposal.data.count || 0;
      } else if (!group.total) {
        group.total = proposal.data.count || 0;
        group.remaining = proposal.data.count || 0;
      }
      root.combat.activeCombatId = root.combat.activeCombatId || ('combat_' + WL.U.hash(id));
      if (proposal.data.groupWord) group.groupWord = proposal.data.groupWord;
      if (proposal.data.pressure) group.pressure = proposal.data.pressure;
      group.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: proposal.type === 'reinforcement_candidate' ? 'reinforcement' : 'count', count: proposal.data.count, phrase: proposal.textPreview, estimated: proposal.data.estimated === true, groupWord: proposal.data.groupWord || group.groupWord || '' });
      WL.U.capArray(group.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'group_count', groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    findGroupId: function (root, name) {
      var id = 'group_' + WL.U.slug(name || '');
      if (root.combat.groupCountsById[id]) return id;
      var singular = 'group_' + WL.U.slug(WL.EventScanner.singularCreature(name || ''));
      if (root.combat.groupCountsById[singular]) return singular;
      var keys = Object.keys(root.combat.groupCountsById);
      var slug = WL.U.slug(name || '');
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) if (keys[i].indexOf(slug) >= 0 || slug.indexOf(keys[i].replace(/^group_/, '')) >= 0) return keys[i];
      return id;
    },
    recordCasualty: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = WL.CombatThreatSystem.findGroupId(root, proposal.data.groupName);
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      if (!group) {
        group = { id: id, name: proposal.data.groupName || 'Unknown Group', total: 0, remaining: 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.combat.groupCountsById[id] = group;
        root.combat.groupIds.push(id);
      }
      var n = WL.U.clamp(proposal.data.casualties || 0, 0, 999999);
      group.casualties += n;
      group.remaining = Math.max(0, (group.remaining || 0) - n);
      group.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'casualty', casualties: n, remaining: group.remaining, phrase: proposal.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(group.history, 30);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'casualty', groupId: id, casualties: n, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    resolveGroupClaim: function (root, proposal, ledger) {
      var id = WL.CombatThreatSystem.findGroupId(root, proposal.data.groupName);
      var group = root.combat.groupCountsById[id];
      var resolution = WL.U.lower(proposal.data.resolution || 'defeated');
      if (group && group.remaining > 0 && !/flee|surrender|dead|killed|slain/.test(resolution)) {
        var block = { key: ledger.key, groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, claim: resolution, phrase: proposal.textPreview, reason: 'remaining_count_blocks_total_defeat' };
        root.combat.blockedClaims.push(block);
        WL.U.capArray(root.combat.blockedClaims, 30);
        return { status: 'BLOCKED_CLAIM', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'all_defeated_blocked', groupId: id, remaining: group.remaining, key: ledger.key };
      }
      if (group && /flee|surrender|dead|killed|slain/.test(resolution)) group.remaining = 0;
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'combat', type: 'group_resolved', groupId: id, remaining: group ? group.remaining : 0, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = ['No-rubber-band scaling: ' + (root.combat.noRubberBandScaling === true ? 'true' : 'false')];
      root.combat.threatProfileIds.forEach(function (id) { var t = root.combat.threatProfilesById[id]; if (t) lines.push(t.name + ' L' + t.level + ' ATK ' + t.stats.atk + ' DEF ' + t.stats.def + ' SPD ' + t.stats.spd); });
      root.combat.groupIds.forEach(function (id) { var g = root.combat.groupCountsById[id]; if (g) lines.push(g.name + ': ' + g.remaining + '/' + g.total + ' remaining'); });
      if (root.combat.blockedClaims.length) lines.push('Blocked claims: ' + root.combat.blockedClaims.length);
      return lines.length ? lines : ['(none)'];
    }
  };


  WL.ContractSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.contracts || typeof root.contracts !== 'object') root.contracts = WL.Schema.root().contracts;
      if (!root.contracts.contractsById || typeof root.contracts.contractsById !== 'object') root.contracts.contractsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.contracts.contractIds)) root.contracts.contractIds = [];
      if (!root.contracts.byParty || typeof root.contracts.byParty !== 'object') root.contracts.byParty = {};
      if (!root.contracts.byStatus || typeof root.contracts.byStatus !== 'object') root.contracts.byStatus = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.contracts.payments)) root.contracts.payments = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.contracts.breaches)) root.contracts.breaches = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.contracts.recent)) root.contracts.recent = [];
      return root.contracts;
    },
    addIndex: function (root, rec) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      if (c.contractIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) c.contractIds.push(rec.id);
      var partyKey = WL.U.slug(rec.partyB || rec.actorName || 'unknown');
      if (!c.byParty[partyKey]) c.byParty[partyKey] = [];
      if (c.byParty[partyKey].indexOf(rec.id) < 0) c.byParty[partyKey].push(rec.id);
      var st = rec.status || 'unknown';
      if (!c.byStatus[st]) c.byStatus[st] = [];
      if (c.byStatus[st].indexOf(rec.id) < 0) c.byStatus[st].push(rec.id);
      Object.keys(c.byStatus).forEach(function (k) { if (k !== st) c.byStatus[k] = (c.byStatus[k] || []).filter(function (id) { return id !== rec.id; }); });
      WL.U.capArray(c.contractIds, 120);
    },
    findLikelyContract: function (root, actorName, reason) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      var actorSlug = WL.U.slug(actorName || '');
      var ids = c.byParty[actorSlug] || [];
      for (var i = ids.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var rec = c.contractsById[ids[i]];
        if (rec && !/completed|terminated/.test(rec.status || '')) return rec;
      }
      var keys = c.contractIds || [];
      for (var j = keys.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        var r = c.contractsById[keys[j]];
        if (r && WL.U.slug(r.partyB) === actorSlug && !/completed|terminated/.test(r.status || '')) return r;
      }
      return null;
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'contract_lifecycle_candidate') return WL.ContractSystem.lifecycle(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'contract_status_candidate') return WL.ContractSystem.status(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'contract_payment_candidate') return WL.ContractSystem.payment(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'contract', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_contract_event' };
    },
    lifecycle: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = p.data || {};
      var id = d.contractId || d.targetId || ('contract_' + WL.U.slug((d.partyB || 'unknown') + '_' + (d.work || 'work')));
      var rec = c.contractsById[id] || { id: id, history: [], payments: [], obligations: [] };
      rec.id = id;
      rec.partyA = d.partyA || rec.partyA || 'Player';
      rec.partyB = d.partyB || rec.partyB || d.actorName || 'Unknown Worker';
      rec.partyBId = d.partyBId || rec.partyBId || ('actor_' + WL.U.slug(rec.partyB));
      rec.work = d.work || rec.work || 'unspecified work';
      rec.role = d.role || rec.role || WL.EventScanner.inferLaborRole(rec.work);
      rec.status = d.status || rec.status || 'active';
      rec.payment = d.payment || rec.payment || null;
      rec.deadline = d.deadline || rec.deadline || '';
      rec.obligations = rec.obligations || [];
      if (rec.obligations.indexOf(rec.work) < 0) rec.obligations.push(rec.work);
      rec.truthStatus = rec.status === 'promised' ? 'terms_promised_not_paid' : 'confirmed_contract_record';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, status: rec.status, phrase: p.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 30);
      c.contractsById[id] = rec;
      WL.ContractSystem.addIndex(root, rec);
      c.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, contractId: id, type: 'lifecycle', status: rec.status });
      WL.U.capArray(c.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'contract', type: 'contract_lifecycle', contractId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    status: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = WL.ContractSystem.findLikelyContract(root, d.actorName || d.partyB || '', d.reason || '') || { id: 'contract_' + WL.U.slug((d.actorName || 'unknown') + '_unspecified_work'), history: [], payments: [], obligations: [], partyA: 'Player', partyB: d.actorName || 'Unknown Worker', partyBId: d.actorId || '' };
      rec.status = d.status || 'status_recorded';
      rec.reason = d.reason || rec.reason || '';
      rec.lastTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn;
      rec.history = rec.history || [];
      rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, status: rec.status, reason: rec.reason, phrase: p.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 30);
      c.contractsById[rec.id] = rec;
      WL.ContractSystem.addIndex(root, rec);
      if (rec.status === 'breached') c.breaches.push({ key: ledger.key, contractId: rec.id, actorName: rec.partyB, reason: rec.reason, phrase: p.textPreview });
      WL.U.capArray(c.breaches, 40);
      c.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, contractId: rec.id, type: 'status', status: rec.status });
      WL.U.capArray(c.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'contract', type: 'contract_status', contractId: rec.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    payment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = WL.ContractSystem.findLikelyContract(root, d.actorName || '', d.reason || '');
      var cid = rec ? rec.id : ('contract_' + WL.U.slug((d.actorName || 'unknown') + '_' + (d.reason || 'work')));
      var pay = { key: ledger.key, contractId: cid, actorName: d.actorName || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'silver', reason: d.reason || '', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      c.payments.push(pay);
      WL.U.capArray(c.payments, 80);
      if (rec) { rec.payments = rec.payments || []; rec.payments.push(pay); WL.U.capArray(rec.payments, 40); rec.lastPaymentTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn; c.contractsById[rec.id] = rec; }
      c.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, contractId: cid, type: 'payment', amount: pay.amount, currency: pay.currency });
      WL.U.capArray(c.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'contract', type: 'contract_payment', contractId: cid, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var c = WL.ContractSystem.ensure(root);
      var lines = ['Contracts: ' + c.contractIds.length + ' | Payments: ' + c.payments.length + ' | Breaches: ' + c.breaches.length];
      var ids = c.contractIds || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && i < 8; i++) {
        var r = c.contractsById[ids[i]];
        if (r) lines.push('- [' + (r.status || 'unknown') + '] ' + (r.partyB || 'Unknown') + ': ' + (r.work || 'unspecified work') + (r.payment ? ' for ' + r.payment.amount + ' ' + r.payment.currency + '/' + r.payment.interval : ''));
      }
      if (ids.length > 8) lines.push('- ... ' + (ids.length - 8) + ' more contract records kept in state.');
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.LaborSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.labor || typeof root.labor !== 'object') root.labor = WL.Schema.root().labor;
      if (!root.labor.employeesById || typeof root.labor.employeesById !== 'object') root.labor.employeesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.labor.employeeIds)) root.labor.employeeIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.labor.assignments)) root.labor.assignments = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.labor.refusals)) root.labor.refusals = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.labor.recent)) root.labor.recent = [];
      return root.labor;
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'labor_employee_candidate') return WL.LaborSystem.employee(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'labor_assignment_candidate') return WL.LaborSystem.assignment(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'labor_refusal_candidate') return WL.LaborSystem.refusal(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'labor', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_labor_event' };
    },
    employee: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var l = WL.LaborSystem.ensure(root);
      var d = p.data || {};
      var id = d.targetId || ('employee_' + WL.U.slug(d.actorName || 'unknown'));
      var e = l.employeesById[id] || { id: id, history: [] };
      e.id = id; e.actorName = d.actorName || e.actorName || 'Unknown Worker'; e.actorId = d.actorId || e.actorId || ('actor_' + WL.U.slug(e.actorName));
      e.role = d.role || e.role || 'Worker'; e.wage = d.wage || e.wage || null; e.contractId = d.contractId || e.contractId || '';
      e.assignment = d.assignment || e.assignment || ''; e.status = d.status || e.status || 'employed'; e.availability = e.status === 'employed' ? 'assigned_or_available' : 'unknown'; e.loyalty = e.loyalty || 'unknown';
      e.history.push({ key: ledger.key, status: e.status, assignment: e.assignment, phrase: p.textPreview }); WL.U.capArray(e.history, 30);
      l.employeesById[id] = e; if (l.employeeIds.indexOf(id) < 0) l.employeeIds.push(id); WL.U.capArray(l.employeeIds, 120);
      if (e.assignment) l.assignments.push({ key: ledger.key, employeeId: id, actorName: e.actorName, contractId: e.contractId, assignment: e.assignment, status: 'assigned_by_contract' });
      WL.U.capArray(l.assignments, 80);
      l.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, employeeId: id, type: 'employee', status: e.status }); WL.U.capArray(l.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'labor', type: 'employee_record', employeeId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    assignment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var l = WL.LaborSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {}; var id = d.employeeId || d.actorId || ('employee_' + WL.U.slug(d.actorName || 'unknown'));
      l.assignments.push({ key: ledger.key, employeeId: id, actorName: d.actorName || '', contractId: d.contractId || '', assignment: d.assignment || '', status: d.status || 'assignment_evidence' }); WL.U.capArray(l.assignments, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'labor', type: 'labor_assignment', employeeId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    refusal: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var l = WL.LaborSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {}; var id = d.targetId || ('labor_refusal_' + WL.U.slug(d.actorName || 'unknown'));
      l.refusals.push({ key: ledger.key, id: id, actorName: d.actorName || '', actorId: d.actorId || '', reason: d.reason || 'refusal', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn }); WL.U.capArray(l.refusals, 60);
      l.recent.push({ key: ledger.key, type: 'refusal', actorName: d.actorName || '', reason: d.reason || '' }); WL.U.capArray(l.recent, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'labor', type: 'labor_refusal', key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var l = WL.LaborSystem.ensure(root);
      var lines = ['Employees: ' + l.employeeIds.length + ' | Assignments: ' + l.assignments.length + ' | Refusals: ' + l.refusals.length];
      for (var i = 0; i < l.employeeIds.length && i < 8; i++) { var e = l.employeesById[l.employeeIds[i]]; if (e) lines.push('- [' + (e.status || 'unknown') + '] ' + (e.actorName || 'Unknown') + ': ' + (e.role || 'Worker') + (e.assignment ? ' -> ' + e.assignment : '')); }
      if (l.employeeIds.length > 8) lines.push('- ... ' + (l.employeeIds.length - 8) + ' more worker records kept in state.');
      if (l.refusals.length) lines.push('Recent refusal: ' + (l.refusals[l.refusals.length - 1].actorName || 'Unknown') + ' — ' + (l.refusals[l.refusals.length - 1].reason || 'refused'));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.ContractLifecycleReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.ContractSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.LaborSystemReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.LaborSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };


  WL.DomainProjectSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.advanced) root.advanced = WL.Schema.root().advanced;
      if (!root.advanced.domain || typeof root.advanced.domain !== 'object') root.advanced.domain = { projectsById: {}, projectIds: [], claims: [], assignments: [], progressLedgers: [], requirementLedgers: [], materialLedgers: [], timeLedgers: [], blockedLedgers: [], completionLedgers: [], readyAssetIds: [] };
      var d = root.advanced.domain;
      if (!d.projectsById || typeof d.projectsById !== 'object') d.projectsById = {};
      ['projectIds','claims','assignments','progressLedgers','requirementLedgers','materialLedgers','timeLedgers','blockedLedgers','completionLedgers','readyAssetIds'].forEach(function (k) { if (!Array.isArray(d[k])) d[k] = []; });
      return d;
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'domain_project_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.project(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_requirement_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.requirement(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_progress_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.progress(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_assignment_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.assignment(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_completion_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.completion(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'domain_blocked_candidate') return WL.DomainProjectSystem.blocked(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'advanced', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_domain_project_event' };
    },
    cleanProjectName: function (name) { return WL.EventScanner.cleanAdvancedName(name || '').replace(/\s+(?:project|construction)$/i, '').trim(); },
    projectId: function (name) { return 'project_' + WL.U.slug(WL.DomainProjectSystem.cleanProjectName(name)); },
    ensureProject: function (root, d) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root);
      var name = WL.DomainProjectSystem.cleanProjectName(d.projectName || d.name || 'Unknown Project') || 'Unknown Project';
      var id = d.projectId || WL.DomainProjectSystem.projectId(name);
      if (!bucket.projectsById[id]) {
        bucket.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: name, status: 'planned_or_started', progressPercent: 0, requirements: { materials: [], time: null, notes: [] }, consumedMaterials: [], timeSpent: [], workers: [], assignments: [], progressNotes: [], blocked: [], completionEvidence: [], history: [], assetConversionStatus: 'not_ready' };
        if (bucket.projectIds.indexOf(id) < 0) bucket.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      var proj = bucket.projectsById[id];
      proj.name = proj.name || name;
      ['consumedMaterials','timeSpent','workers','assignments','progressNotes','blocked','completionEvidence','history'].forEach(function (k) { if (!Array.isArray(proj[k])) proj[k] = []; });
      if (!proj.requirements || typeof proj.requirements !== 'object') proj.requirements = { materials: [], time: null, notes: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(proj.requirements.materials)) proj.requirements.materials = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(proj.requirements.notes)) proj.requirements.notes = [];
      return proj;
    },
    addMaterials: function (list, mats, key, mode) { mats = Array.isArray(mats) ? mats : []; for (var i = 0; i < mats.length; i++) if (mats[i] && mats[i].name) list.push({ key: key, name: mats[i].name, quantity: mats[i].quantity || 1, mode: mode || 'recorded' }); },
    addTime: function (list, time, key, mode) { if (time && time.amount > 0) list.push({ key: key, amount: time.amount, unit: time.unit || 'day', mode: mode || 'recorded' }); },
    project: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      proj.status = proj.progressPercent > 0 ? 'in_progress' : 'started';
      proj.history.push({ key: ledger.key, type: p.type, phrase: p.textPreview });
      if (p.data.materials && p.data.materials.length) { WL.DomainProjectSystem.addMaterials(proj.requirements.materials, p.data.materials, ledger.key, 'initial_requirement_or_allocation'); bucket.requirementLedgers.push({ key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, materials: p.data.materials, phrase: p.textPreview }); }
      if (p.data.time) { proj.requirements.time = p.data.time; bucket.timeLedgers.push({ key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, time: p.data.time, mode: 'estimated_requirement' }); }
      WL.U.capArray(proj.history, 30); WL.U.capArray(bucket.requirementLedgers, 80); WL.U.capArray(bucket.timeLedgers, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_project', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    requirement: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      proj.status = proj.status || 'planned_or_started';
      WL.DomainProjectSystem.addMaterials(proj.requirements.materials, p.data.materials || [], ledger.key, 'required');
      if (p.data.time) proj.requirements.time = p.data.time;
      if (p.data.rawRequirements) proj.requirements.notes.push({ key: ledger.key, note: p.data.rawRequirements });
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, materials: p.data.materials || [], time: p.data.time || null, phrase: p.textPreview };
      bucket.requirementLedgers.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(proj.requirements.notes, 20); WL.U.capArray(bucket.requirementLedgers, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_requirement', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    progress: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      var pct = WL.U.clamp(p.data.progressPercent || 0, 0, 100);
      proj.progressPercent = Math.max(proj.progressPercent || 0, pct);
      proj.status = proj.progressPercent >= 100 ? 'completion_evidence_pending_asset_conversion' : 'in_progress';
      WL.DomainProjectSystem.addMaterials(proj.consumedMaterials, p.data.materials || [], ledger.key, 'consumed'); WL.DomainProjectSystem.addTime(proj.timeSpent, p.data.time, ledger.key, 'spent');
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, progressPercent: proj.progressPercent, materials: p.data.materials || [], time: p.data.time || null, phrase: p.textPreview };
      proj.progressNotes.push(rec); bucket.progressLedgers.push(rec);
      if (p.data.materials && p.data.materials.length) bucket.materialLedgers.push({ key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, materials: p.data.materials, mode: 'consumed' });
      if (p.data.time) bucket.timeLedgers.push({ key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, time: p.data.time, mode: 'spent' });
      WL.U.capArray(proj.progressNotes, 40); WL.U.capArray(bucket.progressLedgers, 80); WL.U.capArray(bucket.materialLedgers, 80); WL.U.capArray(bucket.timeLedgers, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_progress', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    assignment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      var actorName = p.data.actorName || ''; var actorId = p.data.actorId || (actorName ? 'actor_' + WL.U.slug(actorName) : '');
      if (actorName && proj.workers.indexOf(actorName) < 0) proj.workers.push(actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, actorId: actorId, actorName: actorName, phrase: p.textPreview };
      proj.assignments.push(rec); bucket.assignments.push(rec);
      if (root.labor && actorId && root.labor.employeesById && root.labor.employeesById[actorId]) root.labor.employeesById[actorId].assignment = proj.name;
      WL.U.capArray(proj.assignments, 40); WL.U.capArray(bucket.assignments, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_assignment', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    completion: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      proj.progressPercent = Math.max(proj.progressPercent || 0, 100); proj.status = 'completion_evidence_pending_asset_conversion'; proj.assetConversionStatus = 'pending_9_6c_asset_validation';
      WL.DomainProjectSystem.addMaterials(proj.consumedMaterials, p.data.materials || [], ledger.key, 'completion_consumed'); WL.DomainProjectSystem.addTime(proj.timeSpent, p.data.time, ledger.key, 'completion_time');
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, phrase: p.textPreview, materials: p.data.materials || [], time: p.data.time || null };
      proj.completionEvidence.push(rec); bucket.completionLedgers.push(rec); if (bucket.readyAssetIds.indexOf(proj.id) < 0) bucket.readyAssetIds.push(proj.id);
      WL.U.capArray(proj.completionEvidence, 20); WL.U.capArray(bucket.completionLedgers, 80); WL.U.capArray(bucket.readyAssetIds, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_completion_evidence', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    blocked: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var bucket = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var proj = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensureProject(root, p.data || {});
      proj.status = 'blocked'; var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: proj.id, reason: p.data.reason || 'unspecified blockage', phrase: p.textPreview };
      proj.blocked.push(rec); bucket.blockedLedgers.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(proj.blocked, 20); WL.U.capArray(bucket.blockedLedgers, 80);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_blocked', projectId: proj.id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var d = WL.DomainProjectSystem.ensure(root); var lines = [];
      lines.push('Domain Projects: ' + d.projectIds.length);
      if (!d.projectIds.length) { lines.push('Active: (none)'); return lines; }
      var shown = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < d.projectIds.length && i < 4; i++) { var p = d.projectsById[d.projectIds[i]]; if (p) shown.push(p.name + ' [' + (p.status || 'unknown') + ' ' + (p.progressPercent || 0) + '%]'); }
      lines.push('Active: ' + (shown.length ? shown.join(' | ') : '(none)'));
      lines.push('Requirements: ' + d.requirementLedgers.length + ' / Material ledgers: ' + d.materialLedgers.length + ' / Time ledgers: ' + d.timeLedgers.length);
