      lines.push('Assignments: ' + d.assignments.length + ' / Blocked: ' + d.blockedLedgers.length + ' / Completion evidence: ' + d.completionLedgers.length);
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.DomainProjectReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) { return WL.DomainProjectSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger); };

  WL.EconomyTransactionSystem = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.economy || typeof root.economy !== 'object') root.economy = WL.Schema.root().economy;
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.priceQuotes)) root.economy.priceQuotes = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.serviceQuotes)) root.economy.serviceQuotes = [];
      if (!root.economy.debtsById || typeof root.economy.debtsById !== 'object') root.economy.debtsById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.debtIds)) root.economy.debtIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.failedPurchases)) root.economy.failedPurchases = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.wagePayments)) root.economy.wagePayments = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.servicePayments)) root.economy.servicePayments = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.purchaseDenials)) root.economy.purchaseDenials = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.transactions)) root.economy.transactions = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.economy.recent)) root.economy.recent = [];
      if (!root.advanced) root.advanced = WL.Schema.root().advanced;
      if (!root.advanced.economy || typeof root.advanced.economy !== 'object') root.advanced.economy = { priceQuotes: [], debts: [], failedPurchases: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes)) root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.advanced.economy.debts)) root.advanced.economy.debts = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases)) root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases = [];
      return root.economy;
    },
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'economy_price_quote_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.priceQuote(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_service_quote_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.serviceQuote(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_failed_purchase_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.failedPurchase(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_debt_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.debt(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_wage_payment_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.wagePayment(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_service_payment_candidate') return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.servicePayment(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'economy', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_economy_event' };
    },
    pushTransaction: function (root, rec) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root);
      e.transactions.push(rec); e.recent.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(e.transactions, 120); WL.U.capArray(e.recent, 60);
      return rec;
    },
    priceQuote: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = { id: d.targetId || ('quote_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key)), key: ledger.key, itemName: d.itemName || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'gold', quoteKind: d.quoteKind || 'item', vendorName: d.vendorName || '', status: 'quoted', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      e.priceQuotes.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(e.priceQuotes, 60);
      root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.push({ key: rec.key, itemName: rec.itemName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, phrase: rec.phrase }); WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'price_quote', subject: rec.itemName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: 'quote_only_no_item' });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'price_quote', key: ledger.key };
    },
    serviceQuote: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = { id: d.targetId || ('service_quote_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key)), key: ledger.key, serviceName: d.serviceName || 'service', actorName: d.actorName || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'silver', status: d.status || 'quoted', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      e.serviceQuotes.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(e.serviceQuotes, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'service_quote', subject: rec.serviceName, actorName: rec.actorName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: 'quote_only_no_completion' });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'service_quote', key: ledger.key };
    },
    failedPurchase: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = { id: d.targetId || ('failed_purchase_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key)), key: ledger.key, itemName: d.itemName || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || '', reason: d.reason || 'failed_purchase', status: 'failed', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      e.failedPurchases.push(rec); e.purchaseDenials.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(e.failedPurchases, 60); WL.U.capArray(e.purchaseDenials, 60);
      root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases.push({ key: rec.key, itemName: rec.itemName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, reason: rec.reason }); WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.failedPurchases, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'failed_purchase', subject: rec.itemName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: 'no_item_no_spend' });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'failed_purchase', key: ledger.key };
    },
    debt: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var id = d.targetId || ('debt_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key));
      if (!e.debtsById[id]) { e.debtsById[id] = { id: id, creditor: d.creditor || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'silver', reason: d.reason || 'debt', debtKind: d.debtKind || 'general', status: d.status || 'owed', createdKey: ledger.key, history: [] }; e.debtIds.push(id); }
      var rec = e.debtsById[id]; rec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'debt_recorded', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn }); WL.U.capArray(rec.history, 20);
      root.advanced.economy.debts.push({ key: ledger.key, creditor: rec.creditor, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, paid: false }); WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.debts, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'debt', subject: rec.creditor, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: rec.status, reason: rec.reason });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'debt', debtId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    wagePayment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = { id: d.targetId || ('wage_payment_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key)), key: ledger.key, actorName: d.actorName || '', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'silver', paymentKind: d.paymentKind || 'wage', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      e.wagePayments.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(e.wagePayments, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'wage_payment', subject: rec.actorName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: 'payment_evidence' });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'wage_payment', key: ledger.key };
    },
    servicePayment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var d = p.data || {};
      var rec = { id: d.targetId || ('service_payment_' + WL.U.hash(ledger.key)), key: ledger.key, actorName: d.actorName || '', serviceName: d.serviceName || 'service', amount: Number(d.amount || 0), currency: d.currency || 'silver', status: d.status || 'paid_evidence', phrase: p.textPreview, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      e.servicePayments.push(rec); WL.U.capArray(e.servicePayments, 60);
      WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.pushTransaction(root, { key: ledger.key, type: 'service_payment', subject: rec.serviceName, actorName: rec.actorName, amount: rec.amount, currency: rec.currency, status: 'payment_evidence_not_completion' });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'economy', type: 'service_payment', key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var e = WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.ensure(root); var lines = [];
      lines.push('Economy: quotes ' + e.priceQuotes.length + ', services ' + e.serviceQuotes.length + ', debts ' + e.debtIds.length + ', failed purchases ' + e.failedPurchases.length + ', wages paid ' + e.wagePayments.length);
      var parts = [];
      if (e.priceQuotes.length) { var q = e.priceQuotes[e.priceQuotes.length - 1]; parts.push('last quote ' + q.itemName + ' ' + q.amount + ' ' + q.currency + ' [no item]'); }
      if (e.serviceQuotes.length) { var sq = e.serviceQuotes[e.serviceQuotes.length - 1]; parts.push('service ' + sq.serviceName + ' ' + sq.amount + ' ' + sq.currency); }
      if (e.debtIds.length) { var d = e.debtsById[e.debtIds[e.debtIds.length - 1]]; if (d) parts.push('debt to ' + d.creditor + ' ' + d.amount + ' ' + d.currency); }
      if (e.failedPurchases.length) { var fp = e.failedPurchases[e.failedPurchases.length - 1]; parts.push('failed ' + fp.itemName + ' [no item/spend]'); }
      lines.push('Recent: ' + (parts.length ? parts.join(' | ') : '(none)'));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.EconomyTransactionReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) { return WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger); };

  WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem = {
    apply: function (root, validation, ledger) {
      var p = validation.proposal;
      if (p.type === 'economy_price_quote_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.price(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'economy_debt_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.debt(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'crafting_recipe_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.recipe(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'crafting_material_candidate' || p.type === 'crafting_completion_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.craftingProject(root, p, ledger);
      if (/^domain_/.test(p.type)) return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'advanced', type: p.type, reason: 'domain_project_events_are_owned_by_DomainProjectSystem' };
      if (p.type === 'warfare_force_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.force(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'warfare_casualty_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.forceCasualty(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'warfare_control_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.forceControl(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'absorption_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.absorption(root, p, ledger);
      if (p.type === 'evolution_candidate') return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.evolution(root, p, ledger);
      return { status: 'IGNORED', applied: false, family: 'advanced', type: p.type, reason: 'unsupported_boundary_event' };
    },
    price: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, itemName: p.data.itemName, amount: p.data.amount, currency: p.data.currency, phrase: p.textPreview };
      root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'price_quote', key: ledger.key };
    },
    debt: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, creditor: p.data.creditor, amount: p.data.amount, currency: p.data.currency, paid: false };
      root.advanced.economy.debts.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.economy.debts, 40);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'debt', key: ledger.key };
    },
    recipe: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.recipeId || ('recipe_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.recipeName));
      if (!root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.recipeName, known: true, history: [] };
        root.advanced.crafting.recipeIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.crafting.recipesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'recipe_known', recipeId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    craftingProject: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.projectId || ('craft_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.projectName || 'unknown_craft'));
      if (!root.advanced.crafting.projectsById[id]) {
        root.advanced.crafting.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.projectName || 'Unknown Craft', status: 'in_progress_evidence_only', materials: [], materialLedger: [], completionEvidence: [], outputItemGranted: false };
        root.advanced.crafting.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      var proj = root.advanced.crafting.projectsById[id];
      var mats = p.data.materials || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < mats.length; i++) proj.materials.push({ key: ledger.key, name: mats[i].name, quantity: mats[i].quantity });
      if (p.type === 'crafting_completion_candidate') {
        proj.status = 'completion_evidence_pending_inventory_validation';
        proj.completionEvidence.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      }
      proj.materialLedger.push({ key: ledger.key, materials: mats, type: p.type, phrase: p.textPreview });
      root.advanced.crafting.materialLedgers.push({ key: ledger.key, projectId: id, materials: mats, type: p.type });
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.crafting.materialLedgers, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'crafting_boundary_project', projectId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    project: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.projectId || ('project_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.projectName));
      if (!root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id]) {
        root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.projectName, status: 'started', progressNotes: [] };
        root.advanced.domain.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id].progressNotes.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_project', projectId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    domainProgress: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.projectId || ('project_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.projectName || 'unknown_project'));
      if (!root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id]) {
        root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.projectName || 'Unknown Project', status: 'in_progress', progressNotes: [], progressPercent: 0, workers: [] };
        root.advanced.domain.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      var proj = root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id];
      if (!proj.progressNotes) proj.progressNotes = [];
      if (!proj.workers) proj.workers = [];
      proj.progressPercent = Math.max(proj.progressPercent || 0, WL.U.clamp(p.data.progressPercent || 0, 0, 100));
      if (proj.progressPercent >= 100) proj.status = 'complete_evidence_pending_validation';
      else proj.status = 'in_progress';
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: id, progressPercent: proj.progressPercent, phrase: p.textPreview };
      proj.progressNotes.push(rec);
      root.advanced.domain.progressLedgers.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.advanced.domain.progressLedgers, 60);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_progress', projectId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    domainAssignment: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.projectId || ('project_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.projectName || 'unknown_project'));
      if (!root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id]) {
        root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.projectName || 'Unknown Project', status: 'assignment_only', progressNotes: [], progressPercent: 0, workers: [] };
        root.advanced.domain.projectIds.push(id);
      }
      var proj = root.advanced.domain.projectsById[id];
      if (!proj.progressNotes) proj.progressNotes = [];
      if (!proj.workers) proj.workers = [];
      var actorName = p.data.actorName || '';
      if (actorName && proj.workers.indexOf(actorName) < 0) proj.workers.push(actorName);
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, projectId: id, actorName: actorName, phrase: p.textPreview };
      root.advanced.domain.assignments.push(rec);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'domain_assignment', projectId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    force: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.forceId || ('force_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.forceName));
      if (!root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id] = { id: id, name: p.data.forceName, total: p.data.count || 0, remaining: p.data.count || 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.push(id);
      }
      root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id].history.push({ key: ledger.key, action: 'force_count', count: p.data.count, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'force_count', forceId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    forceCasualty: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.forceId || ('force_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.forceName));
      var f = root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id];
      if (!f) {
        f = { id: id, name: p.data.forceName, total: 0, remaining: 0, casualties: 0, history: [] };
        root.advanced.warfare.forcesById[id] = f;
        root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.push(id);
      }
      var n = WL.U.clamp(p.data.casualties || 0, 0, 999999);
      f.casualties += n;
      f.remaining = Math.max(0, (f.remaining || 0) - n);
      root.advanced.warfare.casualties.push({ key: ledger.key, forceId: id, casualties: n, reason: p.data.reason || '', phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'force_casualty', forceId: id, remaining: f.remaining, key: ledger.key };
    },
    forceControl: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var forceName = p.data.forceName || 'Unknown Force';
      var placeName = p.data.placeName || 'Unknown Place';
      var rec = { key: ledger.key, forceName: forceName, placeName: placeName, truthStatus: 'claim_or_control_evidence', phrase: p.textPreview };
      root.advanced.warfare.controlClaims.push(rec);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'warfare_control_claim', key: ledger.key };
    },
    absorption: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.candidateId || ('absorb_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.sourceName));
      if (!root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id] = { id: id, sourceName: p.data.sourceName, status: 'candidate_evidence_only', bounded: true, progress: 0, thresholdNotes: [], history: [] };
        root.advanced.absorption.candidateIds.push(id);
      }
      var ac = root.advanced.absorption.candidatesById[id];
      ac.progress = Math.min(100, (ac.progress || 0) + (p.data.progress || 1));
      if (p.data.thresholdNote) ac.thresholdNotes.push({ key: ledger.key, note: p.data.thresholdNote });
      ac.history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview, progress: p.data.progress || 1 });
      WL.U.capArray(ac.history, 20);
      if (root.advanced.absorption.sourceUseKeys.indexOf(id) < 0) root.advanced.absorption.sourceUseKeys.push(id);
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'absorption_candidate', candidateId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    evolution: function (root, p, ledger) {
      var id = p.data.candidateId || ('evolve_' + WL.U.slug(p.data.targetForm));
      if (!root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id]) {
        root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id] = { id: id, targetForm: p.data.targetForm, status: 'candidate_evidence_only', bounded: true, progress: 0, history: [] };
        root.advanced.evolution.candidateIds.push(id);
      }
      var ec = root.advanced.evolution.candidatesById[id];
      ec.progress = Math.min(100, (ec.progress || 0) + (p.data.progress || 1));
      ec.history.push({ key: ledger.key, phrase: p.textPreview, progress: p.data.progress || 1 });
      WL.U.capArray(ec.history, 20);
      root.advanced.evolution.thresholdNotes.push({ key: ledger.key, candidateId: id, progress: ec.progress, phrase: p.textPreview });
      return { status: 'APPLIED', applied: true, family: 'advanced', type: 'evolution_candidate', candidateId: id, key: ledger.key };
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Price quotes: ' + root.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.length);
      lines.push('Recipes known: ' + root.advanced.crafting.recipeIds.length);
      lines.push('Domain projects: ' + root.advanced.domain.projectIds.length);
      lines.push('Warfare forces: ' + root.advanced.warfare.forceIds.length);
      lines.push('Crafting material ledgers: ' + root.advanced.crafting.materialLedgers.length);
      lines.push('Domain progress ledgers: ' + root.advanced.domain.progressLedgers.length);
      lines.push('Warfare control claims: ' + root.advanced.warfare.controlClaims.length);
      lines.push('Absorption candidates: ' + root.advanced.absorption.candidateIds.length);
      lines.push('Evolution candidates: ' + root.advanced.evolution.candidateIds.length);
      lines.push('Contracts: ' + ((root.contracts && root.contracts.contractIds || []).length));
      lines.push('Employees: ' + ((root.labor && root.labor.employeeIds || []).length));
      return lines;
    }
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.CombatThreatReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.CombatThreatSystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.ReducerRegistry.handlers.AdvancedBoundaryReducer = function (root, validation, ledger) {
    return WL.AdvancedBoundarySystem.apply(root, validation, ledger);
  };

  WL.DirtyQueue = {
    mark: function (root, reducerResults) {
      var anyApplied = false;
      for (var i = 0; i < reducerResults.length; i++) if (reducerResults[i].applied) anyApplied = true;
      if (anyApplied) {
        root.dirty.targets.push('diagnostics');
        for (var d = 0; d < reducerResults.length; d++) {
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'inventory' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('inventory') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('inventory');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'quest' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('quests') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('quests');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'form' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('body') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('body');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'form' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('profile') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('profile');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'ability' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('abilities') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('abilities');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'progression' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('progression') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('progression');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'time' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('time') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('time');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'place' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('places') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('places');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'actor' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('actors') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('actors');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'social' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('actors') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('actors');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'social' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('places') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('places');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'combat' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('combat') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('combat');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'advanced' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('advanced') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('advanced');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'contract' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('contracts') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('contracts');
          if (reducerResults[d].family === 'labor' && root.dirty.targets.indexOf('labor') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('labor');
        }
        root.dirty.reasons.push('pipeline_or_owner_update');
        WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.targets, 40);
        WL.U.capArray(root.dirty.reasons, 40);
      }
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 08. OperationLedger and Diagnostics
  // ============================================================
  WL.OperationLedger = {
    makeKey: function (parts) { return WL.U.slug((parts || []).join('|')); },
    has: function (root, key) { return !!(root.operationLedger.keysById[WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([key])]); },
    add: function (root, key, reason) {
      var k = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey([key]);
      if (root.operationLedger.keysById[k]) {
        root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips.push({ key: k, reason: reason || 'duplicate', turn: root.runtime.currentTurn });
        WL.U.capArray(root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips, 80);
        return { applied: false, duplicate: true, key: k };
      }
      root.operationLedger.keysById[k] = { key: k, reason: reason || '', turn: root.runtime.currentTurn };
      root.operationLedger.recentKeys.push(k);
      WL.U.capArray(root.operationLedger.recentKeys, WL.Constants.maxRecentKeys);
      return { applied: true, duplicate: false, key: k };
    }
  };

  WL.Diagnostics = {
    row: function (root, subsystem, level, message, extra) {
      var rec = { turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, subsystem: subsystem || 'system', level: level || 'info', message: WL.U.norm(message).slice(0, 240), extra: extra || null };
      root.diagnostics.audit.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.diagnostics.audit, WL.Constants.maxAuditRows);
      return rec;
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 09. ContextBudgeter and Card Mirror Systems
  // ============================================================
  WL.ContextBudgeter = {
    modeCaps: Object.freeze({ small: 1100, medium: 2200, high: 3600 }),
    hardMaxChars: function (infoObj) {
      var maxChars = 0;
      try { if (typeof infoObj === 'object' && infoObj && typeof infoObj.maxChars === 'number' && infoObj.maxChars > 0) maxChars = Math.floor(infoObj.maxChars); } catch (e) {}
      try { if (!maxChars && typeof info === 'object' && info && typeof info.maxChars === 'number' && info.maxChars > 0) maxChars = Math.floor(info.maxChars); } catch (e2) {}
      return maxChars;
    },
    maxPacketChars: function (root, infoObj) {
      var mode = (root && root.features && root.features.contextMode) || 'small';
      if (!WL.ContextBudgeter.modeCaps[mode]) mode = 'small';
      var cap = WL.ContextBudgeter.modeCaps[mode];
      var maxChars = WL.ContextBudgeter.hardMaxChars(infoObj);
      if (maxChars > 0) {
        var share = Math.floor(maxChars * 0.35);
        if (maxChars < 700) share = Math.max(40, Math.floor(maxChars * 0.60));
        cap = Math.min(cap, Math.max(40, share), maxChars);
      }
      return cap;
    },
    clip: function (text, max) {
      var t = WL.U.norm(text);
      max = WL.U.clamp(max || 0, 0, 999999);
      if (!max) return '';
      if (t.length <= max) return t;
      if (max <= 3) return t.slice(0, max);
      return t.slice(0, Math.max(0, max - 3)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '...';
    },
    microPacket: function (root, mode, max) {
      var line = '[WL Context ' + (mode || 'small') + '] HP ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + '; ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.conditionLine(root) + '; place ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root) + '; read-only.';
      return WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(line, max || 120);
    },
    joinNames: function (arr, map, field, max) {
      arr = Array.isArray(arr) ? arr : [];
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < arr.length && out.length < (max || 6); i++) {
        var obj = map ? map[arr[i]] : arr[i];
        var name = obj && (obj[field || 'name'] || obj.displayName || obj.title) || obj;
        name = WL.U.safeString(name);
        if (name) out.push(name);
      }
      return out.length ? out.join(', ') : '(none)';
    },
    currentPlaceName: function (root) {
      var id = root.places && root.places.currentId;
      var p = id && root.places.placesById && root.places.placesById[id];
      return p && p.name ? WL.U.safeString(p.name) : '(unknown)';
    },
    activeQuestLine: function (root) {
      var ids = root.quests && root.quests.activeIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return 'Active Quest: (none)';
      var q = root.quests.questsById[ids[ids.length - 1]];
      if (!q) return 'Active Quest: (none)';
      return 'Active Quest: ' + WL.U.safeString(q.title || q.objective) + ' — ' + WL.U.safeString(q.objective || q.status);
    },
    threatLine: function (root) {
      var ids = root.combat && root.combat.groupIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) return 'Threats: (none confirmed)';
      var parts = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && parts.length < 4; i++) {
        var g = root.combat.groupCountsById[ids[i]];
        if (g) parts.push(WL.U.safeString(g.name || ids[i]) + ' x' + WL.U.safeString(g.remaining != null ? g.remaining : g.count));
      }
      return 'Threats: ' + (parts.join(', ') || '(none confirmed)');
    },
    itemLine: function (root, max) {
      var ids = root.inventory && root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      var parts = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && parts.length < (max || 5); i++) {
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[ids[i]];
        if (item) parts.push(WL.U.safeString(item.displayName) + (item.quantity > 1 ? ' x' + item.quantity : ''));
      }
      return 'Carried: ' + (parts.join(', ') || '(none)');
    },
    abilityLine: function (root, max) {
      var parts = [];
      var ids = root.skills && root.skills.skillIds || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && parts.length < (max || 4); i++) if (root.skills.skillsById[ids[i]]) parts.push(root.skills.skillsById[ids[i]].name + ' [skill]');
      ids = root.abilities && root.abilities.abilityIds || [];
      for (var j = 0; j < ids.length && parts.length < (max || 4); j++) if (root.abilities.abilitiesById[ids[j]]) parts.push(root.abilities.abilitiesById[ids[j]].name + ' [ability]');
      return 'Relevant Abilities: ' + (parts.length ? parts.join(', ') : '(none stable)');
    },
    conditionLine: function (root) {
      var parts = [];
      var injuries = root.body && root.body.injuries || [];
      var conds = root.body && root.body.conditions || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < injuries.length && parts.length < 3; i++) parts.push(WL.U.safeString(injuries[i].name) + ' [' + WL.U.safeString(injuries[i].severity || 'injury') + ']');
      for (var j = 0; j < conds.length && parts.length < 5; j++) parts.push(WL.U.safeString(conds[j].name) + ' [' + WL.U.safeString(conds[j].severity || 'condition') + ']');
      return 'Conditions: ' + (parts.join(', ') || '(none)');
    },
    recentLine: function (root) {
      var r = root.pending && root.pending.lastPipeline;
      if (!r) return 'Recent Changes: (none this turn)';
      return 'Recent Changes: ' + WL.U.safeString(r.hook) + ' events=' + r.events + ', proposals=' + r.proposals + ', reducers=' + r.reducers;
    },
    identitySurfaceLine: function (root, mode) {
      var ids = root.inventory && root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      var stacked = [], uniques = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[ids[i]];
        if (!item || !item.displayName) continue;
        var label = WL.U.safeString(item.displayName) + ((item.quantity || 1) > 1 ? ' x' + item.quantity : '');
        if (item.stackable) stacked.push(label);
        else uniques.push(label);
      }
      var max = mode === 'high' ? 4 : (mode === 'medium' ? 3 : 2);
      var parts = [];
      if (stacked.length) parts.push('stacked ' + stacked.slice(0, max).join(', '));
      if (uniques.length) parts.push('unique ' + uniques.slice(0, max).join(', '));
      return 'Identity: clean item names; ' + (parts.join('; ') || 'no carried item identity records');
    },
    costSurfaceLine: function (root, mode) {
      var max = mode === 'high' ? 3 : (mode === 'medium' ? 2 : 1);
      var costs = WL.AbilityCostSystem && WL.AbilityCostSystem.summary ? WL.AbilityCostSystem.summary(root).filter(function (line) { return line && line !== '(none)'; }).slice(0, max) : [];
      return 'Ability Costs: ' + (costs.length ? costs.join(' | ') : '(none observed/hardened yet)');
    },
    graphSurfaceLine: function (root, mode) {
      if (!WL.WorldGraphIndex || typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.rebuild !== 'function') return 'World Graph: (unavailable)';
      var graph = WL.WorldGraphIndex.rebuild(root);
      if (mode === 'small') return 'World Graph: nodes ' + graph.nodeIds.length + ', links ' + graph.edges.length;
      var names = graph.nodeIds.slice(0, mode === 'high' ? 5 : 3).map(function (id) { return graph.nodesById[id] && graph.nodesById[id].title; }).filter(Boolean);
      return 'World Graph: nodes ' + graph.nodeIds.length + ', links ' + graph.edges.length + (names.length ? ' — ' + names.join(', ') : '');
    },
    renderPacket: function (root, mode) {
      var lines = [];
      var time = 'Day ' + root.time.day + ' ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0');
      lines.push('[WorldLedger Context — Build 9.6B2 Truth Packet / ' + mode + ']');
      lines.push('Resources: HP ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + ', MP ' + root.body.mp + '/' + root.body.maxMp + ', EP ' + root.body.ep + '/' + root.body.maxEp + '. ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.conditionLine(root));
      lines.push('Scene: ' + time + ' | Place: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root));
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.threatLine(root));
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.activeQuestLine(root));
      if (mode === 'high') lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.recentLine(root));
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.itemLine(root, mode === 'high' ? 10 : (mode === 'medium' ? 6 : 3)));
      lines.push('Actors: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.actors.presentIds.concat(root.actors.referencedIds).slice(0, mode === 'high' ? 10 : 5), root.actors.actorsById, 'name', mode === 'high' ? 10 : 5));
      if (root.features && root.features.presenceKnowledgeFoundationEnabled !== false && root.social) {
        var sp = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.presenceSummary(root);
        lines.push('Presence: ' + sp[0].replace(/^Named present:\s*/, '') + '; ' + sp[1].replace(/^Generic groups:\s*/, 'groups '));
        if (root.features.witnessEligibilityLedgerEnabled !== false) {
          var ws = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.witnessSummary(root);
          lines.push('Witnesses: ' + ws.slice(0, mode === 'small' ? 1 : 2).join(' | ').replace(/^Witnesses:\s*/, ''));
        }
        if (root.features.reputationLocalStandingEnabled !== false) {
          var rs = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.reputationSummary(root);
          lines.push('Reputation: ' + rs.slice(0, mode === 'small' ? 1 : 2).join(' | ').replace(/^Reputation:\s*/, ''));
        }
        if (root.features.rumorPropagationEnabled !== false) {
          var rums = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.rumorSummary(root);
          lines.push('Rumors: ' + rums.slice(0, mode === 'small' ? 1 : 2).join(' | ').replace(/^Rumors:\s*/, ''));
        }
        if (root.features.crimeEvidenceClueFoundationEnabled !== false) {
          var cs = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.crimeSummary(root);
          var cls = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.clueSummary(root);
          lines.push('Crime/Evidence: ' + cs.slice(0, 1).join(' | ').replace(/^Crime Cases:\s*/, '') + ' / ' + cls.slice(0, 1).join(' | ').replace(/^Clues:\s*/, ''));
        }
        if (root.features.factionLawResponseFoundationEnabled !== false) {
          var lsum = WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.lawSummary(root);
          lines.push('Law Risk: ' + lsum.slice(0, 1).join(' | ').replace(/^Law\/Enforcement:\s*/, ''));
        }
        if (root.features.contractLifecycleFoundationEnabled !== false && WL.ContractSystem) lines.push('Contracts/Labor: ' + WL.ContractSystem.summary(root).slice(0, 1).join(' | ') + ' / ' + WL.LaborSystem.summary(root).slice(0, 1).join(' | '));
        if (root.features.domainProjectMvpEnabled !== false && WL.DomainProjectSystem) lines.push('Domain Projects: ' + WL.DomainProjectSystem.summary(root).slice(0, mode === 'small' ? 2 : 3).join(' | ').replace(/^Domain Projects:\s*/, ''));
        if (root.features.economyTransactionFoundationEnabled !== false && WL.EconomyTransactionSystem) lines.push('Economy: ' + WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.summary(root).slice(0, mode === 'small' ? 1 : 2).join(' | ').replace(/^Economy:\s*/, ''));
        if (mode !== 'small') lines.push('Knowledge: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.knowledgeSummary(root).slice(0, 2).join(' | ').replace(/^Knowledge:\s*/, ''));
      }
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.abilityLine(root, mode === 'high' ? 8 : 4));
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.identitySurfaceLine(root, mode));
      lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.costSurfaceLine(root, mode));
      if (mode !== 'small') {
        lines.push(WL.ContextBudgeter.graphSurfaceLine(root, mode));
        lines.push('Player: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.name) + ' | True Race: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.trueRace || root.player.raceSpecies) + ' | Current Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.currentForm));
      }
      if (mode === 'high') {
        lines.push('Places Known: ' + Object.keys(root.places.placesById || {}).length + ' | Contradictions: ' + (root.places.contradictions || []).length + ' | Routes: ' + (root.time.routeIds || []).length);
        lines.push('Progression: ' + WL.ProgressionSystem.summary(root)[0] + ' | ' + WL.ProgressionSystem.summary(root)[4]);
      }
      lines.push('Canon: read-only; commands/cards/context do not grant mechanics; candidate records stay uncertain until confirmed.');
      return lines.join('\n');
    },
    build: function (root, baseText, infoObj) {
      if (!root || !root.features || root.features.contextBudgeterEnabled === false) {
        var disabledBase = String(baseText == null ? '' : baseText);
        var disabledMax = WL.ContextBudgeter.hardMaxChars(infoObj || (typeof info === 'object' ? info : null));
        return disabledMax ? WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(disabledBase, disabledMax) : disabledBase;
      }
      var mode = root.features.contextMode || 'small';
      if (!WL.ContextBudgeter.modeCaps[mode]) mode = 'small';
      var infoRef = infoObj || (typeof info === 'object' ? info : null);
      var maxChars = WL.ContextBudgeter.hardMaxChars(infoRef);
      var cap = WL.ContextBudgeter.maxPacketChars(root, infoRef);
      var rendered = WL.ContextBudgeter.renderPacket(root, mode);
      var packet = WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(rendered, cap);
      var base = String(baseText == null ? '' : baseText);
      if (maxChars > 0 && maxChars <= 80) return WL.ContextBudgeter.microPacket(root, mode, maxChars);
      if (!base) {
        if (maxChars && packet.length > maxChars) return WL.ContextBudgeter.microPacket(root, mode, maxChars);
        return packet;
      }
      var joined = base + '\n\n' + packet;
      if (!maxChars || joined.length <= maxChars) return joined;
      var sep = '\n\n';
      var packetBudget = Math.min(cap, Math.max(40, Math.floor(maxChars * 0.35)));
      if (maxChars < 700) packetBudget = Math.max(40, Math.floor(maxChars * 0.50));
      packet = WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(rendered, packetBudget);
      if (packet.length + sep.length >= maxChars) return WL.ContextBudgeter.microPacket(root, mode, maxChars);
      var keepBase = Math.max(0, maxChars - packet.length - sep.length);
      var finalText = WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(base, keepBase) + sep + packet;
      return finalText.length > maxChars ? WL.ContextBudgeter.clip(finalText, maxChars) : finalText;
    }
  };

  WL.ContextSystem = {
    build: function (root, baseText) {
      return WL.ContextBudgeter.build(root, baseText, (typeof info === 'object' ? info : null));
    }
  };


  WL.WorldGraphIndex = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.worldGraph || typeof root.worldGraph !== 'object') root.worldGraph = { nodesById: {}, nodeIds: [], edges: [], lastRebuildTurn: 0, warnings: [] };
      if (!root.worldGraph.nodesById || typeof root.worldGraph.nodesById !== 'object') root.worldGraph.nodesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.worldGraph.nodeIds)) root.worldGraph.nodeIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.worldGraph.edges)) root.worldGraph.edges = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.worldGraph.warnings)) root.worldGraph.warnings = [];
      return root.worldGraph;
    },
    node: function (root, kind, id, title, data) {
      var graph = WL.WorldGraphIndex.ensure(root);
      if (!id || !title || (WL.GeneratedCardPolicy && WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle && WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle(title))) return null;
      var key = kind + ':' + id;
      var node = graph.nodesById[key] || { id: key, refId: id, kind: kind, title: WL.U.safeString(title), weight: 0, tags: [], links: [] };
      node.title = WL.U.safeString(title);
      node.kind = kind;
      node.refId = id;
      node.weight = Math.max(node.weight || 0, data && data.weight || 1);
      node.tags = WL.U.unique((node.tags || []).concat(data && data.tags || []));
      node.summary = WL.U.safeString(data && data.summary || node.summary || '');
      graph.nodesById[key] = node;
      if (graph.nodeIds.indexOf(key) < 0) graph.nodeIds.push(key);
      return node;
    },
    edge: function (root, fromKey, toKey, relation, weight) {
      if (!fromKey || !toKey || fromKey === toKey) return null;
      var graph = WL.WorldGraphIndex.ensure(root);
      var id = WL.U.hash(fromKey + '|' + toKey + '|' + relation);
      for (var i = 0; i < graph.edges.length; i++) if (graph.edges[i].id === id) return graph.edges[i];
      var edge = { id: id, from: fromKey, to: toKey, relation: relation || 'related', weight: weight || 1 };
      graph.edges.push(edge);
      WL.U.capArray(graph.edges, 240);
      return edge;
    },
    touchItem: function (root, item, relation) {
      if (!item || !item.id || !item.displayName) return null;
      return WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'item', item.id, item.displayName, { weight: item.cardEligible === false ? 1 : 2, tags: item.tags || [], summary: 'Inventory item: ' + (item.category || 'misc') });
    },
    rebuild: function (root) {
      var graph = WL.WorldGraphIndex.ensure(root);
      graph.nodesById = {}; graph.nodeIds = []; graph.edges = [];
      var playerName = root.player && root.player.name && root.player.name !== 'Unknown' ? root.player.name : 'Player';
      var player = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'player', 'player', playerName, { weight: 5, tags: ['player'] });
      var actorKeys = Object.keys(root.actors.actorsById || {});
      for (var a = 0; a < actorKeys.length; a++) {
        var actor = root.actors.actorsById[actorKeys[a]];
        if (actor && actor.name) {
          var an = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'actor', actor.id || actorKeys[a], actor.name, { weight: 4, tags: ['actor'], summary: actor.summary || actor.status || '' });
          if (player && an) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, player.id, an.id, 'knows_or_observed', 2);
        }
      }
      var placeKeys = Object.keys(root.places.placesById || {});
      for (var p = 0; p < placeKeys.length; p++) {
        var place = root.places.placesById[placeKeys[p]];
        if (place && place.name) WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'place', place.id || placeKeys[p], place.name, { weight: 4, tags: ['place', place.type || 'place'], summary: place.type || 'confirmed place' });
      }
      var itemIds = root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < itemIds.length; i++) {
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[itemIds[i]];
        if (item && item.displayName) {
          var inode = WL.WorldGraphIndex.touchItem(root, item, 'owned_by_player');
          if (player && inode) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, player.id, inode.id, 'owns', item.stackable ? 1 : 3);
        }
      }
      var questIds = [].concat(root.quests.activeIds || [], root.quests.offeredIds || [], root.quests.completedIds || []);
      for (var q = 0; q < questIds.length; q++) {
        var quest = root.quests.questsById[questIds[q]];
        if (quest && quest.title) WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'quest', quest.id || questIds[q], quest.title, { weight: quest.status === 'active' ? 5 : 3, tags: ['quest', quest.status || 'unknown'], summary: quest.objective || '' });
      }
      var contractIds = root.contracts && root.contracts.contractIds || [];
      for (var ct = 0; ct < contractIds.length; ct++) {
        var con = root.contracts.contractsById[contractIds[ct]];
        if (con && con.partyB) {
          var cn = WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'contract', con.id || contractIds[ct], con.partyB + ' Contract', { weight: con.status === 'active' ? 4 : 2, tags: ['contract', con.status || 'unknown'], summary: con.work || '' });
          var actorKey = 'actor:' + (con.partyBId || ('actor_' + WL.U.slug(con.partyB)));
          if (cn) WL.WorldGraphIndex.edge(root, actorKey, cn.id, 'party_to_contract', 2);
        }
      }
      var empIds = root.labor && root.labor.employeeIds || [];
      for (var le = 0; le < empIds.length; le++) {
        var emp = root.labor.employeesById[empIds[le]];
        if (emp && emp.actorName) WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'employee', emp.id || empIds[le], emp.actorName + ' — ' + (emp.role || 'Worker'), { weight: emp.status === 'employed' ? 3 : 1, tags: ['labor', emp.status || 'unknown'], summary: emp.assignment || '' });
      }
      var abilityIds = [].concat(root.abilities.abilityIds || [], root.skills.skillIds || [], root.talents.talentIds || [], root.passives.passiveIds || []);
      for (var ab = 0; ab < abilityIds.length; ab++) {
        var rec = root.abilities.abilitiesById[abilityIds[ab]] || root.skills.skillsById[abilityIds[ab]] || root.talents.talentsById[abilityIds[ab]] || root.passives.passivesById[abilityIds[ab]];
        if (rec && rec.name) WL.WorldGraphIndex.node(root, 'ability', rec.id || abilityIds[ab], rec.name, { weight: rec.stable || rec.status === 'stable' ? 4 : 2, tags: ['ability', rec.status || 'candidate'], summary: 'Ability/skill/passive record' });
      }
      graph.lastRebuildTurn = root.runtime.currentTurn || 0;
      return graph;
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var graph = WL.WorldGraphIndex.rebuild(root);
      return ['Nodes: ' + graph.nodeIds.length, 'Edges: ' + graph.edges.length].concat(graph.nodeIds.slice(0, 12).map(function (id) { var n = graph.nodesById[id]; return '- ' + n.kind + ': ' + n.title; }));
    }
  };

  WL.CardCandidateRegistry = {
    ensure: function (root) {
      if (!root.cards.candidatesById || typeof root.cards.candidatesById !== 'object') root.cards.candidatesById = {};
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.candidateIds)) root.cards.candidateIds = [];
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cards.candidateLog)) root.cards.candidateLog = [];
    },
    rejectReason: function (kind, title, rec) {
      var clean = WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.cleanTitle(title || '');
      if (!clean || WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle(clean)) return 'trash_title';
      if (WL.CardRegistry.isBroadTrigger(clean)) return 'broad_trigger';
      if (/\b(?:thing|object|person|someone|something|unknown|none|empty)\b/i.test(clean)) return 'abstract_or_generic_title';
      if (kind === 'item') {
        if (!rec || rec.cardEligible === false) return 'item_not_card_eligible';
        if (rec.stackable && /^(?:material|ammunition|consumable)$/.test(rec.category || '')) return 'ordinary_stack_not_carded';
      }
      return '';
    },
    score: function (kind, rec) {
      var s = 1;
      if (kind === 'actor' || kind === 'place' || kind === 'quest') s += 4;
      if (kind === 'ability') s += 3;
      if (kind === 'item') s += rec && rec.stackable ? 1 : 3;
      if (rec && rec.status === 'active') s += 2;
      if (rec && rec.tags && rec.tags.length) s += rec.tags.length;
      if (rec && (rec.summary || rec.objective || rec.sourceHistory && rec.sourceHistory.length > 1)) s += 1;
      return s;
    },
    addCandidate: function (root, out, kind, id, title, summary, rec) {
      WL.CardCandidateRegistry.ensure(root);
      title = WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.cleanTitle(title || '');
      var reason = WL.CardCandidateRegistry.rejectReason(kind, title, rec);
      if (reason) {
        root.cards.candidateLog.push({ turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, kind: kind, title: title || '(blank)', status: 'rejected', reason: reason });
        WL.U.capArray(root.cards.candidateLog, 80);
        return;
      }
      var cid = id || ('gen_' + kind + '_' + WL.U.slug(title));
      var score = WL.CardCandidateRegistry.score(kind, rec || {});
      var cand = { kind: kind, id: cid, title: title, summary: WL.U.safeString(summary || ''), score: score, source: 'confirmed_reducer_state', graphNodeId: kind + ':' + (rec && rec.id || id || WL.U.slug(title)) };
      root.cards.candidatesById[cid] = cand;
      if (root.cards.candidateIds.indexOf(cid) < 0) root.cards.candidateIds.push(cid);
      out.push(cand);
    },
    collect: function (root, max) {
      WL.CardCandidateRegistry.ensure(root);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.rebuild === 'function') WL.WorldGraphIndex.rebuild(root);
      var out = [];
      var actorIds = WL.U.unique((root.actors.presentIds || []).concat(root.actors.referencedIds || [], Object.keys(root.actors.actorsById || {})));
      for (var a = 0; a < actorIds.length; a++) { var actor = root.actors.actorsById[actorIds[a]]; if (actor && actor.name) WL.CardCandidateRegistry.addCandidate(root, out, 'actor', 'gen_actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor.name), actor.name, actor.summary || actor.status || 'Named actor remembered by WorldLedger.', actor); }
      var placeKeys = Object.keys(root.places.placesById || {});
      for (var p = 0; p < placeKeys.length; p++) { var place = root.places.placesById[placeKeys[p]]; if (place && place.name) WL.CardCandidateRegistry.addCandidate(root, out, 'place', 'gen_place_' + WL.U.slug(place.name), place.name, (place.type || 'place') + '; confirmed world structure mirror.', place); }
      var abilityIds = (root.abilities.abilityIds || []).concat(root.skills.skillIds || [], root.talents.talentIds || [], root.passives.passiveIds || []);
      for (var i = 0; i < abilityIds.length; i++) { var rec = root.abilities.abilitiesById[abilityIds[i]] || root.skills.skillsById[abilityIds[i]] || root.talents.talentsById[abilityIds[i]] || root.passives.passivesById[abilityIds[i]]; if (rec && rec.name) WL.CardCandidateRegistry.addCandidate(root, out, 'ability', 'gen_ability_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name), rec.name, 'Ability/skill/passive mirror; mechanics remain reducer-owned.', rec); }
      var itemIds = root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      for (var it = 0; it < itemIds.length; it++) { var item = root.inventory.itemsById[itemIds[it]]; if (item && item.displayName) WL.CardCandidateRegistry.addCandidate(root, out, 'item', 'gen_item_' + WL.U.slug(item.displayName), item.displayName, 'Confirmed possessed item mirror; no card-prose grants.', item); }
      var questIds = (root.quests.activeIds || []).concat(root.quests.offeredIds || []);
      for (var q = 0; q < questIds.length; q++) { var quest = root.quests.questsById[questIds[q]]; if (quest && quest.title) WL.CardCandidateRegistry.addCandidate(root, out, 'quest', 'gen_quest_' + WL.U.slug(quest.title), quest.title, WL.U.safeString(quest.objective || quest.status || 'Quest mirror.'), quest); }
      out.sort(function (a, b) { return (b.score || 0) - (a.score || 0) || WL.U.lower(a.title).localeCompare(WL.U.lower(b.title)); });
      return out.slice(0, max || 8);
    },
    summary: function (root) {
      var cands = WL.CardCandidateRegistry.collect(root, 20);
      return ['Candidates: ' + cands.length].concat(cands.map(function (c) { return '- [' + c.kind + '/' + c.score + '] ' + c.title; }));
    }
  };

  WL.CardRegistry = {
    markerPrefix: 'WL-PROTECTED:',
    metaPrefix: '<!-- WL:',
    protectedDefs: Object.freeze([
      { id: 'player_sheet', title: 'Player Sheet', keys: 'WL — Player Sheet' },
      { id: 'body_status', title: 'Body & Status', keys: 'WL — Body & Status' },
      { id: 'inventory_equipment', title: 'Inventory & Equipment', keys: 'WL — Inventory & Equipment' },
      { id: 'abilities', title: 'Abilities', keys: 'WL — Abilities' },
      { id: 'skills', title: 'Skills', keys: 'WL — Skills' },
      { id: 'passives', title: 'Passives', keys: 'WL — Passives' },
      { id: 'quest_log', title: 'Quest Log', keys: 'WL — Quest Log' },
      { id: 'people_factions', title: 'People & Factions', keys: 'WL — People & Factions' },
      { id: 'places_time', title: 'Places & Time', keys: 'WL — Places & Time' },
      { id: 'settings', title: 'WorldLedger Settings', keys: 'WL — WorldLedger Settings' },
      { id: 'command_audit', title: 'Command Log & Audit', keys: 'WL — Command Log & Audit' }
    ]),
    defById: function (id) {
      for (var i = 0; i < WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs.length; i++) if (WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs[i].id === id) return WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs[i];
      return null;
    },
    marker: function (id) { return 'WL-PROTECTED:' + id; },
    generatedMarker: function (id) { return 'WL-GENERATED:' + id; },
    cleanVisibleKey: function (title) { return WL.U.title(title || '').replace(/\b(?:WorldLedger\s+Generated|WL[- ](?:PROTECTED|GENERATED)|schema|owner)\b/ig, '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); },
    generatedKeys: function (title, id) { return WL.CardRegistry.cleanVisibleKey(title) || 'WorldLedger Memory'; },
    isBroadTrigger: function (value) {
      var v = WL.U.lower(value).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/, '');
      return /^(?:inn|street|door|counter|town|city|magic|item|quest|road|room|guard|guards|shop|market|person|people|none|unknown|empty|are_empty|are empty|no items|known places|command|audit|pass|fail|status)$/.test(v);
    }
  };

  WL.CardRenderer = {
    lines: function (title) { return ['# ' + WL.U.safeString(title), '']; },
    cleanUserNotes: function (oldEntry) {
      var text = String(oldEntry || '');
      var marker = '## User Notes';
      var idx = text.indexOf(marker);
      if (idx < 0) return '';
      var notes = text.slice(idx + marker.length);
      notes = notes.replace(/\[WL-META[^\]]*\]/g, '');
      notes = notes.replace(/<!--\s*WL:[\s\S]*?-->/g, '');
      notes = notes.replace(/\(Add personal notes below this heading\. WorldLedger preserves this section once, but never treats it as mechanics\.\)/g, '');
      notes = notes.replace(/\s+$/g, '').replace(/^\s+/g, '');
      return notes ? notes.slice(0, 2000) : '';
    },
    appendMeta: function (lines, id, userNotes) {
      var marker = /^gen_/i.test(id || '') ? WL.CardRegistry.generatedMarker(id) : WL.CardRegistry.marker(id);
      lines.push('');
      lines.push('## User Notes');
      lines.push(userNotes ? WL.U.safeString(userNotes) : '(Add personal notes below this heading. WorldLedger preserves this section once, but never treats it as mechanics.)');
      lines.push('');
      lines.push('<!-- WL: ' + marker + ' id=' + id + ' schema=' + WL.SCHEMA_VERSION + ' build=' + WL.BUILD_VERSION + ' owner=CardRenderer -->');
      return lines;
    },
    renderProtected: function (root, id, oldEntry) {
      var def = WL.CardRegistry.defById(id) || { id: id, title: WL.U.title(id), keys: 'WorldLedger, ' + id };
      var lines = WL.CardRenderer.lines(def.title);
      if (id === 'player_sheet') WL.CardRenderer.renderPlayer(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'body_status') WL.CardRenderer.renderBody(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'inventory_equipment') WL.CardRenderer.renderInventory(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'abilities') WL.CardRenderer.renderAbilities(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'skills') WL.CardRenderer.renderSkills(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'passives') WL.CardRenderer.renderPassives(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'quest_log') WL.CardRenderer.renderQuests(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'people_factions') WL.CardRenderer.renderPeople(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'places_time') WL.CardRenderer.renderPlacesTime(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'settings') WL.CardRenderer.renderSettings(root, lines);
      else if (id === 'command_audit') WL.CardRenderer.renderAudit(root, lines);
      else lines.push('No renderer installed for this protected card.');
      WL.CardRenderer.appendMeta(lines, id, WL.CardRenderer.cleanUserNotes(oldEntry));
      return lines.join('\n').replace(/\[object Object\]/g, '[object]');
    },
    renderGenerated: function (root, rec, oldEntry) {
      rec = rec || {};
      var title = WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.cleanTitle(rec.title || rec.name || 'WorldLedger Memory');
      var lines = WL.CardRenderer.lines(title);
      lines.push('Kind: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.kind || 'memory'));
      lines.push('Summary: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.summary || 'Durable WorldLedger mirror.'));
      if (rec.score != null) lines.push('Importance: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.score));
      if (rec.graphNodeId) lines.push('World Link: ' + WL.U.safeString(rec.graphNodeId));
      if (rec.kind === 'actor') lines.push('Actor memory is evidence-tracked; extras remain scene-only.');
      if (rec.kind === 'place') lines.push('Place memory mirrors confirmed structure and contradictions.');
      if (rec.kind === 'ability') lines.push('Ability memory mirrors candidate/stable records; card prose does not grant mechanics.');
      if (rec.kind === 'item') lines.push('Item memory mirrors confirmed possession/history; card prose does not grant items.');
      if (rec.kind === 'quest') lines.push('Quest memory mirrors lifecycle/rewards; card prose does not pay rewards.');
      WL.CardRenderer.appendMeta(lines, rec.id || ('generated_' + WL.U.slug(title)), WL.CardRenderer.cleanUserNotes(oldEntry));
      return lines.join('\n').replace(/\[object Object\]/g, '[object]');
    },
    renderPlayer: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Name: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.name));
      lines.push('Pronouns: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.pronouns || 'unknown'));
      lines.push('Race / True Race / Current Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.raceSpecies) + ' / ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.trueRace) + ' / ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.currentForm));
      lines.push('Appearance: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.appearance));
      lines.push('Class / Profession / Origin: ' + WL.U.safeString([root.player.classLabel, root.player.profession, root.player.origin].filter(Boolean).join(' / ') || 'undeclared'));
      lines.push('Progression: ' + WL.ProgressionSystem.summary(root)[0]);
    },
    renderBody: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('HP/MP/EP: ' + root.body.hp + '/' + root.body.maxHp + ' | ' + root.body.mp + '/' + root.body.maxMp + ' | ' + root.body.ep + '/' + root.body.maxEp);
      lines.push('Disguise: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.body.activeDisguise || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Illusion: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.body.activeIllusion || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Temporary Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.body.temporaryForm || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Body Traits: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.body.bodyTraits, null, 'name', 12));
      lines.push('Injuries: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.body.injuries, null, 'name', 12));
      lines.push('Conditions: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.body.conditions, null, 'name', 12));
    },
    renderInventory: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Currency: ' + (root.inventory.currency.gold || 0) + ' gold, ' + (root.inventory.currency.silver || 0) + ' silver, ' + (root.inventory.currency.copper || 0) + ' copper');
      lines.push('Stack Policy: stack ordinary identical consumables/materials/ammunition; keep weapons, armor, named, magical, quest, damaged, equipped, or history-bearing items separate.');
      lines.push('Owned Items:');
      var ids = root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('- (none)');
      var itemLimit = 80;
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && i < itemLimit; i++) { var it = root.inventory.itemsById[ids[i]]; if (it) lines.push('- ' + WL.U.safeString(it.displayName) + ((it.quantity || 1) > 1 ? ' x' + (it.quantity || 1) : '') + ' [' + WL.U.safeString(it.condition || 'unknown') + '; ' + (it.stackable ? 'stacked' : 'unique') + '; ' + WL.U.safeString(it.category || 'misc') + ']'); }
      if (ids.length > itemLimit) lines.push('- ... ' + (ids.length - itemLimit) + ' more owned item records kept in state.');
    },
    renderAbilities: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Stable Abilities: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.abilities.abilityIds, root.abilities.abilitiesById, 'name', 20));
      lines.push('Inbox/Candidates: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.abilities.inboxIds, root.abilities.inboxById, 'name', 20));
      lines.push('Denied Transfers: ' + (root.abilities.denied || []).length);
      var costLines = WL.AbilityCostSystem.summary(root).slice(0, 8);
      lines.push('Development Matrix: inbox -> unstable -> practiced -> stable -> hardened; costs harden from story evidence and repeated use.');
      lines.push('Costs: ' + (costLines.length && costLines[0] !== '(none)' ? costLines.join(' | ') : '(none)'));
    },
    renderSkills: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Skills: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.skills.skillIds, root.skills.skillsById, 'name', 30));
      lines.push('Unstable Skills: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.skills.unstableIds, root.skills.unstableById, 'name', 30));
    },
    renderPassives: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Passives: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.passives.passiveIds, root.passives.passivesById, 'name', 30));
      lines.push('Weak Candidates: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.joinNames(root.passives.weakCandidateIds, root.passives.weakCandidateById, 'name', 30));
    },
    renderQuests: function (root, lines) {
      var ids = [].concat(root.quests.activeIds || [], root.quests.offeredIds || [], root.quests.completedIds || []);
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      var seen = {};
      var questLimit = 50;
      var shown = 0;
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && shown < questLimit; i++) { var q = root.quests.questsById[ids[i]]; if (q && !seen[q.id]) { seen[q.id] = true; shown++; lines.push('- [' + WL.U.safeString(q.status) + '] ' + WL.U.safeString(q.title) + ': ' + WL.U.safeString(q.objective)); } }
      if (ids.length > shown) lines.push('- ... ' + (ids.length - shown) + ' more quest records kept in state.');
    },
    renderPeople: function (root, lines) {
      var ids = root.actors.referencedIds.concat(root.actors.presentIds || []);
      if (!ids.length) lines.push('(none)');
      var seen = {};
      var shown = 0;
      var actorLimit = 80;
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length && shown < actorLimit; i++) { var a = root.actors.actorsById[ids[i]]; if (a && !seen[a.id]) { seen[a.id] = true; shown++; lines.push('- ' + WL.U.safeString(a.name) + ': ' + WL.U.safeString(a.summary || a.status || 'known actor')); } }
      var actorCount = Object.keys(root.actors.actorsById || {}).length;
      if (actorCount > shown) lines.push('- ... ' + (actorCount - shown) + ' more actor records kept in state.');
      lines.push('Scene Extras: ' + ((root.actors.sceneExtras || []).length || 0));
      if (root.social && root.features && root.features.presenceKnowledgeFoundationEnabled !== false) {
        lines.push('Presence: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.presenceSummary(root).join(' | '));
        lines.push('Knowledge Facts: ' + ((root.social.knowledge && root.social.knowledge.factIds || []).length));
        if (root.features.witnessEligibilityLedgerEnabled !== false) lines.push('Witness Ledger: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.witnessSummary(root).join(' | '));
        if (root.features.reputationLocalStandingEnabled !== false) lines.push('Local Standing: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.reputationSummary(root).join(' | '));
        if (root.features.rumorPropagationEnabled !== false) lines.push('Rumors: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.rumorSummary(root).join(' | '));
        if (root.features.crimeEvidenceClueFoundationEnabled !== false) { lines.push('Crime Cases: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.crimeSummary(root).join(' | ')); lines.push('Clues: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.clueSummary(root).join(' | ')); }
        if (root.features.factionLawResponseFoundationEnabled !== false) lines.push('Law/Enforcement: ' + WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.lawSummary(root).join(' | '));
      }
      if (root.features && root.features.contractLifecycleFoundationEnabled !== false && WL.ContractSystem) {
        lines.push('Contracts: ' + WL.ContractSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
        lines.push('Labor: ' + WL.LaborSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
      }
      if (root.features && root.features.domainProjectMvpEnabled !== false && WL.DomainProjectSystem) lines.push('Domain Projects: ' + WL.DomainProjectSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
      if (root.features && root.features.economyTransactionFoundationEnabled !== false && WL.EconomyTransactionSystem) lines.push('Economy: ' + WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
    },
    renderPlacesTime: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Current Time: Day ' + root.time.day + ' ' + String(root.time.hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(root.time.minute).padStart(2, '0'));
      lines.push('Current Place: ' + WL.ContextBudgeter.currentPlaceName(root));
      var keys = Object.keys(root.places.placesById || {});
      lines.push('Known Places: ' + keys.length);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length && i < 20; i++) { var p = root.places.placesById[keys[i]]; lines.push('- ' + WL.U.safeString(p.name) + ' [' + WL.U.safeString(p.type || 'place') + ']'); }
      lines.push('Contradictions: ' + (root.places.contradictions || []).length);
      if (WL.WorldGraphIndex && typeof WL.WorldGraphIndex.summary === 'function') lines.push('World Graph: ' + WL.WorldGraphIndex.summary(root).slice(0, 2).join(' | '));
      if (root.features && root.features.domainProjectMvpEnabled !== false && WL.DomainProjectSystem) lines.push('Domain Projects: ' + WL.DomainProjectSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
      if (root.features && root.features.economyTransactionFoundationEnabled !== false && WL.EconomyTransactionSystem) lines.push('Economy: ' + WL.EconomyTransactionSystem.summary(root).join(' | '));
    },
    renderSettings: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Build: ' + WL.BUILD_VERSION);
      lines.push('Stage: ' + WL.BUILD_STAGE);
      lines.push('Release Status: ' + WL.STATUS);
      lines.push('Context Mode: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.contextMode));
      lines.push('Story Card Writes: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.storyCardWritesEnabled));
      lines.push('Generated Cards: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.generatedCardsEnabled));
      lines.push('Surface Integration: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.identityAbilitySurfaceEnabled));
      lines.push('Presence/Knowledge Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.presenceKnowledgeFoundationEnabled));
      lines.push('Witness Ledger: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.witnessEligibilityLedgerEnabled));
      lines.push('Reputation/Standing Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.reputationLocalStandingEnabled));
      lines.push('Rumor Propagation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.rumorPropagationEnabled));
      lines.push('Crime/Evidence/Clue Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.crimeEvidenceClueFoundationEnabled));
      lines.push('Faction/Law Response Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.factionLawResponseFoundationEnabled));
      lines.push('Contract Lifecycle Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.contractLifecycleFoundationEnabled));
      lines.push('Labor Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.laborFoundationEnabled));
      lines.push('Domain Project MVP: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.domainProjectMvpEnabled));
      lines.push('Economy Transaction Foundation: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.features.economyTransactionFoundationEnabled));
    },
    renderAudit: function (root, lines) {
      lines.push('Last Pipeline: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.runtime.lastPipelineSummary || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Recent Operation Keys: ' + (root.operationLedger.recentKeys || []).length);
      lines.push('Duplicate Skips: ' + (root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips || []).length);
      lines.push('Card Candidates: ' + ((root.cards && root.cards.candidateIds || []).length));
      if (root.worldGraph) lines.push('World Graph: nodes ' + ((root.worldGraph.nodeIds || []).length) + ', links ' + ((root.worldGraph.edges || []).length));
      lines.push('Cost Records: ' + ((root.abilityCosts && root.abilityCosts.costIds || []).length));
      var audit = root.diagnostics.audit || [];
      for (var i = Math.max(0, audit.length - 8); i < audit.length; i++) lines.push('- ' + WL.U.safeString(audit[i].subsystem) + ': ' + WL.U.safeString(audit[i].message));
    }
  };

  WL.GeneratedCardPolicy = {
    cleanTitle: function (title) {
      return WL.U.title(title || '')
        .replace(/\b(?:WorldLedger\s+Generated|WL[- ](?:PROTECTED|GENERATED)|Generated Card|schema|owner)\b/ig, '')
        .replace(/\s*,\s*/g, ' ')
        .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
        .trim();
    },
    isTrashTitle: function (title) {
      var t = WL.U.lower(title).replace(/^(?:the|a|an)\s+/, '');
      if (!t) return true;
      if (/^(?:are empty|empty|none|unknown|no items?|no armor|no weapon|no coin|known places|command log|command|audit|status|pass|fail|worldledger generated)$/.test(t)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:are empty|pockets? are empty|no armor|no weapon|no coin|known places|generated-card policy|card check|release check|summary pass)\b/.test(t)) return true;
      if (/\b(?:WL-PROTECTED|WL-GENERATED|WL-META|WorldLedger Generated)\b/i.test(title)) return true;
      return false;
    },
    canGenerateTitle: function (title) {
      var t = WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.cleanTitle(title || '');
      if (!t || t.length < 3 || t.length > 80) return false;
      if (WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.isTrashTitle(t)) return false;
      if (WL.CardRegistry.isBroadTrigger(t)) return false;
      if (/\b(?:quest|task|mission|reward|item|thing|object|person|someone|something)\b/i.test(t)) return false;
      return true;
    }
  };

  WL.CardApiAdapter = {
    cards: function () { return WL.U.getStoryCards(WL.U.getGlobalState()); },
    hasWriteApi: function () { return typeof addStoryCard === 'function' && typeof updateStoryCard === 'function'; },
    cardText: function (card) { return WL.U.safeString((card && (card.entry || card.description || card.text || card.body || card.value)) || ''); },
    cardKeys: function (card) { return WL.U.safeString((card && (card.keys || card.key || card.title || card.name)) || ''); },
    findByMarker: function (marker) {
      var cards = WL.CardApiAdapter.cards();
      var out = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < cards.length; i++) {
        var hay = WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(cards[i]) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(cards[i]);
        if (hay.indexOf(marker) >= 0) out.push(i);
      }
      return out;
    },
    ensureSingleton: function (root, def, entry) {
      var marker = WL.CardRegistry.marker(def.id);
      var found = WL.CardApiAdapter.findByMarker(marker);
      if (!WL.CardApiAdapter.hasWriteApi()) return { status: 'API_UNAVAILABLE', applied: false, id: def.id };
      try {
        if (!found.length) {
          var idx = addStoryCard(def.keys, entry, 'world');
          if (idx === false) return { status: 'ADD_SKIPPED_DUP_KEYS', applied: false, id: def.id, index: idx };
          if (root.cards.protectedIds.indexOf(def.id) < 0) root.cards.protectedIds.push(def.id);
          return { status: 'ADDED', applied: true, id: def.id, index: idx };
        }
        var index = found[0];
        var old = WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[index] || {};
        if (WL.U.hash(WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(old)) === WL.U.hash(entry) && (WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(old) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(old)).indexOf(marker) >= 0) return { status: 'UNCHANGED', applied: false, id: def.id, index: index };
        updateStoryCard(index, def.keys, entry, old.type || 'world');
        if (found.length > 1) root.cards.warnings.push({ id: def.id, warning: 'duplicate_protected_card_markers_detected', count: found.length });
        WL.U.capArray(root.cards.warnings, 40);
        if (root.cards.protectedIds.indexOf(def.id) < 0) root.cards.protectedIds.push(def.id);
        return { status: 'UPDATED', applied: true, id: def.id, index: index };
      } catch (e) {
        root.cards.warnings.push({ id: def.id, warning: 'card_api_error', message: WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e) });
        WL.U.capArray(root.cards.warnings, 40);
        return { status: 'ERROR', applied: false, id: def.id, error: WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e) };
      }
    },
    ensureGeneratedSingleton: function (root, rec, entry) {
      var marker = WL.CardRegistry.generatedMarker(rec.id);
      var found = WL.CardApiAdapter.findByMarker(marker);
      if (!WL.CardApiAdapter.hasWriteApi()) return { status: 'API_UNAVAILABLE', applied: false, id: rec.id, generated: true };
      try {
        var keys = WL.CardRegistry.generatedKeys(rec.title, rec.id);
        if (!found.length) {
          var idx = addStoryCard(keys, entry, 'world');
          if (idx === false) return { status: 'ADD_SKIPPED_DUP_KEYS', applied: false, id: rec.id, generated: true };
          if (root.cards.generatedIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.cards.generatedIds.push(rec.id);
          return { status: 'GENERATED_ADDED', applied: true, id: rec.id, index: idx, generated: true };
        }
        var index = found[0];
        var old = WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[index] || {};
        if (WL.U.hash(WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(old)) === WL.U.hash(entry) && (WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(old) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(old)).indexOf(marker) >= 0) return { status: 'GENERATED_UNCHANGED', applied: false, id: rec.id, index: index, generated: true };
        updateStoryCard(index, keys, entry, old.type || 'world');
        if (root.cards.generatedIds.indexOf(rec.id) < 0) root.cards.generatedIds.push(rec.id);
        return { status: 'GENERATED_UPDATED', applied: true, id: rec.id, index: index, generated: true };
      } catch (e) {
        root.cards.warnings.push({ id: rec.id, warning: 'generated_card_api_error', message: WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e) });
        WL.U.capArray(root.cards.warnings, 40);
        return { status: 'ERROR', applied: false, id: rec.id, generated: true, error: WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e) };
      }
    }
  };

  WL.CardSync = {
    generatedCandidates: function (root, max) {
      if (WL.CardCandidateRegistry && typeof WL.CardCandidateRegistry.collect === 'function') return WL.CardCandidateRegistry.collect(root, max || 8);
      var out = [];
      var seen = {};
      function add(kind, id, title, summary) {
        title = WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.cleanTitle(title || '');
        id = id || ('generated_' + WL.U.slug(kind + '_' + title));
        if (!WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle(title)) return;
        if (seen[id]) return;
        seen[id] = true;
        out.push({ kind: kind, id: id, title: title, summary: summary || '' });
      }
      var actorIds = WL.U.unique((root.actors.presentIds || []).concat(root.actors.referencedIds || []));
      for (var a = 0; a < actorIds.length && out.length < (max || 8); a++) {
        var actor = root.actors.actorsById[actorIds[a]];
        if (actor && actor.name) add('actor', 'gen_actor_' + WL.U.slug(actor.name), actor.name, actor.summary || actor.status || 'Named actor remembered by WorldLedger.');
      }
      var placeKeys = Object.keys(root.places.placesById || {});
      for (var p = 0; p < placeKeys.length && out.length < (max || 8); p++) {
        var place = root.places.placesById[placeKeys[p]];
        if (place && place.name) add('place', 'gen_place_' + WL.U.slug(place.name), place.name, (place.type || 'place') + '; confirmed world structure mirror.');
      }
      var abilityIds = (root.abilities.abilityIds || []).concat(root.skills.skillIds || [], root.talents.talentIds || [], root.passives.passiveIds || []);
      for (var i = 0; i < abilityIds.length && out.length < (max || 8); i++) {
        var rec = root.abilities.abilitiesById[abilityIds[i]] || root.skills.skillsById[abilityIds[i]] || root.talents.talentsById[abilityIds[i]] || root.passives.passivesById[abilityIds[i]];
        if (rec && rec.name) add('ability', 'gen_ability_' + WL.U.slug(rec.name), rec.name, 'Ability/skill/passive mirror; mechanics remain reducer-owned.');
      }
      var itemIds = root.inventory.ownedIds || [];
      for (var it = 0; it < itemIds.length && out.length < (max || 8); it++) {
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[itemIds[it]];
        if (item && item.displayName) add('item', 'gen_item_' + WL.U.slug(item.displayName), item.displayName, 'Confirmed possessed item mirror; no card-prose grants.');
      }
      var questIds = (root.quests.activeIds || []).concat(root.quests.offeredIds || []);
      for (var q = 0; q < questIds.length && out.length < (max || 8); q++) {
        var quest = root.quests.questsById[questIds[q]];
        if (quest && quest.title) add('quest', 'gen_quest_' + WL.U.slug(quest.title), quest.title, WL.U.safeString(quest.objective || quest.status || 'Quest mirror.'));
      }
      return out.slice(0, max || 8);
    },
    flushGenerated: function (root, reason) {
      if (!root || !root.features || root.features.generatedCardsEnabled === false || root.features.generatedCardPolicyEnabled === false) return { status: 'GENERATED_DISABLED', applied: false, results: [] };
      if (root.features.storyCardWritesEnabled === false) return { status: 'WRITES_DISABLED', applied: false, results: [] };
      var candidates = WL.CardSync.generatedCandidates(root, 8);
      var createLimit = root.features.generatedCardCreatesPerTurn || 1;
      var updateLimit = root.features.generatedCardUpdatesPerTurn || 4;
      var maxGenerated = WL.U.clamp(root.features.maxGeneratedCards || 96, 0, 500);
      var created = 0, updated = 0, results = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
        var rec = candidates[i];
        var marker = WL.CardRegistry.generatedMarker(rec.id);
        var found = WL.CardApiAdapter.findByMarker(marker);
        if (!found.length && maxGenerated && (root.cards.generatedIds || []).length >= maxGenerated) {
