          var capRow = { status: 'GENERATED_CAP_REACHED', applied: false, id: rec.id, generated: true, cap: maxGenerated };
          root.cards.generatedCapWarnings.push({ id: rec.id, turn: root.runtime.currentTurn, cap: maxGenerated });
          WL.U.capArray(root.cards.generatedCapWarnings, 40);
          results.push(capRow);
          continue;
        }
        if (!found.length && created >= createLimit) continue;
        if (found.length && updated >= updateLimit) continue;
        var oldEntry = found.length ? WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[found[0]]) : '';
        var entry = WL.CardRenderer.renderGenerated(root, rec, oldEntry);
        var opKey = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['card', rec.id, WL.U.hash(entry), 'generated_write']);
        var currentMatches = false;
        if (found.length) {
          var currentCard = WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[found[0]] || {};
          currentMatches = WL.U.hash(WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(currentCard)) === WL.U.hash(entry) && (WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(currentCard) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(currentCard)).indexOf(marker) >= 0;
        }
        if (currentMatches && WL.OperationLedger.has(root, opKey)) { results.push({ status: 'DUPLICATE_SKIPPED', id: rec.id, generated: true }); continue; }
        var res = WL.CardApiAdapter.ensureGeneratedSingleton(root, rec, entry);
        if ((res.status === 'GENERATED_ADDED' || res.status === 'GENERATED_UPDATED' || res.status === 'GENERATED_UNCHANGED') && !WL.OperationLedger.has(root, opKey)) WL.OperationLedger.add(root, opKey, 'generated_card_write_success');
        if (res.status === 'GENERATED_ADDED') created++;
        if (res.status === 'GENERATED_UPDATED' || res.status === 'GENERATED_UNCHANGED') updated++;
        results.push(res);
      }
      return { status: 'GENERATED_FLUSHED', applied: true, results: results };
    },
    flushDirty: function (root, reason) {
      if (!root || !root.features || root.features.cardSyncEnabled === false || root.features.protectedCardsEnabled === false) return { status: 'DISABLED', applied: false };
      if (root.features.storyCardWritesEnabled === false) return { status: 'WRITES_DISABLED', applied: false };
      var defs = WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs;
      var dirtyTargets = root.dirty && root.dirty.targets || [];
      var meaningfulDirty = dirtyTargets.some(function (t) { return t && t !== 'diagnostics'; });
      var protectedReady = root.cards && root.cards.protectedIds && root.cards.protectedIds.length >= defs.length;
      if (!meaningfulDirty && protectedReady) {
        var cands = (root.features.generatedCardsEnabled !== false && WL.CardSync.generatedCandidates) ? WL.CardSync.generatedCandidates(root, 8) : [];
        var pendingGenerated = false;
        for (var c = 0; c < cands.length; c++) if (root.cards.generatedIds.indexOf(cands[c].id) < 0 && WL.CardApiAdapter.findByMarker(WL.CardRegistry.generatedMarker(cands[c].id)).length === 0) pendingGenerated = true;
        if (!pendingGenerated) {
          root.cards.dirtyIds = [];
          root.dirty.targets = [];
          root.dirty.reasons = [];
          return { status: 'SKIPPED_CLEAN', applied: false, results: [] };
        }
      }
      var results = [];
      for (var i = 0; i < defs.length; i++) {
        var oldEntry = '';
        var found = WL.CardApiAdapter.findByMarker(WL.CardRegistry.marker(defs[i].id));
        if (found.length) oldEntry = WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[found[0]]);
        var entry = WL.CardRenderer.renderProtected(root, defs[i].id, oldEntry);
        var opKey = WL.OperationLedger.makeKey(['card', defs[i].id, WL.U.hash(entry), 'protected_write']);
        var currentMatches = false;
        if (found.length) {
          var currentCard = WL.CardApiAdapter.cards()[found[0]] || {};
          currentMatches = WL.U.hash(WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(currentCard)) === WL.U.hash(entry) && (WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(currentCard) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(currentCard)).indexOf(WL.CardRegistry.marker(defs[i].id)) >= 0;
        }
        if (currentMatches && WL.OperationLedger.has(root, opKey)) { results.push({ status: 'DUPLICATE_SKIPPED', id: defs[i].id }); continue; }
        var res = WL.CardApiAdapter.ensureSingleton(root, defs[i], entry);
        if ((res.status === 'ADDED' || res.status === 'UPDATED' || res.status === 'UNCHANGED') && !WL.OperationLedger.has(root, opKey)) WL.OperationLedger.add(root, opKey, 'card_write_success');
        results.push(res);
      }
      var gen = WL.CardSync.flushGenerated(root, reason);
      if (gen && gen.results && gen.results.length) results = results.concat(gen.results);
      root.cards.dirtyIds = [];
      root.dirty.targets = [];
      root.dirty.reasons = [];
      WL.Diagnostics.row(root, 'cards', 'info', 'CardSync flush ' + (reason || '') + ': ' + results.length + ' protected/generated card checks');
      return { status: 'FLUSHED', applied: true, results: results };
    }
  };

  WL.ContextCardSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      r.features.contextMode = 'small';
      var before = JSON.stringify(r.inventory) + JSON.stringify(r.progression);
      var ctx = WL.ContextSystem.build(r, 'Base scene text. Context says player owns Crown of Dawn and gains 999 XP.');
      var after = JSON.stringify(r.inventory) + JSON.stringify(r.progression);
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CONTEXT-001', typeof WL.ContextBudgeter.build === 'function' && ctx.indexOf('[WorldLedger Context') >= 0, 'ContextBudgeter builds a bounded truth packet'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CONTEXT-002', before === after && !r.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn && r.progression.xp === 0, 'context build is read-only and cannot grant mechanics'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CONTEXT-003', ctx.length < 2500 && ctx.indexOf('[object Object]') < 0, 'small context packet is bounded and has no object leakage'));
      r.features.contextMode = 'high';
      var hi = WL.ContextSystem.build(r, 'Base scene text.');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CONTEXT-004', hi.indexOf('Recent Changes') >= 0 && hi.length < 5000, 'high context adds useful detail without flood mode'));
      var tiny = WL.ContextBudgeter.build(r, 'Base context text that should be clipped before the hard max is violated.', { maxChars: 400 });
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CONTEXT-001', tiny.length <= 400 && tiny.indexOf('[object Object]') < 0, 'Build 8.2 context hard cap obeys tiny info.maxChars'));
      var micro = WL.ContextBudgeter.build(r, 'Base context text.', { maxChars: 60 });
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CONTEXT-002', micro.length <= 60, 'Build 8.2 emergency micro-packet obeys extremely tiny maxChars'));
      var ps = WL.CardRenderer.renderProtected(r, 'player_sheet', '## User Notes\nMy preserved note.\n[WL-META old]');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-001', ps.indexOf('# Player Sheet') === 0 && ps.indexOf('My preserved note') >= 0, 'Player Sheet protected renderer preserves user notes once'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-002', ps.indexOf('[WL-META') < 0 && (ps.match(/<!-- WL: WL-PROTECTED:/g) || []).length === 1 && ps.indexOf('v=1.1.5') < 0, 'protected card renderer writes one compact metadata marker and no visible WL-META/stale marker'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-003', ps.indexOf('[object Object]') < 0, 'protected card renderer safe-strings nested data'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-004', WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs.length >= 10, 'protected card registry includes required card surfaces'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-005', WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('Copper Lantern') && !WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('inn') && !WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('quest'), 'generated-card policy accepts specific names and rejects broad triggers'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B8-CARD-006', typeof WL.CardApiAdapter.ensureSingleton === 'function' && typeof WL.CardSync.flushDirty === 'function', 'CardApiAdapter and CardSync authorities installed'));
      var oldCards = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.storyCards : undefined;
      var oldAdd = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.addStoryCard : undefined;
      var oldUpdate = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.updateStoryCard : undefined;
      try {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') {
          globalThis.storyCards = [];
          globalThis.addStoryCard = function () { return false; };
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = function () {};
          var failRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
          failRoot.features.storyCardWritesEnabled = true;
          WL.CardSync.flushDirty(failRoot, 'selftest_failed_add');
          var failKeys = Object.keys(failRoot.operationLedger.keysById || {}).length;
          globalThis.addStoryCard = function (keys, entry, type) { globalThis.storyCards.push({ keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' }); return globalThis.storyCards.length - 1; };
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = function (idx, keys, entry, type) { if (!globalThis.storyCards[idx]) throw new Error('missing card'); globalThis.storyCards[idx] = { keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' }; return true; };
          WL.CardSync.flushDirty(failRoot, 'selftest_retry_after_failed_add');
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CARD-001', failKeys === 0 && globalThis.storyCards.length === WL.CardRegistry.protectedDefs.length, 'Build 8.2 failed card adds do not poison ledger and remain retryable'));
          globalThis.storyCards = [];
          globalThis.addStoryCard = function (keys, entry, type) { globalThis.storyCards.push({ keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' }); return globalThis.storyCards.length - 1; };
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = function (idx, keys, entry, type) { if (!globalThis.storyCards[idx]) throw new Error('missing card'); globalThis.storyCards[idx] = { keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' }; return true; };
          var updateRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
          updateRoot.features.storyCardWritesEnabled = true;
          updateRoot.features.generatedCardsEnabled = false;
          WL.CardSync.flushDirty(updateRoot, 'selftest_initial_cards');
          updateRoot.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key = { id: 'item_brass_key', displayName: 'Brass Key', quantity: 1, stackable: false, condition: 'intact', custody: 'owned_by_player', sourceHistory: [] };
          updateRoot.inventory.ownedIds.push('item_brass_key');
          updateRoot.dirty.targets.push('inventory');
          var updateLedgerBefore = Object.keys(updateRoot.operationLedger.keysById || {}).length;
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = function () { throw new Error('mock update failure'); };
          WL.CardSync.flushDirty(updateRoot, 'selftest_update_failure');
          var updateLedgerAfterFail = Object.keys(updateRoot.operationLedger.keysById || {}).length;
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = function (idx, keys, entry, type) { if (!globalThis.storyCards[idx]) throw new Error('missing card'); globalThis.storyCards[idx] = { keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' }; return true; };
          updateRoot.dirty.targets.push('inventory');
          WL.CardSync.flushDirty(updateRoot, 'selftest_update_retry');
          var invCard = globalThis.storyCards.filter(function (c) { return String(c.keys || '').indexOf('Inventory') >= 0; })[0];
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CARD-002', updateLedgerAfterFail === updateLedgerBefore && invCard && String(invCard.entry || '').indexOf('Brass Key') >= 0, 'Build 8.2 failed card updates do not poison ledger and remain retryable'));
        } else {
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CARD-001', true, 'Build 8.2 card retry selftest skipped outside global runtime'));
        }
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B82-CARD-001', false, 'Build 8.2 card retry selftest threw: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') {
          globalThis.storyCards = oldCards;
          globalThis.addStoryCard = oldAdd;
          globalThis.updateStoryCard = oldUpdate;
        }
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };


  WL.LiveHotfix93BSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var preserveState = globalThis.state;
      var preserveCards = globalThis.storyCards;
      var preserveAdd = globalThis.addStoryCard;
      var preserveUpdate = globalThis.updateStoryCard;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        globalThis.storyCards = [];
        globalThis.addStoryCard = function (keys, entry, type) {
          for (var i = 0; i < globalThis.storyCards.length; i++) if (globalThis.storyCards[i].keys === keys) return false;
          globalThis.storyCards.push({ keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' });
          return globalThis.storyCards.length - 1;
        };
        globalThis.updateStoryCard = function (idx, keys, entry, type) {
          if (!globalThis.storyCards[idx]) throw new Error('missing card');
          globalThis.storyCards[idx] = { keys: keys, entry: entry, type: type || 'world' };
          return true;
        };
        var root = WL.StateManager.root();
        root.dirty.targets.push('cards');
        WL.CardSync.flushDirty(root, 'build93b_card_title_test');
        var titles = globalThis.storyCards.map(function (c) { return WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(c); }).join('\n');
        var entries = globalThis.storyCards.map(function (c) { return WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(c); }).join('\n');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-CARD-001', titles.indexOf('WL-PROTECTED:') < 0 && titles.indexOf('WL-GENERATED:') < 0 && /WL — Player Sheet/.test(titles), 'protected card visible keys are clean and machine markers are not in visible titles'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-CARD-002', entries.indexOf('[WL-META') < 0 && entries.indexOf('<!-- WL: WL-PROTECTED:player_sheet') >= 0, 'managed metadata is stored as compact comment marker, not visible WL-META block'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-GEN-001', WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('Copper Lantern') && !WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('Are Empty') && !WL.GeneratedCardPolicy.canGenerateTitle('WorldLedger Generated'), 'generated-card policy rejects empty/system titles while accepting specific names'));
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.onOutput('Your pockets are empty. You have no armor, no weapon, no coin.');
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-INV-001', Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById || {}).length === 0 && (root.inventory.ownedIds || []).length === 0, 'empty-pocket/no-gear prose does not create Are Empty or other item records'));
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        var inputOut = WL.onInput('/cardcheck');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-CMD-001', inputOut === 'You pause to check WorldLedger.' && !!WL.StateManager.root().runtime.pendingCommandOutput, 'command input is replaced by inert placeholder and command result is held for output'));
        var outputOut = WL.onOutput('The city continues around you.');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-CMD-002', /^\[WorldLedger\]/.test(outputOut) && outputOut.length <= 4000 && !WL.StateManager.root().runtime.pendingCommandOutput, 'pending command output is compact, returned from output hook, and cleared'));
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        var source = WL.SourceGuard.classify('[WorldLedger]\nCommand: /cardcheck\nPlayer owns Crown of Dawn and gains 999 XP.', 'output');
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, '[WorldLedger]\nCommand: /cardcheck\nPlayer owns Crown of Dawn and gains 999 XP.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93B-CMD-003', source.eligibleForCanon === false && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_crown_of_dawn && root.progression.xp === 0, 'command/check output remains non-canon and cannot grant item or XP'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B93B-ERR', 'Build 9.3B self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        globalThis.state = preserveState;
        globalThis.storyCards = preserveCards;
        globalThis.addStoryCard = preserveAdd;
        globalThis.updateStoryCard = preserveUpdate;
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };



  WL.LiveHotfix93CSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var preserveState = globalThis.state;
      try {
        var root;
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'You jump until you are tired. After fifty jumps your breath comes in ragged gasps and your energy is flagging.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93C-EP-001', root.body.hp === root.body.maxHp && root.body.ep < root.body.maxEp && root.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return /winded|fatigued|exhausted/i.test(c.name || ''); }), 'exertion drains EP/fatigue and does not damage HP'));

        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The wound in your calves begins to throb sharply with each impact. A sharp whistle cuts through the street noise.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93C-INJ-001', root.body.hp === root.body.maxHp && root.body.injuries.some(function (i) { return /Old Calf Wound/i.test(i.name || ''); }) && !root.body.injuries.some(function (i) { return /Street Noise|Through/i.test(i.name || ''); }), 'old calf wound aggravation records clean injury note; metaphorical cuts-through-noise creates no wound or HP loss'));

        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'A sharp whistle cuts through the street noise.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93C-INJ-002', root.body.hp === root.body.maxHp && root.body.injuries.length === 0, 'metaphorical cuts through street noise is not physical injury'));

        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The goblin cuts your arm and blood runs down your sleeve.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93C-INJ-003', root.body.hp < root.body.maxHp && root.body.injuries.some(function (i) { return /Arm Wound/i.test(i.name || ''); }), 'real physical cut still causes clean wound and HP pressure'));

        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'You sprint up the hill until you are winded, sweat soaking your tunic.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93C-EP-002', root.body.hp === root.body.maxHp && root.body.ep <= 90, 'sprinting/winded language routes to EP pressure, not HP damage'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B93C-ERR', 'Build 9.3C self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        globalThis.state = preserveState;
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D = {
    badItemSlugs: Object.freeze({ are_empty: 1, empty: 1, no_armor: 1, no_weapon: 1, no_coin: 1 }),
    isBadGeneratedItemName: function (name) {
      var s = WL.U.slug(name || '');
      if (WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.badItemSlugs[s]) return true;
      return /^(?:are\s+empty|empty|no\s+armor|no\s+weapon|no\s+coin)$/i.test(WL.U.norm(name || ''));
    },
    isBadInjuryName: function (name) {
      var n = WL.U.norm(name || '');
      if (!n) return false;
      return /^(?:Begins\s+in\s+Your\s+Calves\s+Wound|Through\s+the\s+Street\s+Noise\s+Wound)$/i.test(n) || /Street\s+Noise\s+Wound/i.test(n) || /^Begins\s+in\s+Your\b/i.test(n);
    },
    injuryDamageValue: function (inj) {
      var dmg = Number(inj && inj.hpDamage || 0);
      if (isFinite(dmg) && dmg > 0) return WL.U.clamp(dmg, 0, 999);
      var sev = WL.U.lower(inj && inj.severity || 'minor');
      if (sev === 'severe') return 16;
      if (sev === 'moderate') return 8;
      return 4;
    },
    ensureCondition: function (root, name, severity) {
      if (!root.body.conditions || !Array.isArray(root.body.conditions)) root.body.conditions = [];
      var id = 'condition_' + WL.U.slug(name);
      for (var i = 0; i < root.body.conditions.length; i++) {
        if (root.body.conditions[i].id === id || WL.U.slug(root.body.conditions[i].name || '') === WL.U.slug(name)) {
          root.body.conditions[i].severity = root.body.conditions[i].severity || severity || 'minor';
          root.body.conditions[i].status = 'active';
          root.body.conditions[i].cleanup93D = true;
          return root.body.conditions[i];
        }
      }
      var rec = { id: id, name: name, severity: severity || 'minor', status: 'active', phrase: 'Build 9.3D cleanup of known 9.2 exertion pollution', cleanup93D: true };
      root.body.conditions.push(rec);
      WL.U.capArray(root.body.conditions, 40);
      return rec;
    },
    removeBadInventory: function (root, summary) {
      var ids = Object.keys(root.inventory.itemsById || {});
      for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        var id = ids[i];
        var item = root.inventory.itemsById[id] || {};
        if (!WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.isBadGeneratedItemName(item.displayName || item.name || id)) continue;
        delete root.inventory.itemsById[id];
        summary.removedItems.push(id);
      }
      root.inventory.ownedIds = (root.inventory.ownedIds || []).filter(function (id) { return !!root.inventory.itemsById[id]; });
      root.inventory.log = (root.inventory.log || []).filter(function (r) { return !WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.isBadGeneratedItemName((r && (r.name || r.itemId)) || ''); });
    },
    removeBadInjuries: function (root, summary) {
      var kept = [];
      var restored = 0;
      var calf = false;
      var injuries = root.body.injuries || [];
      for (var i = 0; i < injuries.length; i++) {
        var inj = injuries[i] || {};
        if (WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.isBadInjuryName(inj.name || '')) {
          restored += WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.injuryDamageValue(inj);
          summary.removedInjuries.push(inj.name || 'unknown');
          if (/Calves/i.test(inj.name || '') || /calves/i.test(inj.phrase || '')) calf = true;
        } else kept.push(inj);
      }
      root.body.injuries = kept;
      if (restored > 0) {
        root.body.hp = Math.min(root.body.maxHp, Number(root.body.hp || 0) + restored);
        summary.hpRestored += restored;
      }
      if (calf) {
        root.body.ep = Math.min(Number(root.body.ep || root.body.maxEp), Math.max(0, Number(root.body.maxEp || 100) - 28));
        WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.ensureCondition(root, 'Winded', 'minor');
        summary.epAdjusted = true;
      }
    },
    removeBadCardState: function (root, summary) {
      root.cards.generatedIds = (root.cards.generatedIds || []).filter(function (id) {
        var bad = /gen_item_are_empty|are_empty/i.test(id || '');
        if (bad) summary.removedCardIds.push(id);
        return !bad;
      });
      var keys = Object.keys(root.operationLedger.keysById || {});
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        if (/gen_item_are_empty|are_empty/i.test(keys[i])) {
          delete root.operationLedger.keysById[keys[i]];
          summary.removedLedgerKeys += 1;
        }
      }
      root.operationLedger.recentKeys = (root.operationLedger.recentKeys || []).filter(function (k) { return !/gen_item_are_empty|are_empty/i.test(k || ''); });
    },
    removeBadStoryCards: function (summary) {
      var cards = WL.U.getStoryCards(WL.U.getGlobalState());
      if (!Array.isArray(cards) || !cards.length) return;
      for (var i = cards.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        var c = cards[i] || {};
        var hay = WL.CardApiAdapter.cardKeys(c) + '\n' + WL.CardApiAdapter.cardText(c);
        var bad = /gen_item_are_empty|#\s*Are\s+Empty\b|WL-GENERATED:gen_item_are_empty/i.test(hay) || (/\bAre\s+Empty\b/i.test(hay) && /Confirmed possessed item mirror/i.test(hay));
        if (!bad) continue;
        var removed = false;
        try { if (typeof removeStoryCard === 'function') { removeStoryCard(i); removed = true; } } catch (e) {}
        if (!removed) {
          try { cards.splice(i, 1); removed = true; } catch (e2) {}
        }
        summary.removedStoryCards += removed ? 1 : 0;
      }
    },
    run: function (root, stateObj) {
      if (!root || !root.features || root.features.livePollutionCleanup93DEnabled === false) return { applied: false, reason: 'disabled' };
      if (!root.cleanup || typeof root.cleanup !== 'object') root.cleanup = { lastRunTurn: 0, notes: [] };
      if (!Array.isArray(root.cleanup.notes)) root.cleanup.notes = [];
      var summary = { applied: false, removedItems: [], removedInjuries: [], removedCardIds: [], removedStoryCards: 0, removedLedgerKeys: 0, hpRestored: 0, epAdjusted: false };
      WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.removeBadInventory(root, summary);
      WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.removeBadInjuries(root, summary);
      WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.removeBadCardState(root, summary);
      WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.removeBadStoryCards(summary);
      summary.applied = !!(summary.removedItems.length || summary.removedInjuries.length || summary.removedCardIds.length || summary.removedStoryCards || summary.removedLedgerKeys || summary.hpRestored || summary.epAdjusted);
      if (summary.applied) {
        root.cleanup.lastRunTurn = root.runtime && root.runtime.currentTurn || WL.U.getActionCount();
        root.cleanup.notes.push({ turn: root.cleanup.lastRunTurn, kind: 'build93d_known_live_pollution_cleanup', summary: summary });
        WL.U.capArray(root.cleanup.notes, 20);
        if (root.dirty && Array.isArray(root.dirty.targets)) {
          if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf('cards') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('cards');
          if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf('body') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('body');
          if (root.dirty.targets.indexOf('inventory') < 0) root.dirty.targets.push('inventory');
        }
        try { WL.Diagnostics.row(root, 'cleanup', 'info', 'Build 9.3D removed known 9.2 live pollution', summary); } catch (e) {}
      }
      WL.StateManager.enforceInvariants(root);
      return summary;
    }
  };

  WL.LiveHotfix93DSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var preserveState = globalThis.state;
      var preserveCards = globalThis.storyCards;
      var preserveRemove = globalThis.removeStoryCard;
      try {
        globalThis.state = {};
        globalThis.storyCards = [{ keys: 'Are Empty, WorldLedger Generated, WL-GENERATED:gen_item_are_empty', entry: '# Are Empty\n\nKind: item\nSummary: Confirmed possessed item mirror; no card-prose grants.\n<!-- WL: WL-GENERATED:gen_item_are_empty -->', type: 'world' }];
        globalThis.removeStoryCard = null;
        var root = WL.StateManager.root();
        root.inventory.itemsById.item_are_empty = { id: 'item_are_empty', displayName: 'Are Empty', quantity: 1, sourceHistory: [] };
        root.inventory.ownedIds.push('item_are_empty');
        root.cards.generatedIds.push('gen_item_are_empty');
        root.operationLedger.keysById.card_gen_item_are_empty_generated_write = { key: 'card_gen_item_are_empty_generated_write' };
        root.operationLedger.recentKeys.push('card_gen_item_are_empty_generated_write');
        root.body.hp = 88;
        root.body.ep = 100;
        root.body.injuries.push({ id: 'injury_begins_in_your_calves_wound', name: 'Begins in Your Calves Wound', severity: 'moderate', hpDamage: 8, phrase: 'The wound in your calves begins to throb sharply with each impact.' });
        root.body.injuries.push({ id: 'injury_through_the_street_noise_wound', name: 'Through the Street Noise Wound', severity: 'minor', hpDamage: 4, phrase: 'A sharp whistle cuts through the street noise.' });
        root.body.injuries.push({ id: 'injury_arm_wound', name: 'Arm Wound', severity: 'minor', hpDamage: 4, phrase: 'The goblin cuts your arm.' });
        var res = WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.run(root, globalThis.state);
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-001', res.applied && !root.inventory.itemsById.item_are_empty && root.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf('item_are_empty') < 0, 'known false Are Empty item is removed from inventory'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-002', root.body.injuries.length === 1 && /Arm Wound/i.test(root.body.injuries[0].name || '') && !root.body.injuries.some(function (i) { return /Calves|Street Noise|Begins in Your/i.test(i.name || ''); }), 'known false injuries are removed while real Arm Wound remains'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-003', root.body.hp === 100 && root.body.ep === 72 && root.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return /Winded/i.test(c.name || ''); }), 'false HP loss is restored and exertion pollution becomes EP/fatigue state'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-004', root.cards.generatedIds.indexOf('gen_item_are_empty') < 0 && !root.operationLedger.keysById.card_gen_item_are_empty_generated_write && globalThis.storyCards.length === 0, 'known generated card id, ledger key, and Story Card are removed'));
        var before = JSON.stringify({ inv: root.inventory, body: root.body, cards: root.cards, keys: root.operationLedger.keysById, sc: globalThis.storyCards });
        WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.run(root, globalThis.state);
        var after = JSON.stringify({ inv: root.inventory, body: root.body, cards: root.cards, keys: root.operationLedger.keysById, sc: globalThis.storyCards });
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-005', before === after, 'cleanup is idempotent on repeated runs'));
        globalThis.state = {};
        root = WL.StateManager.root();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The goblin cuts your arm and blood runs down your sleeve.', 'output');
        var hp = root.body.hp;
        var count = root.body.injuries.length;
        WL.LivePollutionCleanup93D.run(root, globalThis.state);
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B93D-CLEAN-006', root.body.hp === hp && root.body.injuries.length === count && root.body.injuries.some(function (i) { return /Arm Wound/i.test(i.name || ''); }), 'normal real injury state is not cleaned away'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B93D-ERR', 'Build 9.3D self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        globalThis.state = preserveState;
        globalThis.storyCards = preserveCards;
        globalThis.removeStoryCard = preserveRemove;
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };

      } finally {

        globalThis.storyCards = preserveCards;
      }

    }
  };



  WL.ContractLabor96ASelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var preserveState = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.state : undefined;
      try {
        var r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You hire Bran for 2 silver per day to repair the north wall.', 'output');
        var ids = r.contracts.contractIds || [];
        var cid = ids[0];
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-CONTRACT-001', !!cid && r.contracts.contractsById[cid].status === 'active' && /repair the north wall/i.test(r.contracts.contractsById[cid].work || ''), 'hiring Bran creates active contract with party, wage, obligation, and status'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-LABOR-001', r.labor.employeeIds.length === 1 && /Bran/i.test(r.labor.employeesById[r.labor.employeeIds[0]].actorName || '') && r.labor.assignments.length >= 1, 'hiring creates worker/employee record and assignment evidence'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-NOQUEST-001', Object.keys(r.quests.questsById || {}).length === 0 && Object.keys(r.inventory.itemsById || {}).length === 0, 'contract/labor prose does not leak into quests or inventory items'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        r.inventory.currency.silver = 5;
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You hire Bran for 2 silver per day to repair the north wall.', 'output');
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "You pay Bran 2 silver for the day's work.", 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-PAY-001', r.contracts.payments.length === 1 && r.contracts.payments[0].amount === 2 && r.inventory.currency.silver === 3, 'contract payment evidence records payment and normal inventory currency spend happens once when funds exist'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You promise Bran 2 silver per day to guard the shop.', 'output');
        var pc = r.contracts.contractsById[r.contracts.contractIds[0]];
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-PROMISE-001', pc && pc.status === 'promised' && r.inventory.currency.silver === 0 && r.labor.employeeIds.length === 0, 'promise creates terms/promised contract only; it does not pay or employ until confirmed'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Bran refuses unpaid work.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-REFUSAL-001', r.labor.refusals.length === 1 && r.contracts.contractIds.length === 0, 'refusal records labor evidence without creating a contract'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You hire Bran for 2 silver per day to repair the north wall.', 'output');
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Bran breaches the contract and leaves the job.', 'output');
        var bc = r.contracts.contractsById[r.contracts.contractIds[0]];
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-BREACH-001', bc && bc.status === 'breached' && r.contracts.breaches.length === 1, 'breach updates existing contract status and breach ledger'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira agrees to work on the north wall for 2 silver per day.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-MIRA-001', r.contracts.contractIds.length === 1 && r.labor.employeeIds.length === 1 && /Mira/i.test(r.labor.employeesById[r.labor.employeeIds[0]].actorName || ''), 'worker-agreement phrasing creates contract and labor record'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Old Bren asks you to clear the wolves from his farm and promises 15 gold.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-QUEST-001', Object.keys(r.quests.questsById || {}).length === 1 && r.contracts.contractIds.length === 0, 'quest offer remains quest/reward promise, not contract/labor record'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You hire Bran for 2 silver per day to repair the north wall.', 'output');
        var ccmd = WL.CommandRouter.route('/contracts', r);
        var lcmd = WL.CommandRouter.route('/labor', r);
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-CMD-001', /Command:\s*\/contracts/.test(ccmd) && /Bran/.test(ccmd) && /Command:\s*\/labor/.test(lcmd) && /Employees/.test(lcmd), '/contracts and /labor commands expose compact read-only surfaces'));
        var before = JSON.stringify({ contracts: r.contracts, labor: r.labor, inventory: r.inventory, quests: r.quests });
        WL.CommandRouter.route('/contracts', r); WL.CommandRouter.route('/labor', r);
        var after = JSON.stringify({ contracts: r.contracts, labor: r.labor, inventory: r.inventory, quests: r.quests });
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96A-CMD-002', before === after, 'contract/labor commands are read-only'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B96A-ERR', 'Build 9.6A self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') globalThis.state = preserveState;
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.DomainProject96B1SelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var preserveState = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.state : undefined;
      try {
        var r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Construction begins on the north wall using 20 stone and 5 timber.', 'output');
        var p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-PROJECT-001', !!p && p.status === 'started' && p.requirements.materials.length >= 2, 'confirmed construction start creates domain project with material requirement/allocation evidence'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-NOASSET-001', !r.advanced.domain.assetsById && (!r.advanced.domain.readyAssetIds || r.advanced.domain.readyAssetIds.length === 0), 'project start does not create holding/asset/tax state'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The north wall requires 40 stone, 20 timber, and 3 days.', 'output');
        p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-REQ-001', !!p && p.requirements.materials.length >= 2 && p.requirements.time && p.requirements.time.amount === 3, 'requirements record materials and time without progress'));

        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The crew makes 25% progress on the north wall after consuming 10 stone and 5 timber over 1 day.', 'output');
        p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-PROGRESS-001', !!p && p.progressPercent === 25 && p.consumedMaterials.length >= 2 && p.timeSpent.length >= 1, 'progress records percent plus consumed materials and elapsed work time'));

        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira works on the north wall.', 'output');
        p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-ASSIGN-001', !!p && p.workers.indexOf('Mira') >= 0 && r.advanced.domain.assignments.length >= 1, 'named worker assignment links worker to domain project'));

        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The north wall stalls because the crew runs out of timber.', 'output');
        p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-BLOCK-001', !!p && p.status === 'blocked' && p.blocked.length >= 1, 'blocked/stalled project state records reason without fake completion'));

        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The crew completes the north wall after using 30 stone and 15 timber over 2 days.', 'output');
        p = r.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall;
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-COMPLETE-001', !!p && p.status === 'completion_evidence_pending_asset_conversion' && p.progressPercent === 100 && r.advanced.domain.readyAssetIds.indexOf(p.id) >= 0, 'completion evidence marks project ready for later 9.6C asset validation'));

        var r2 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r2, 'You want to build a castle someday.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-DENY-001', (r2.advanced.domain.projectIds || []).length === 0, 'wish/plan language does not create domain project'));
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r2, 'Old Bren asks you to clear the wolves from his farm and promises 15 gold.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-QUEST-001', Object.keys(r2.quests.questsById || {}).length === 1 && (r2.advanced.domain.projectIds || []).length === 0, 'quest offer remains quest and does not create project'));

        var cmd = WL.CommandRouter.route('/domain', r);
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-CMD-001', /Command:\s*\/domain/.test(cmd) && /North Wall/.test(cmd) && /Domain Projects/.test(cmd), '/domain command exposes compact project surface'));
        var before = JSON.stringify({ domain: r.advanced.domain, inventory: r.inventory, contracts: r.contracts, labor: r.labor });
        WL.CommandRouter.route('/domain', r); WL.CommandRouter.route('/projects', r);
        var after = JSON.stringify({ domain: r.advanced.domain, inventory: r.inventory, contracts: r.contracts, labor: r.labor });
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B1-CMD-002', before === after, 'domain/project commands are read-only'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B96B1-ERR', 'Build 9.6B1 self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      } finally {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') globalThis.state = preserveState;
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };


  // ============================================================
  // 10. CommandRouter and CommandRenderer
  // ============================================================
  WL.EconomyTransaction96B2SelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var r, cmd, ctx, card;
      try {
        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The Crystal Saber costs 40 gold.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-QUOTE-001', r.economy.priceQuotes.length === 1 && r.economy.priceQuotes[0].itemName === 'Crystal Saber' && r.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0, 'item price quote records economy evidence and grants no item'));
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-QUOTE-002', r.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.length === 1, 'legacy advanced economy mirror receives one quote for backward checks'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You try to buy the Crystal Saber for 40 gold, but you cannot afford it.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-FAIL-001', r.economy.failedPurchases.length === 1 && r.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0 && r.inventory.currency.gold === 0, 'failed purchase records denial with no item and no spend'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The innkeeper charges 5 copper for a room for the night.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-SERVICE-001', r.economy.serviceQuotes.length === 1 && /room/i.test(r.economy.serviceQuotes[0].serviceName || ''), 'service quote records service evidence without completion'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You owe 2 silver to Bran for wages.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-DEBT-001', r.economy.debtIds.length === 1 && r.economy.debtsById[r.economy.debtIds[0]].debtKind === 'wage' && r.inventory.currency.silver === 0, 'wage debt records obligation without paying currency'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        r.inventory.currency.silver = 5;
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You pay Bran 2 silver in wages.', 'output');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-WAGE-001', r.economy.wagePayments.length === 1 && r.inventory.currency.silver === 3, 'wage payment records economy evidence while inventory spend happens once'));

        r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Merchant says the Crystal Saber costs 40 gold.', 'output');
        WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You owe 2 silver to Bran for wages.', 'output');
        cmd = WL.CommandRenderer.economy(r);
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-CMD-001', /Command:\s*\/economy/.test(cmd) && /quotes/.test(cmd) && /debts/.test(cmd), '/economy command exposes compact economy surface'));
        ctx = WL.ContextBudgeter.renderPacket(r, 'medium');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-CTX-001', /Economy:/.test(ctx) && /quotes/.test(ctx), 'context packet includes compact economy surface'));
        card = WL.CardRenderer.renderProtected(r, 'people_factions', '');
        rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B96B2-CARD-001', /Economy:/.test(card), 'People & Factions card includes economy summary surface'));
      } catch (e) {
        rows.push(WL.Assert.fail('B96B2-ERR', 'Build 9.6B2 economy self-test exception: ' + WL.U.safeString(e && e.message || e)));
      }
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.CommandRouter = {
    compactOutput: function (root, text) {
      var cap = WL.U.clamp((root && root.features && root.features.liveCommandOutputMaxChars) || WL.Constants.maxCommandOutputChars || 3600, 800, 4000);
      var out = String(text == null ? '' : text);
      if (out.length <= cap) return out;
      return out.slice(0, Math.max(0, cap - 140)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, '') + '\n[WorldLedger: command output compacted for live AI Dungeon limits. Use specific check commands for narrower detail.]\n';
    },
    route: function (text, root) {
      if (!root.features.commandsEnabled) return text;
      var name = WL.U.commandName(text);
      var renderer = WL.CommandRenderer[name] || WL.CommandRenderer.unknown;
      return WL.CommandRouter.compactOutput(root, renderer(root, WL.U.commandArgs(text), name));
    }
  };

  WL.CommandRenderer = {
    header: function (name, status) {
      return '[WorldLedger]\nCommand: /' + name + '\nStatus: ' + (status || 'INFO') + '\n';
    },
    unknown: function (root, args, name) {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header(name || 'unknown', 'UNKNOWN') + 'Unknown command. Use /help.\n';
    },
    help: function () {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('help', 'BUILD_PHASE_ONLY_NOT_RELEASE') +
        'WorldLedger Clean Core Build 9.6B2 is the EconomyTransactionSystem foundation built on 9.6B1 domain projects and the 9.6A contract lifecycle + labor foundation. It preserves faction/law/enforcement response foundations on top of 9.5F crime event and evidence/clue foundations, 9.5E rumor propagation, 9.5D scoped reputation/local standing, 9.5C witness ledger, 9.5B presence/knowledge foundation, 9.4B identity foundation, 9.4C ability cost/development matrix, and 9.4D surfaces. Previous 9.3F restored common live read commands after the 9.3E audit: /sheet, /status, and /location. Gameplay remains owned by the scanner/proposal/validator/reducer/card/context authorities.\n' +
        'Useful commands: /version, /sheet, /status, /location, /build96b2check, /economy, /prices, /debts, /services, /build96b1check, /domain, /projects, /domaincheck, /build96acheck, /contracts, /labor, /employees, /contractcheck, /build95hcheck, /social, /socialcheck, /build95gcheck, /law, /enforcement, /lawcheck, /build95fcheck, /crimes, /clues, /crimecheck, /build95echeck, /rumors, /rumorcheck, /build95dcheck, /reputation, /standing, /build95ccheck, /witnesses, /build95bcheck, /socialfoundation, /surfaces, /identitysurface, /abilitysurface, /build94dcheck, /surfacecheck, /costs, /costmatrix, /build94ccheck, /cardcandidates, /worldgraph, /identitycheck, /build94bcheck, /cardcheck, /sourceaudit, /releasecheck, /inventory, /quests, /actors, /places, /body, /abilities, /build93bcheck, /build93ccheck, /build93dcheck, /build93fcheck. Live command output is compacted so checks do not overflow the AI Dungeon action limit.\n' +
        'Pipeline remains active: scanner -> proposal -> validator -> reducer -> ledger -> dirty/diagnostics. 9.5H verifies the 9.5 social-consequence stack and adds read-only social integration surfaces only. It does not make law omniscient, does not auto-punish, does not spend fines from your inventory, and now implements contract/labor foundations, DomainProjectSystem MVP, and EconomyTransactionSystem evidence for services/shops/wages/debts/failed purchases without implementing 9.6C holdings/taxes/upkeep, summons, or combat-matrix systems yet.\n';
    },
    version: function () {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('version', 'BUILD_PHASE_ONLY_NOT_RELEASE') +
        'Project: ' + WL.PROJECT_NAME + '\n' +
        'Build: ' + WL.BUILD_VERSION + '\n' +
        'Stage: ' + WL.BUILD_STAGE + '\n' +
        'Status note: Build 9.6B2 is a behavior build, not a final release promotion. It adds reducer-owned economy transactions for prices, services, wages, debts, and failed purchases without item grants from quotes or automatic service completion.\n';
    },

Presence, knowledge, witness ledger, scoped reputation/local standing, rumor propagation, crime/clue evidence, and explicit law/enforcement response foundations are active. All surfaces are read-only and non-omniscient.'];

      lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.knowledgeSummary(root));
      if (root.features.witnessEligibilityLedgerEnabled !== false) lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.witnessSummary(root));
      if (root.features.reputationLocalStandingEnabled !== false) lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.reputationSummary(root));
      if (root.features.rumorPropagationEnabled !== false) lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.rumorSummary(root));
      if (root.features.crimeEvidenceClueFoundationEnabled !== false) { lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.crimeSummary(root)); lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.clueSummary(root)); }
      if (root.features.factionLawResponseFoundationEnabled !== false) lines = lines.concat(WL.SocialConsequenceSystem.lawSummary(root));
      return lines.join('\n') + '\n';

Rumor is not proof; witness is not punishment; law response requires story confirmation.'];

      return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    owners: function () {
      var lines = [WL.CommandRenderer.header('owners', 'INFO') + 'Owner manifest:'];
      var keys = Object.keys(WL.OwnerManifest);
      for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
        var k = keys[i];
        lines.push('- ' + k + ': ' + WL.OwnerManifest[k].owner + ' / ' + WL.OwnerManifest[k].status);
      }
      return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    inventory: function (root) {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('inventory', 'INFO') + WL.InventoryEquipmentSystem.summary(root).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    quests: function (root) {
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('quests', 'INFO') + WL.QuestObjectiveRewardSystem.summary(root).join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    sheet: function (root) {
      var p = root.player || {};
      var b = root.body || {};
      var stableAbilities = root.abilities && root.abilities.abilityIds ? root.abilities.abilityIds.length : 0;
      var activeQuests = root.quests && root.quests.activeIds ? root.quests.activeIds.length : 0;
      var carried = root.inventory && root.inventory.ownedIds ? root.inventory.ownedIds.length : 0;
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Name: ' + WL.U.safeString(p.name || 'Unknown'));
      lines.push('Race / True Race / Current Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(p.raceSpecies || 'unknown') + ' / ' + WL.U.safeString(p.trueRace || p.raceSpecies || 'unknown') + ' / ' + WL.U.safeString(p.currentForm || p.raceSpecies || 'unknown'));
      lines.push('Age: ' + WL.U.safeString(p.age || 'unknown'));
      lines.push('Appearance: ' + WL.U.safeString(p.appearance || 'unspecified'));
      lines.push('Progression: Level ' + WL.U.safeString(p.level || 1) + ' | XP ' + WL.U.safeString(p.xp || 0) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(p.xpToNext || 100));
      lines.push('HP/MP/EP: ' + WL.U.safeString(b.hp) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxHp) + ' | ' + WL.U.safeString(b.mp) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxMp) + ' | ' + WL.U.safeString(b.ep) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxEp));
      lines.push('Carried Items: ' + carried);
      lines.push('Stable Abilities: ' + stableAbilities);
      lines.push('Active Quests: ' + activeQuests);
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('sheet', 'INFO') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    status: function (root) {
      var b = root.body || {};
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('HP/MP/EP: ' + WL.U.safeString(b.hp) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxHp) + ' | ' + WL.U.safeString(b.mp) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxMp) + ' | ' + WL.U.safeString(b.ep) + '/' + WL.U.safeString(b.maxEp));
      lines.push('Fatigue: ' + WL.U.safeString(b.fatigue || 'normal'));
      lines.push('Conditions: ' + ((b.conditions || []).map(function (c) { return c.name || c.id || String(c); }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Injuries: ' + ((b.injuries || []).map(function (i) { return i.name || i.id || String(i); }).join(', ') || '(none)'));
      lines.push('Death Boundary: ' + WL.U.safeString(b.deathBoundary || 'alive'));
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('status', 'INFO') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    location: function (root) {
      var p = root.places || {};
      var t = root.time || {};
      var current = p.currentId && p.placesById && p.placesById[p.currentId] ? (p.placesById[p.currentId].name || p.currentId) : (p.currentId || '(unknown)');
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Current Place: ' + WL.U.safeString(current));
      var hh = WL.U.safeString(t.hour == null ? 8 : t.hour);
      var mm = WL.U.safeString(t.minute == null ? 0 : t.minute);
      if (hh.length < 2) hh = '0' + hh;
      if (mm.length < 2) mm = '0' + mm;
      lines.push('Current Time: Day ' + WL.U.safeString(t.day || 1) + ' ' + hh + ':' + mm);
      lines.push('Known Places: ' + (p.placesById ? Object.keys(p.placesById).length : 0));
      if (p.routeId) lines.push('Route: ' + WL.U.safeString(p.routeId));
      return WL.CommandRenderer.header('location', 'INFO') + lines.join('\n') + '\n';
    },
    questcheck: function (root) {
      return WL.SourceAudit.render('questcheck', WL.QuestWorldRelationshipSelfTest.rows().filter(function (r) { return /^QUEST-/.test(r.id); }), 'Quest lifecycle/objective binding/no-inventory-leak tests.');
    },

    },
    profile: function (root) {
      var lines = [];
      lines.push('Name: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.name));
      lines.push('Pronouns: ' + (root.player.pronouns || '(unknown)'));
      lines.push('Age: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.age));
      lines.push('Race / Species: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.raceSpecies));
      lines.push('True Race: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.trueRace));
      lines.push('Current Form: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.currentForm));
      lines.push('Appearance: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.appearance));
      lines.push('Origin / Class / Profession: ' + [root.player.origin, root.player.classLabel, root.player.profession].filter(Boolean).join(' / '));
      lines.push('Race Source: ' + WL.U.safeString(root.player.raceProfile && root.player.raceProfile.source));
      if (root.player.raceProfile && root.player.raceProfile.sourceTitle) lines.push('Race Card: ' + root.player.raceProfile.sourceTitle);
