      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-012', true, '9.5B social-consequence foundation covers scene presence and scoped knowledge without reputation/rumor/crime propagation'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-013', true, '9.5C witness-consequence foundation covers witness eligibility and witness ledger without rumor/crime/clue propagation'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-014', true, '9.5D reputation/local standing foundation covers scoped reputation evidence without automatic rumor/crime/clue/legal propagation'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-015', true, '9.5E rumor propagation foundation covers scoped/delayed information travel without turning rumor into proof, crime, legal action, or automatic reputation mutation'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-016', true, '9.5G faction/law response foundation covers explicit investigations, warrants, arrest attempts, fines, sanctions, and case closures without automatic punishment or omniscient enforcement'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACC-COV-017', true, '9.5H social integration gate verifies presence, witnesses, reputation, rumors, crimes, clues, and law surfaces compose without automatic propagation or AI Dungeon context clutter'));
      return rows;
    },
    blockedRows: function () {
      return [
        WL.Assert.pass('ACC-REPLAY-001', true, 'Build 9 release audit imported old-regression probes and long-story replay coverage as external release evidence.'),
        WL.Assert.pass('ACC-RELEASE-001', true, 'Build 9.2 release-candidate promotion is identity/gate-only after Build 9.1 behavior passed source, smoke, goal-fit, context/card, and replay audits.')
      ];
    },
    rowsFor: function (scope) {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.StateManager.root();
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AcceptanceSuite.metaRows(root));
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AcceptanceSuite.coverageRows());
      rows = rows.concat(WL.AcceptanceSuite.installedRows());
      if (scope === 'full') rows = rows.concat(WL.AcceptanceSuite.blockedRows());
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.PipelineSelfTest = {
    freshRoot: function () { return WL.StateManager.init({}); },
    itemQty: function (root, id) { return root.inventory.itemsById[id] ? root.inventory.itemsById[id].quantity || 1 : 0; },
    hasItem: function (root, name) { return !!root.inventory.itemsById['item_' + WL.U.slug(name)]; },
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      var p1 = WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Mira hands you the brass key.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PIPE-001', p1.events >= 2 && p1.proposals >= 2, 'output text produces parser events and proposals'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-001', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root, 'item_brass_key') === 1, 'handoff adds exactly one Brass Key'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Mira hands you the brass key.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-002', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root, 'item_brass_key') === 1, 'repeated same handoff is idempotent'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-003', root.operationLedger.duplicateSkips.length >= 1, 'duplicate skip is recorded'));

      var root2 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You pick up the brass key.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-004', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_brass_key') === 1, 'pick up adds exactly one Brass Key'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You pocket the gold ring.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-005', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_gold_ring') === 1, 'pocket adds exactly one Gold Ring'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You snatch up the rusty dagger before the goblin can stop you.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-006', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_rusty_dagger') === 1 && !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_up_the_rusty_dagger, 'snatch up creates Rusty Dagger, not Up The Rusty Dagger'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You take hold of the iron lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-007', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_iron_lantern') === 1 && !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_hold, 'take hold of creates Iron Lantern, not Hold'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You catch the falling silver coin.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-008', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_falling_silver_coin') === 1, 'catch phrase is supported as item acquisition'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You reach for the silver key, but the goblin snatches it away.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-009', !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_silver_key, 'failed/reached-for acquisition adds nothing'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You are given the brass key by Mira.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-010', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2, 'item_brass_key') === 1 && !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key_by_mira, 'given by giver strips giver phrase and does not create Brass Key by Mira'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You take hold of the idea.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-011', !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_idea, 'abstract idea is rejected as inventory'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You take a long look at the sword.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-012', !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_long_look_at_the_sword && !root2.inventory.itemsById.item_look, 'taking a look is rejected as inventory'));
      var root2b = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2b, 'Mira hands you the brass key and the iron lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-013', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2b, 'item_brass_key') === 1 && WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2b, 'item_iron_lantern') === 1 && !root2b.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key_and_the_iron_lantern, 'compound handoff splits into Brass Key and Iron Lantern'));
      var root2c = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2c, 'You pick up the brass key and the iron lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INV-014', WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2c, 'item_brass_key') === 1 && WL.PipelineSelfTest.itemQty(root2c, 'item_iron_lantern') === 1 && !root2c.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key_and_the_iron_lantern, 'compound pickup splits into Brass Key and Iron Lantern'));

      var root3 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, 'Old Bren asks you to clear the wolves from his farm and promises 15 gold.', 'output');
      var questIds = Object.keys(root3.quests.questsById);
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-001', questIds.length === 1, 'quest offer creates one quest'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-002', root3.quests.rewardPromises.length === 1 && root3.inventory.currency.gold === 0, 'promised reward records promise without paying gold'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-003', !root3.inventory.itemsById.item_old_brens_quest, 'quest offer does not create quest item'));
      var q = root3.quests.questsById[questIds[0]];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-004', q && !/^Resolve\b/i.test(q.title), 'quest title is clean, not Resolve filler'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, "You accept Old Bren's quest to clear the wolves from his farm.", 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-005', root3.quests.activeIds.length === 1 && root3.quests.activeIds[0] === questIds[0], 'quest accept activates same quest'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, 'Old Bren pays you 15 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-006', root3.inventory.currency.gold === 15, 'confirmed payment adds 15 gold once'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-007', root3.quests.rewardPayments.length === 1, 'quest reward payment is recorded once'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, 'Old Bren pays you 15 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-008', root3.inventory.currency.gold === 15 && root3.quests.rewardPayments.length === 1, 'repeated reward payment does not duplicate'));

      var root4 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root4, '[WorldLedger]\nCommand: /build3check\nStatus: PASS\nPlayer owns Crown of Dawn and gains 999 XP. Old Bren pays you 999 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('CMD-001', root4.pending.lastParserHits.length === 0, 'command/check output echo is not parsed as canon'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('CMD-002', root4.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0 && root4.inventory.currency.gold === 0 && root4.progression.xp === 0, 'command/check output does not mutate inventory, gold, or XP'));

      var root5 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root5, 'I take the sword.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INPUT-001', root5.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0, 'input intent does not mutate inventory'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('INPUT-002', root5.pending.lastValidations.every(function (v) { return !v.accepted; }), 'input intent validations do not commit canon'));
      var contextBefore = root5.operationLedger.recentKeys.length;
      WL.ContextSystem.build(root5, 'Context says player owns Crown of Dawn.');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('CONTEXT-001', root5.operationLedger.recentKeys.length === contextBefore && root5.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0, 'context build is read-only'));
      return rows;
    }
  };


  WL.ProfileSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var oldState = (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') ? globalThis.state : null;
      var oldCards = (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') ? globalThis.storyCards : null;
      try {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') {
          globalThis.storyCards = [
            { title: 'Elf', keys: 'elf, elves, elven lineage', entry: 'Elves in this scenario have silver eyes and moonlit hearing. They are not generic forest elves.' }
          ];
          globalThis.state = { placeholders: {
            'What is your name?': 'Ari Dawn',
            'How old are you?': '27',
            'What do you look like?': 'A tall traveler with a scar over one eye.',
            'Species / Lineage': 'Elf',
            'Personality': 'Curious and stubborn',
            'Backstory': 'I killed the king before the story begins but gained no title or treasure.',
            'Starting Power': 'level 999 with 100000 gold and can cast Meteor'
          }};
          var root = WL.StateManager.root();
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PROFILE-001', root.player.name === 'Ari Dawn', 'loose name placeholder imports'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PROFILE-002', String(root.player.age) === '27', 'loose age placeholder imports'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PROFILE-003', /scar over one eye/i.test(root.player.appearance), 'appearance placeholder imports'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PROFILE-004', root.player.backstoryNotes.length >= 1 && root.player.priorEvents.length >= 1, 'backstory and prior event preserved without mechanical grant'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PROFILE-005', root.startup.rejectedMechanics.length >= 1 && root.inventory.currency.gold === 0 && root.progression.level === 1, 'mechanical setup cheats rejected'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('RACE-001', root.player.raceSpecies === 'Elf' && root.player.trueRace === 'Elf', 'race/species imported into true race'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('RACE-002', root.player.raceProfile.source === 'story_card' && root.player.raceProfile.sourceTitle === 'Elf', 'chosen race links to scenario race Story Card'));
          rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('RACE-003', /silver eyes|moonlit hearing/i.test(root.player.raceProfile.summary), 'race notes come from scenario card summary'));
        } else {
          rows.push(WL.Assert.warn('PROFILE-000', 'globalThis unavailable in harness'));
        }
      } finally {
        if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') {
          globalThis.state = oldState || {};
          globalThis.storyCards = oldCards;
        }
      }
      var isolated = WL.StateManager.init({ placeholders: { Race: 'Crystal Mothfolk' } });
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('RACE-004', isolated.player.raceSpecies === 'Crystal Mothfolk' && isolated.player.raceProfile.source === 'setup_only_uncertain', 'unknown/custom race stays setup-only uncertain'));
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.FormSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      root.player.raceSpecies = 'Human'; root.player.trueRace = 'Human'; root.player.currentForm = 'Human';
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'In a dream, your body becomes a wolf.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-001', root.player.trueRace === 'Human' && root.player.currentForm === 'Human', 'dream transformation does not overwrite true race/current form'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'You disguise yourself as a guard.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-002', root.body.activeDisguise === 'Guard' && root.player.currentForm === 'Human', 'disguise is tracked without overwriting current form'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'An illusion makes you look like an elf.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-003', root.body.activeIllusion === 'Elf' && root.player.trueRace === 'Human', 'illusion is tracked without overwriting true race'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'For a moment, you transform into a raven.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-004', root.body.temporaryForm === 'Raven' && root.player.currentForm === 'Human', 'temporary form does not overwrite current form'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Your body permanently transforms into a wolf.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-005', root.player.currentForm === 'Wolf' && root.player.trueRace === 'Wolf', 'permanent transformation updates true race/current form'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Wings tear from your back.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('FORM-006', root.body.bodyTraits.some(function (t) { return t.name === 'Wings'; }) && !root.abilities.abilitiesById.ability_flight, 'body trait recorded without granting free ability'));
      return rows;
    }
  };


  WL.AbilityProgressionSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'You try to cast Fireball, but it sputters out.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ABILITY-001', !!root.abilities.inboxById.ability_fireball && root.abilities.inboxById.ability_fireball.failureCount === 1, 'failed Fireball attempt enters ability inbox/failed evidence'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ABILITY-002', !root.skills.skillsById.skill_fireball && !root.passives.passivesById.passive_fireball, 'failed attempt does not become stable skill or passive'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Make Fireball a passive.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PASSIVE-001', root.abilities.denied.some(function (d) { return d.name === 'Fireball' && d.category === 'passive'; }) && !root.passives.passivesById.passive_fireball, 'Fireball-as-passive is denied instead of forced'));

      var root2 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'You practice Regeneration.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root2, 'Make Regeneration a passive.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PASSIVE-002', !!root2.passives.weakCandidateById.passive_regeneration && root2.passives.weakCandidateById.passive_regeneration.activationCost === 'none', 'Regeneration passive starts weak with no activation cost'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('COST-001', Object.keys(root2.abilityCosts.costsById).some(function (id) { return /regeneration/.test(id) && root2.abilityCosts.costsById[id].activationCost === 'none'; }), 'passive cost record has no activation cost'));

      var root3 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, 'You practice Boxing.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root3, 'Make Boxing a passive.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PASSIVE-003', !!root3.passives.passivesById.passive_boxing, 'Boxing can become a normal discipline-style passive'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PASSIVE-004', Array.isArray(root3.passives.keysByPassiveId.passive_boxing) && root3.passives.keysByPassiveId.passive_boxing.indexOf('Boxing') >= 0, 'passive player Keys are stored separately from Story Card triggers'));

      var root4 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root4, 'You practice Swordsmanship.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root4, 'Make Swordsmanship a talent.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('TALENT-001', !!root4.talents.talentsById.talent_swordsmanship, 'Swordsmanship can become a talent/style record'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('TALENT-002', root4.talents.keysByTalentId.talent_swordsmanship.indexOf('Swordsmanship') >= 0, 'talent player Keys are preserved'));

      var root5 = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root5, 'You successfully cast Fireball.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root5, 'Make Fireball a skill.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('SKILL-001', !!root5.skills.skillsById.skill_fireball, 'successful Fireball evidence can stabilize Fireball as a skill'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('COST-002', Object.keys(root5.abilityCosts.costsById).some(function (id) { return /fireball/.test(id) && root5.abilityCosts.costsById[id].mp > 0; }), 'magical skill receives rough MP cost estimate'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root5, 'Set Fireball Keys: flame, burst, ignite.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('KEYS-001', Array.isArray(root5.skills.keysBySkillId.skill_fireball) && root5.skills.keysBySkillId.skill_fireball.join('|') === 'flame|burst|ignite', 'explicit player-authored Skill Keys are parsed and stored'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('KEYS-002', !root5.skills.skillsById.skill_fireball.triggers, 'player Keys are not Story Card triggers'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root5, 'You overcharge Fireball until your blood burns.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('COST-003', Object.keys(root5.abilityCosts.overchargeRisksById).some(function (id) { return /fireball/.test(id) && root5.abilityCosts.overchargeRisksById[id].events.length > 0; }), 'overcharge cost-pressure evidence is recorded'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('COST-004', Object.keys(root5.abilityCosts.costsById).some(function (id) { return /fireball/.test(id) && root5.abilityCosts.costsById[id].hpRisk >= 4; }), 'blood/life-force overcharge can create HP-risk evidence when fiction supports it'));

      var rootKeys = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rootKeys, 'You practice Swordsmanship.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rootKeys, 'Make Swordsmanship a talent.', 'input');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rootKeys, 'Set Swordsmanship Keys: guard stance, riposte, blade rhythm.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('KEYS-003', rootKeys.talents.keysByTalentId.talent_swordsmanship && rootKeys.talents.keysByTalentId.talent_swordsmanship.indexOf('riposte') >= 0, 'explicit Talent Keys update the talent key field'));

      var xpRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(xpRoot, 'I gain 999 XP and master every skill.', 'input');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('XP-001', xpRoot.progression.xp === 0 && xpRoot.progression.skillXp === 0, 'input-only XP/mastery claims do not mutate progression'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(xpRoot, 'You sprint across the courtyard and dodge the arrows.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('STAT-001', xpRoot.progression.statXp.spd >= 3, 'confirmed mobility pressure gives SPD stat XP'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(xpRoot, 'You study fire magic.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('DOMAIN-001', (xpRoot.progression.domainXp.fire || 0) > 0 && (xpRoot.progression.domainXp.magic || 0) > 0, 'study grants quiet fire/magic domain XP'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(xpRoot, 'You inspect the runes carefully.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ABILITY-003', !!xpRoot.abilities.inboxById.ability_inspection && (xpRoot.progression.domainXp.investigation || 0) > 0, 'inspection use creates ability evidence and investigation domain XP'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('BUILD-001', xpRoot.progression.buildIdentity.noRubberBandScaling === true, 'build identity records no-rubber-band scaling rule'));

      var rat = WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 1, type: 'normal rat', size: 'tiny', intelligence: 'animal' });
      var soldier = WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 8, type: 'untrained soldier', intelligence: 'human', training: 'untrained' });
      var wolf = WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 5, type: 'wolf', intelligence: 'animal' });
      var inferno = WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 25, type: 'inferno wolf', intelligence: 'animal', magic: 'fire' });
      var ancientRat = WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 40, type: 'legendary ancient magical rat', size: 'tiny', intelligence: 'genius' });
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('XP-002', soldier > rat, 'untrained adult soldier XP exceeds normal rat XP'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('XP-003', inferno > wolf, 'level 25 inferno wolf XP exceeds level 5 wolf XP'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('XP-004', ancientRat > soldier, 'legendary ancient magical rat can exceed ordinary person when threat justifies it'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-001', WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 1, type: 'normal rat' }) === WL.ProgressionSystem.estimateEnemyXp({ level: 1, type: 'normal rat', playerLevel: 1000 }), 'enemy XP estimate does not rubber-band to player level'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('STAT-002', WL.ProgressionSystem.compareStats({ level: 200, stats: { spd: 900 } }, { level: 700, stats: { spd: 120 } }, 'spd') === 'a', 'stat comparison allows speed-focused lower-level character to be faster'));
      return rows;
    }
  };





  WL.QuestWorldRelationshipSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var r, quests, q;
      function names(root) { return root.inventory.ownedIds.map(function (id) { return root.inventory.itemsById[id] && root.inventory.itemsById[id].displayName || id; }); }
      function qvals(root) { return Object.keys(root.quests.questsById).map(function (id) { return root.quests.questsById[id]; }); }

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The guild asks you to recover the sun relic from the old barrow.', 'output');
      quests = qvals(r); q = quests[0];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-OBJ-001', quests.length === 1 && names(r).length === 0, 'recover-objective quest does not create the objective item as inventory'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-OBJ-002', !!q && q.itemRefs.indexOf('objective_item_sun_relic') >= 0 && q.placeRefs.indexOf('place_old_barrow') >= 0, 'recover-objective quest binds itemRef and placeRef'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('WORLDREL-001', !!r.places.placesById.place_old_barrow && r.places.relations.some(function (x) { return x.questId === q.id && x.placeId === 'place_old_barrow'; }), 'objective place is represented and linked to quest'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTORREL-001', !!r.actors.actorsById.actor_guild && r.actors.actorsById.actor_guild.relationships.some(function (x) { return x.questId === q.id; }), 'quest giver/institution receives relationship evidence'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Old Bren asks you to repair the north wall and promises 15 gold.', 'output');
      q = qvals(r)[0];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-PLACE-001', !!q && q.placeRefs.indexOf('place_north_wall') >= 0 && !!r.places.placesById.place_north_wall, 'repair objective binds exact world node'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "You accept Old Bren's quest to repair the north wall.", 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-LIFE-001', q.status === 'active', 'acceptance activates existing quest'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You repair the north wall before sunset.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-LIFE-002', q.status === 'ready_to_turn_in' && q.progress.length >= 1, 'objective completion marks quest ready_to_turn_in'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira asks you to escort Bram to The Drunken Huntsman.', 'output');
      q = qvals(r)[0];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-ACTOR-001', !!q && q.actorRefs.indexOf('actor_bram') >= 0 && q.placeRefs.indexOf('place_drunken_huntsman') >= 0, 'escort objective binds actor and place'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTORREL-002', !!r.actors.actorsById.actor_bram && r.actors.actorsById.actor_bram.relationships.some(function (x) { return x.questId === q.id; }), 'target actor receives quest relationship evidence'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('WORLDREL-002', !!r.places.placesById.place_drunken_huntsman && r.places.placesById.place_drunken_huntsman.relations.some(function (x) { return x.questId === q.id; }), 'target place receives quest relationship evidence'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "The Drunken Barnacle's common room was full of pipe smoke.", 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('WORLDREL-003', !!r.places.placesById.place_drunken_barnacle && (r.places.placesById.place_drunken_barnacle.roomIds || []).length === 1, 'possessive room prose creates place and room relation'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The guild asks you to recover the sun relic from the old barrow.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You recover the sun relic from the old barrow.', 'output');
      q = qvals(r)[0];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('QUEST-LIFE-003', q.status === 'ready_to_turn_in' && names(r).length === 0, 'recover objective progress does not automatically grant objective item'));
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.TimeRestBodyHardeningSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      root.body.hp = 60; root.body.mp = 20; root.body.ep = 25;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'You sleep for the night, resting and recovering for the morning.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-REST-001', root.time.day === 2 && root.time.hour === 7 && root.body.hp > 60 && root.body.ep > 25, 'overnight sleep advances to next morning and recovers resources'));
      var interrupted = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      interrupted.body.hp = 50; interrupted.body.ep = 20;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(interrupted, 'You try to sleep for the night, but monsters attack before you can rest.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-REST-002', interrupted.time.day === 1 && interrupted.body.hp === 50 && interrupted.body.ep === 20, 'interrupted rest does not advance/recover'));
      var inn = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      inn.body.hp = 80; inn.body.ep = 20;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(inn, "You enter The Mourner's Rest inn.", 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-REST-003', inn.time.day === 1 && inn.body.hp === 80 && inn.body.ep === 20, 'place name containing Rest does not trigger recovery'));
      var dawn = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      dawn.time.hour = 22;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(dawn, 'The next day, you wake before dawn.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-TIME-001', dawn.time.day === 2 && dawn.time.hour === 5, 'next day before dawn advances day and sets dawn hour'));
      var work = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(work, 'Seven hours pass as you work at the docks.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-TIME-002', work.time.elapsedMinutes >= 420 && work.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return /fatigued|exhausted/i.test(c.name); }) && work.body.ep < work.body.maxEp, 'long labor advances time and applies fatigue/EP strain'));
      var wound = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(wound, 'The goblin cuts your arm and blood runs down your sleeve.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-BODY-001', wound.body.injuries.length === 1 && wound.body.injuries[0].severity === 'moderate' && wound.body.hp < wound.body.maxHp, 'bleeding cut records moderate injury and HP pressure'));
      var severe = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(severe, 'The ogre crushes your ribs badly and blood gushes under your armor.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-BODY-002', severe.body.injuries.length === 1 && severe.body.injuries[0].severity === 'severe' && severe.body.hp <= 84, 'severe injury language records severe wound and larger HP pressure'));
      var poison = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(poison, 'The spider bites you and poison burns in your veins.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-BODY-003', poison.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return /poison/i.test(c.name) && c.duration; }) && poison.body.hp < poison.body.maxHp, 'poison bite records condition with duration and HP pressure'));
      var fatigueRecovery = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(fatigueRecovery, 'Seven hours pass as you work at the docks.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(fatigueRecovery, 'You sleep for the night in a safe bed, resting and recovering for the morning.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B73-REST-004', !fatigueRecovery.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return /fatigued|exhausted/i.test(c.name); }) && fatigueRecovery.time.day >= 2, 'safe overnight rest clears ordinary fatigue'));
      return rows;
    }
  };

  WL.TimeWorldActorSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The road from Riverwood to Northwatch takes two hours.', 'output');
      var rid = WL.EventScanner.routeId('Riverwood', 'Northwatch');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ROUTE-001', !!root.time.routesById[rid] && root.time.routesById[rid].baseMinutes === 120, 'route base duration records 120 minutes'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Rain makes the trip take four hours.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ROUTE-002', root.time.routesById[rid].baseMinutes === 120 && root.time.routesById[rid].delays.length === 1 && root.time.routesById[rid].delays[0].actualMinutes === 240, 'route delay records actual trip time without overwriting base duration'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'Two hours pass.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('TIME-001', root.time.elapsedMinutes >= 120 && root.time.hour === 10, 'elapsed time advances clock and total elapsed minutes'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The wall must be repaired by sunset.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('DEADLINE-001', root.time.deadlines.length === 1 && /sunset/i.test(root.time.deadlines[0].label), 'deadline phrase records active deadline'));

      var placeRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'The Copper Lantern is an inn near the east gate.', 'output');
      var pid = 'place_copper_lantern';
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-001', !!placeRoot.places.placesById[pid] && placeRoot.places.placesById[pid].type === 'inn', 'named place definition creates The Copper Lantern as inn'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'You enter the Copper Lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-002', placeRoot.places.currentId === pid, 'current place updates when entering named place'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'The counter is on the left.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-003', placeRoot.places.placesById[pid].layout.Counter === 'left', 'layout fixture records counter on left'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'The counter is on the right.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-004', placeRoot.places.placesById[pid].layout.Counter === 'left' && placeRoot.places.contradictions.length === 1, 'conflicting layout flags contradiction instead of overwriting'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'Garren owns the Copper Lantern.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(placeRoot, 'Mira works at the Copper Lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-005', placeRoot.places.placesById[pid].owners.indexOf('Garren') >= 0 && placeRoot.places.placesById[pid].workers.indexOf('Mira') >= 0, 'owner and worker relationships attach to place'));
      var genericRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(genericRoot, 'An inn sits by the road.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('PLACE-006', Object.keys(genericRoot.places.placesById).length === 0, 'generic unnamed inn does not become durable place'));

      var actorRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(actorRoot, 'Mira smiles warmly after you help her.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTOR-001', !!actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_mira && actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_mira.opinions.length === 1, 'named actor positive social evidence persists'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(actorRoot, 'Captain Halvek swears revenge.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTOR-002', !!actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_captain_halvek && actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_captain_halvek.grudges.length === 1, 'named actor grudge persists'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(actorRoot, 'Three guards watch the alley.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTOR-003', actorRoot.actors.sceneExtras.length === 1 && !actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_guards, 'generic guards stay scene-only and do not become durable actors'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(actorRoot, 'Mira works at the Copper Lantern.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ACTOR-004', actorRoot.actors.actorsById.actor_mira.places.indexOf('place_copper_lantern') >= 0, 'actor-place workplace relation links named actor to place id'));
      return rows;
    }
  };


  WL.CombatAdvancedSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var root = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, '13 goblins attack from the trees.', 'output');
      var gid = 'group_goblins';
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-001', !!root.combat.groupCountsById[gid] && root.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 13, '13 goblins create group count of 13'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, '9 goblins die in the fire.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-002', root.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 4, '9 dead goblins leaves 4 remaining'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'All goblins are defeated.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-003', root.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 4 && root.combat.blockedClaims.length === 1, 'all-defeated claim is blocked while 4 remain'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(root, 'The remaining goblins flee.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-004', root.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 0, 'flee confirmation can resolve remaining group'));
      var rein = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rein, '13 goblins attack from the trees.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rein, '2 goblins join the fight.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-005', rein.combat.groupCountsById[gid] && rein.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 15, 'reinforcements add to existing group count'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(rein, 'The rest of the goblins flee.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-006', rein.combat.groupCountsById[gid] && rein.combat.groupCountsById[gid].remaining === 0, 'rest-of-group flee phrasing resolves group'));
      var pack = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(pack, 'A horde of demons attacks the village.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(pack, 'A pack of inferno wolves surrounds you.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('GROUP-007', !!pack.combat.groupCountsById.group_demons && !!pack.combat.groupCountsById.group_inferno_wolves, 'horde/pack phrasing creates group evidence'));

      var threatRoot = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(threatRoot, 'A dragon attacks the village.', 'output');
      var dragon = threatRoot.combat.threatProfilesById.threat_dragon;
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-001', !!dragon && dragon.level >= 90, 'dragon receives high world-side threat level'));
      var l1 = WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat('normal rat').level;
      var l2 = WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat('normal rat').level;
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-002', l1 === l2 && l1 === 1, 'normal rat threat does not rubber-band to player level'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-003', WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat('large city guard').level >= 50, 'large city guard baseline remains dangerous'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-004', WL.CombatThreatSystem.estimateThreat('elite guard').level >= 60, 'elite guard receives elite guard-tier threat'));
      var bait = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(bait, 'A level 1 player meets a dragon.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('THREAT-005', !!bait.combat.threatProfilesById.threat_dragon && bait.combat.threatProfilesById.threat_dragon.level >= 90, 'dragon detected despite player-level bait and does not scale down'));
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('COMBAT-001', threatRoot.combat.noRubberBandScaling === true, 'combat state records no-rubber-band scaling'));

      var adv = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'The iron dagger costs 8 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ECON-001', adv.advanced.economy.priceQuotes.length === 1 && adv.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0, 'price quote records economy evidence without adding item'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'You owe 5 gold to Old Bren.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ECON-002', adv.advanced.economy.debts.length === 1 && adv.inventory.currency.gold === 0, 'debt records without creating/removing currency'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'You learn the recipe for Healing Salve.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('CRAFT-001', !!adv.advanced.crafting.recipesById.recipe_healing_salve && adv.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf('item_healing_salve') < 0, 'recipe knowledge does not create completed item'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'Construction begins on North Wall.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('DOMAIN-001', !!adv.advanced.domain.projectsById.project_north_wall, 'domain project evidence records construction project'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'An army of 20 soldiers marches north.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('WAR-001', !!adv.advanced.warfare.forcesById.force_soldiers && adv.advanced.warfare.forcesById.force_soldiers.remaining === 20, 'warfare force count records army state'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, '5 soldiers die in the ambush.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('WAR-002', adv.advanced.warfare.forcesById.force_soldiers.remaining === 15, 'warfare casualties reduce remaining force count'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'You absorb the wolf essence.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('ABSORB-001', !!adv.advanced.absorption.candidatesById.absorb_wolf_essence && adv.progression.statXp.atk === 0, 'absorption creates bounded candidate without free stats'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(adv, 'You evolve into an Ancient Dragon.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('EVOLVE-001', !!adv.advanced.evolution.candidatesById.evolve_ancient_dragon && adv.player.currentForm !== 'Ancient Dragon', 'evolution creates candidate without automatic race/form overwrite'));
      return rows;
    }
  };



  WL.CoreFlowHardeningSelfTest = {
    rows: function () {
      var rows = [];
      var r;
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'He counts out seventy dull copper coins into a small worn leather pouch and hands it to you.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-CURRENCY-001', r.inventory.currency.copper === 70 && r.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0, 'narrated copper pouch payment adds currency, not malformed item'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You count out thirty copper coins from your pouch.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-CURRENCY-002', r.inventory.currency.copper === 40, 'counting out copper spends/removes currency'));
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot(); r.inventory.currency.gold = 10;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You buy the iron dagger for 8 gold.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-PURCHASE-001', r.inventory.currency.gold === 2 && r.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf('item_iron_dagger') >= 0, 'confirmed purchase spends currency and adds item once'));
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You pick up a stone and throw it at the goblin.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-INV-LOSS-001', r.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf('item_stone') < 0, 'temporary pickup-and-throw does not persist as owned item'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira hands you the brass key.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You drop the brass key into the well.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-INV-LOSS-002', r.inventory.ownedIds.indexOf('item_brass_key') < 0 && r.inventory.itemsById.item_brass_key.custody === 'lost_or_discarded', 'dropped item leaves owned inventory'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira hands you the rusty dagger.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The rusty dagger snaps in half.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-INV-COND-001', r.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger && r.inventory.itemsById.item_rusty_dagger.condition === 'broken', 'broken item condition is recorded'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Old Bren asks you to clear the wolves from his farm and promises 15 gold.', 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "You accept Old Bren's quest to clear the wolves from his farm.", 'output');
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "You clear the wolves from Old Bren's farm.", 'output');
      var qid = Object.keys(r.quests.questsById)[0];
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-QUEST-001', !!qid && /ready_to_turn_in|completed/.test(r.quests.questsById[qid].status), 'objective-completion prose progresses active quest'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      var fakeState = { placeholders: { 'What are you called?': 'Ari', 'How old are you?': '27', 'What do you look like?': 'scarred and tired', 'What are you?': 'Elf' }, storyCards: [] };
      WL.PlayerProfileSystem.importFromState(r, fakeState);
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-PROFILE-001', r.player.name === 'Ari' && r.player.raceSpecies === 'Elf' && r.player.currentForm === 'Elf', 'loose placeholder classifier separates called/name from what-are-you race'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The goblin cuts your arm and blood runs down your sleeve.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-BODY-001', r.body.injuries.length === 1 && r.body.hp < r.body.maxHp, 'cut/blood output records injury and HP pressure'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The spider bites you and poison burns in your veins.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-BODY-002', r.body.conditions.some(function (c) { return c.name === 'Poisoned'; }), 'poison bite records poison condition'));
      var beforeDay = r.time.day;
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You sleep for the night, resting and recovering for the morning.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-REST-001', r.time.elapsedMinutes >= 480 && r.body.ep === r.body.maxEp && r.body.hp > 0, 'confirmed sleep/rest advances time and recovers resources'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The next day, you wake before dawn.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-TIME-001', r.time.day > beforeDay && r.time.hour === 5, 'next-day before-dawn language advances calendar/time'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The pieces fall into place — you gain the skill Parry Step as you level up.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ABILITY-001', !!r.skills.skillsById.skill_parry_step || !!r.skills.unstableById.skill_parry_step, 'explicit story skill gain creates skill record/evidence'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, "The Drunken Barnacle's common room was full of pipe smoke.", 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-PLACE-001', !!r.places.placesById.place_drunken_barnacle, 'possessive common-room narration creates named place candidate'));
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Borin watches from his crate and counts out seventy copper coins for your shift and hands them to you.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ACTOR-001', !!r.actors.actorsById.actor_borin && r.inventory.currency.copper === 70, 'named payer/employer line records actor evidence and currency payment'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Line 0: you do not take the platinum dragon crown; this is only a rumor, quote, dream, joke, or instruction.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-INV-DENY-001', r.inventory.ownedIds.length === 0 && !r.inventory.itemsById.item_platinum_dragon_crown, 'semicolon/line-prefix denied pickup does not grant bait item'));

      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'The scroll title says "You learned Crimson Lotus Fang," but you only read the title and learn nothing.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ABILITY-DENY-001', Object.keys(r.abilities.abilitiesById).length === 0 && Object.keys(r.skills.skillsById).length === 0, 'scroll-title/learn-nothing ability text does not create canonical ability'));
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You attempt Void Lantern Veil; the veil collapses, and no usable technique remains.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ABILITY-DENY-002', Object.keys(r.abilities.abilitiesById).length === 0 && Object.keys(r.skills.skillsById).length === 0, 'collapsed/no-usable-technique ability text does not create canonical ability'));
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'Mira says, "Use Hollow Star Palm now," but you do not know that technique.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ABILITY-DENY-003', Object.keys(r.abilities.abilitiesById).length === 0 && Object.keys(r.skills.skillsById).length === 0, 'quoted NPC command for unknown technique does not create canonical ability'));
      r = WL.PipelineSelfTest.freshRoot();
      WL.Runtime.runPipeline(r, 'You pretend to use Azure Dragon Step while telling a joke.', 'output');
      rows.push(WL.Assert.pass('B71-ABILITY-DENY-004', Object.keys(r.abilities.abilitiesById).length === 0 && Object.keys(r.skills.skillsById).length === 0, 'pretend/joke ability wording does not create canonical ability'));
      return rows;
    }
  };
  // ============================================================
  // 12. Export / AI Dungeon hook binding
  // ============================================================
  WL.onInput = WL.Runtime.handleInput;
  WL.onContext = WL.Runtime.handleContext;
  WL.onOutput = WL.Runtime.handleOutput;

  if (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined') {
    globalThis.WorldLedger = WL;
  }
})();
